# Spec 1 W3 (Home: W3a, W3b, W3c) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Behind `flow_paths_v2`, make the signed-in home follow the server's home state: one `homeStateProvider` per account, a `HomeScreen` whose Tab 2 is Chat (Relationship) or Me (Dating, Just for me) and is rebuilt only when that layout changes, the A6 path choice and the `OnboardingGate` v2 catch-up steps (A5c, A2, A1, A6, H1) for new and legacy accounts, the device wipe registry with its three call sites, the Relationship space (R3) and the Me home with the Dating slot and the Dating lock, Settings → How you use Attune with every confirmation, the in-context push asks (R2, J3), and the weekly journal reminder (migration 8 and its cron). With `flow_paths_v2` off, today's home, onboarding and push ask behave exactly as now, and tests prove it.

**Architecture:** Three sub-waves. **W3a** adds the flag reader (`flowPathsV2Provider`), `HomeStateService` over W1's RPCs, `homeStateProvider` (a per-account `FamilyNotifier` over W1's `HomeStateStore`, which caches only tab, link, needs and hold, D20), the device wipe registry (§5.8), a path-aware `HomeScreen` with three test seams and a keyed shell (its latch byte-identical), A6, and `OnboardingGate` v2 behind `/onboarding`. **W3b** adds `RelationshipSpaceScreen` (Tab 2 on Relationship before a link), `MeHomeScreen` with J1/J4, the Dating slot (no Dating RPC, I-4) and the Dating lock, and removes "Meet someone in Dating mode" from `ChatCouplesLockedScreen` unconditionally (R-5). **W3c** adds migration 8 (`journal_reminder_settings`, three RPCs, cron `enqueue-journal-reminders`), the in-context push asks with an `onesignal_service.dart` regression pass, J3 and its tap route, Settings → How you use Attune, and its manual topic. Every Dart and SQL block below was run while writing this plan: the Dart in a scratch copy of the tree, replayed task by task from a fresh copy (each task's tests green at its own step, the full suite green at the end), the SQL on a scratch Postgres 17 restored from `attune_spec3a`'s schema (read-only on `attune_spec3a`): the migration applied twice, the contract green, red before the migration, and each mutant red with the message quoted.

**Tech Stack:** Flutter 3.29.1 (Dart 3.7), Riverpod 2.6, GoRouter 10.2, Supabase Postgres 17 (pg_cron), OneSignal Flutter SDK (existing), `permission_handler` (existing), `crypto` (existing, for the Games route test), psql.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-24-app-flow-identity-design.md` — §1.3–1.5, §2 (copy, motion, accessibility, §2.4 permissions, §2.5 flags), §3.7–3.15, §4 rows tagged W3 (migration 8: `journal_reminder_settings`, `set_my_journal_reminder`, `get_my_journal_reminder`, `enqueue_journal_reminders`, cron `enqueue-journal-reminders`), §5.1–5.8 rows tagged W3* (§5.8 the device wipe registry), §7 (W3 contracts, widget tests, mutants), §8 W3a–W3c and the W3 group gate, §9, §11. Format reference: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-25-spec1-w2-signin.md`.

## Global Constraints

- Work in the main working tree on `main` (`/Users/user/attune`). W1 (360db8d1) and W2 (8b3477ad) are committed; the owner may have uncommitted edits: never revert, stash, stage, checkout or reformat them. Every edit below is an exact-string replacement; if the text to replace has moved because a later fix changed it, apply the same change to the current text and say so in the task report.
- **NO commits.** No `git add`, `git commit`, `git stash`, `git checkout`, `git reset`, `git rm`. Tasks end with "Run tests", not "Commit".
- Never `dart format` a pre-existing file (the repo predates the 3.7 formatter). Format only the files this plan creates, by path; the code below is already formatter output, so formatting them changes nothing.
- **SQL:** contracts run only on the local scratch database `attune_spec3a`, only as `psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f <file>`, inside the file's own `BEGIN … ROLLBACK`. Confirm the target first (`psql -X -d attune_spec3a -Atc 'select current_database()'` prints `attune_spec3a`). NEVER `attune_test`. NEVER run `scripts/local_pg_setup.sh` or `scripts/local_pg_grants.sql`; Task 9 **edits** `scripts/local_pg_grants.sql` and never runs it.
- **Migrations** (W3c only): `20261026100000_journal_reminders.sql` + `20261026100010_journal_reminders_grants.sql`. If `ls supabase/migrations | sort | tail -1` shows anything at or after `20261026100000` when Task 9 starts, shift both to the next free hour, keeping the order, and change the `\i` line and the twin's header comment to match. Each file is one `BEGIN; … COMMIT;`, idempotent (applied twice, `exit=0` both times), `SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s'` where it creates or alters tables, every `SECURITY DEFINER` function has `SET search_path = ''` and schema-qualifies every name, server-only functions are revoked inside the migration itself and again in the twin, grants are explicit, nothing reaches `anon`; `authenticated` gets SELECT on its own rows (RLS) and EXECUTE on the two `*_my_*` RPCs only; `service_role` keeps DML on the new public table. No `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY`.
- **Flags:** W3 adds no flag. Everything here is behind `flow_paths_v2` (inserted false by W1c; `flow_paths_v2` needs `flow_signin_v2`, `flow_couples_v2` needs `flow_paths_v2`, §2.5). `supabase/sql/dev_enable_spec1_flags.sql` already turns them on in development, so it doesn't change. With `flow_paths_v2` off, HomeScreen, `/onboarding`, Settings and the automatic push ask are today's (Task 3's `flow_paths_v2 off` test, Task 5's `OnboardingEntry`, Task 11's settings rows test, Task 10's `flow_paths_v2 off: today's ask`, and the untouched `test/home/*`, `onboarding_flow_test.dart`, `widget_test.dart` prove it). The one change with the flag off is review R-5: "Meet someone in Dating mode" leaves `ChatCouplesLockedScreen` unconditionally (Task 7).
- **Never touch:** `lib/features/games/**`, `ChatScreen`'s Games entry points, `UniversalBubble` / `ChatTextField` parameters, `lib/features/auth/utility/auth_exports.dart`, every Games route in `app_router.dart` (Task 3's architecture test pins each one's source), `lib/features/opinions/**` (Opinions stays exactly as is), `ATTUNE_SOUL.md`, `ATTUNE_THESIS.md`, every applied migration, every existing SQL contract. HomeScreen's latch block (from `if (isAuthenticated && !_gateData.isComplete) {` to `_redirectingToOnboarding = false;`) stays byte-identical (Task 3, Step 6 checks it).
- **Dates and times** shown to people use `MyDateFormat` only (`MyDateFormat.toTime` for J3's time); a date and its detail are joined with `MyDateFormat.join` (`' - '`).
- **The couple's name:** wherever copy names the couple, it uses `coupleNameProvider` with the placeholder `'Couples'`; this plan's copy names only the partner (`partner_first_name`, else "your partner"), never "both of you".
- **Copy:** every new string is an English `flow_*` key appended to `lib/i10n/app_en.arb` by `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-26-spec1-w3/arb_append.py` (Task 3, Step 1) and generated with `flutter gen-l10n` (exact command in each task); the other locales fall back to English. Every fragment is ASCII only (non-ASCII is written as `\uXXXX` by script; `grep -cP '[^\x00-\x7F]'` prints `0`). No vendor name appears in anything a person sees.
- **UI:** Material 3 from the app theme (`AppTheme`, `Spacing`, `BorderRadiusTokens`, `ContentWidth`, `.w`/`.h`) and W2's flow widgets (`FlowFrame`, `FlowPrimaryButton`, `FlowSecondaryButton`, `FlowTextLink`, `flowAccent()`); the app's core widgets where they fit (`BottomSheetHeader`, `BottomSheetUtils`, `AppTextButton`, `EmptyStateWidget`); every new screen wrapped in `AllowLargeText`, every control labelled, targets ≥ 48 dp, text contrast ≥ 4.5:1, errors announced and shown with an icon (never colour alone), goldens at 1.0, 1.3, 2.0 and right to left, `androidTapTargetGuideline`, `labeledTapTargetGuideline` and `textContrastGuideline` on every new screen. Never animated: H1, A5c.
- **Permanent constraints:** no couple report combining both people's insights; asymmetric behaviour data stays self-facing; safety notifications go to the at-risk person only; quick exit works everywhere (the registry never blocks or throws in it, Task 2). No Dating answer is ever stored on the phone (D20): no prefs key or value this plan writes names Dating (Tasks 1, 2 and 8 test it), and a Dating path choice is never queued offline.
- **D18 is a gate item, not built here:** Dating → Just for me pauses Dating only through Spec 2 W2's path trigger. This plan's confirmation copy and `confirm_pause_dating` handling assume it; the W3 group gate holds `flow_paths_v2` in production until it is live.
- **No deploys.** `supabase db push`, dashboard work and store work are the owner's ("Owner deploy checklist"). Don't read `.env*`, credentials or the keychain. No new package.
- **Regression gate at the end of each sub-wave** (Tasks 5, 8, 12): the full `flutter test` passes; `flutter analyze --no-pub lib test` shows no `error` line that the Task 1 baseline didn't; Task 3's Games test passes; no Opinions file, `auth_exports.dart` or Games file changed since Task 1; W3c also: every SQL contract on `attune_spec3a` shows no new FAIL against the Task 1 baseline and `journal_reminder_contracts.sql` is PASS.

## Review Focus

1. **A legacy account whose server answer is `needs: [birth_date]`** (made before W1, today's onboarding finished) opens the v2 app for the first time: it expects one "Add your date of birth" and then home, not a loop between `/home` and `/onboarding`, and not a flash of `/onboarding` before the server has answered. Tests: Task 5, `needs [birth_date]: "Add your date of birth", then home`; Task 3, `Relationship: Tab 2 is Chat and opens first` (the legacy flag stands in until the first answer, P-6).
2. **A6 tapped with no connection**: Just for me is kept and replayed later; Dating is never queued (D20) and says why. Tests: Task 5, `A6 offline: Just for me is queued; Dating is not (D20)`; Task 1, `a Dating choice is never queued (D20)`.
3. **An account on EULA v1 whose app was killed at A5c** (W2 final review M-3): every later launch shows A5c before home until it is accepted, and "Not now" signs out. Tests: Task 5, `needs [consent]: A5c before home, every launch until accepted (M-3)` and `A5c "Not now" signs out`.
4. **"Not ready?" on the Dating slot while a Dating profile exists**: switching to Just for me pauses Dating, so it must ask first; with nothing enrolled it is one tap. Tests: Task 8, `"Not ready?" while enrolled asks first (§3.12)` and `"Not ready?" with nothing enrolled is one tap`.
5. **A journal reminder for 22:30** (or 07:59): refused by the server and the sheet, explained in words, nothing saved, and no push ever lands in quiet hours. Tests: Task 9, contract section B (`22:30 was accepted`, `07:59 was accepted`); Task 11, `a refused quiet-hours time is explained, not saved`.

## Decisions found while verifying the code (read before Task 1)

Each is a verified fact about today's code (W1 360db8d1, W2 8b3477ad) or the database, with the smallest change that keeps the spec's intent. *Changes spec intent* marks the ones the owner should see.

- **P-1 (G3 still not built).** §3.2 G3 ("Sign in to write" in Opinions, return to Opinions once) is placed in W2 by the spec, not W3, and needs edits to four Opinions files (`opinion_card.dart:310`, `comment_thread_screen.dart:174`, `tag_browse_screen.dart:43`, `anonymous_profile_screen.dart:174`); Opinions stays exactly as is. W3 doesn't build it either; guests sign up from Tab 2 ("Sign in", Task 3) or Welcome. Recorded as deferred to the Opinions redesign (Q9). *Changes spec intent* (carried from W2 P-1).
- **P-2 (guest tab label).** HomeScreen's guest Tab 2 reads "Sign in" (`flow_tabSignIn`) under `flow_signin_v2` (§3.2 G2), "Chat" otherwise; a signed-in Me layout reads "Me" (`flow_tabMe`). The tab's `id` follows the layout (`chat`/`me`). The latch block is untouched (Task 3, Step 6 diffs it).
- **P-3 (back to Tab 2).** `HomeWidget` reads `initialTabIndex` once, so `/home` can't reopen Tab 2. The route passes the query to `HomeScreen(tabRequest:)`; `homeLocation(tab:)` (`lib/home/home_location.dart`) builds `/home?tab=chat|me&n=<counter>` so a repeated request differs, and GoRouter's page key keeps the same `HomeScreen`, whose `didUpdateWidget` bumps the shell key. "Join Attune"'s two sign-up links pass `back: homeLocation(tab: AppTab.chat)`; two W2 tests (`login_profile_v2_test.dart`, `login_profile_v2_gate_test.dart`) assert the new `back` (W2 B6 m-4).
- **P-4 (HomeScreen seams).** HomeScreen built `PasswordlessAuthService()` and `OpinionsTab()` inline, which made the v2 shell untestable without Supabase. Three providers in `lib/home/home_seams.dart` — `homeAuthProvider` (`HomeAuth`: `currentUser`, `authStateChanges`), `homeOpinionsTabProvider`, `homeTabTwoBuilderProvider` — default to exactly today's objects; nothing else in HomeScreen changes behaviour with the flag off.
- **P-5 (`homeStateProvider` shape).** §5.3's provider is a `NotifierProvider.family<HomeStateNotifier, HomeSnapshot, String>` keyed by user id, not an `AsyncNotifier`: the shell must render synchronously from W1's `HomeStateStore` cache on the first frame (airplane-mode cold start, §3.14). `refresh()` deduplicates concurrent calls; `apply()` writes the cache and, on the first v2 answer, removes the account's legacy `attune.onboarding.user.<uid>.mode` key once; sign-out invalidates the account's provider so the live answer (path, Dating) is forgotten (D20).
- **P-6 (before the first v2 answer).** Until this phone has any v2 answer for the account (`HomeSnapshot.known` false), HomeScreen keeps the legacy `isComplete` (today's onboarding flag) and the legacy mode for the layout, so an already set-up account never flashes through `/onboarding`; the first answer then decides (it can also close the home: a new need, a hold).
- **P-7 (queued A5 and A6 count).** `HomeSnapshot.withQueued` treats a queued `complete_sign_up` (W2) as `consent`, `birth_date` and `name` done, and a queued A6 (`OnboardingStore.pendingPath`, Just for me or Relationship only; `savePendingPath` throws for Dating and `choosePath` never calls it with Dating) as `path` done with its layout, so an offline sign-up reaches home. `OnboardingSyncService.flush` replays the A6 after the A5 and hands the answer to `homeStateProvider` (`onPathSynced`).
- **P-8 (R1 until W4b).** §3.8 R1's full invite screen (I-1 share sheet, RI-4) is W4b's. W3a's `InvitePartnerScreen` (`/invite-partner`) creates today's invite through `RelationshipInviteService` and shows today's `InviteCard`; with `switch=1` the path switch to Relationship happens when the invite is created, not before (§3.12), and `paused=1` carries a Dating pause already confirmed.
- **P-9 (OnboardingGate v2).** `/onboarding` builds `OnboardingEntry`: `OnboardingGateV2` under `flow_paths_v2`, today's `OnboardingGate` otherwise (and for the debug preview). The v2 gate replays the queue, reads the server once, then shows only what's missing in order — hold → H1; an unknown need → "Update Attune" (never home); `consent` → A5c; `birth_date` → A2 ("Add your date of birth", no back); `name` → A1 (no back); `path` → A6 (or a stored invite code, or the quiet Relationship of P-11); then R1 after a Relationship choice. W2 final review **M-3**: an account with `needs` ∋ `consent` gets A5c before home on every launch until accepted (HomeScreen's latch sends it because `isComplete` is false; A5c's "Not now" signs out). **M-5**: a signed-in person whose server answer has `has_account` false and nothing queued goes to `returningSignUpLocation` (W2's `/signup?entry=returning`), where W2's coordinator adopts the signed-in session without a second code and shows N1; never home.
- **P-10 (H1 lands in W3a).** §3.15's H1 (account on hold) and the under-18 catch-up answer (`set_my_birth_date` → `{status:'not_eligible'}`) both open `AccountHoldStep`, which only offers "Contact us" and "Sign out" (no animation). It is built with the gate, not later.
- **P-11 (an open space is Relationship).** A legacy account with a linked or paused relationship whose server still asks for `path` is set to Relationship without a question (§3.11), once per visit (`_quietTried`); a server that keeps asking then gets A6, never a loop.
- **P-12 (stored invite after the name).** A stored invite code (W2's `OnboardingStore.pendingInviteCode`) is accepted only once the name is known, through today's `RelationshipInviteService.acceptInvite`, and then the path is set to Relationship quietly; RI-3's confirmation is W4b's.
- **P-13 (W2 step widgets reused).** `NameStep.onBack` and `BirthDateStep.onBack` become nullable (null: no back button) and `BirthDateStep` takes an optional `title`; W2's own calls are unchanged.
- **P-14 (the device wipe registry, §5.8).** `DeviceWipeRegistry.instance`: entries with an id, an order, moments (`signOut`, `quickExit`), a bound ≤ 2 s (default 1 s), `protective` and the prefs `keys` they touch. `register` refuses a duplicate id, no moment, a bad bound, or a protective entry on quick exit (assert in debug). `run(moment)` runs orders in sequence and one order side by side, each entry in its own try with its bound, the whole run bounded at 3 s, never throws. Call sites: `quick_exit_service.dart` (unawaited, after the story wipe, so the exit never waits), `auth_provider.dart` (in the existing `Future.wait`), `safety_sign_out.dart` (after the story wipe). `main.dart` registers Spec 1's entries after `_initializeDeepLinks()`. The home-state cache entry (`spec1.home_state_cache`) runs at sign-out and clears **every** account's `HomeStateStore` key (`attune.home.<uid>.state`), so the next person on a shared phone inherits none; quick exit leaves it (the session stays). W2's age-gate keys (`attune.age_gate.guest_ok`, `attune.age_block.until`) are not registered and so survive both, as W2 requires.
- **P-15 (ChatCouplesLockedScreen copy).** Removing the Dating button (R-5) leaves three sentences that point to it ("…tapping "Meet someone in Dating mode."", "…prepare for dating.", "…or meet someone new in Dating mode."). They are trimmed so nothing on the Chat tab mentions Dating. *Changes spec intent* (the spec names only the button).
- **P-16 (Dating slot states).** §3.9's slot shows "Set up Dating" / "Not ready?" when Dating is open in the person's country (a country chosen on this phone, else the account's phone number's, against W1's `datingOpenCountriesProvider`), "Not open in {country} yet" with "Change country", a closed state when Dating is off everywhere, "Choose your country" when none is known, and the gentle wait inside an ending's window (§3.13). Behind the Dating lock it is one neutral row until the Safety PIN is entered. The chosen country is kept under the neutral key `attune.flow.<uid>.country_override` (D20), wiped at sign-out (`spec1.me_device`). The slot reads only the home state's `dating` summary; no Dating RPC (I-4).
- **P-17 ("Not ready?").** With nothing enrolled it switches to Just for me in one tap; with a Dating profile it asks first ("Pause Dating?"), because the switch pauses Dating (D18).
- **P-18 (one switch helper).** A6, J4 ("More ways"), the Dating slot and Settings all switch through `choosePath` (`path_chooser.dart`) and `switchPath` (`path_switch.dart`), which map every `set_my_path` refusal to its confirmation: `path:confirm_withdraw` → "Cancel your invite?", `path:confirm_pause_dating` → "Pause Dating?", `path:relationship_open` → Settings' "Pause or end first" sheet. Settings never queues a switch offline ("Connect to change this").
- **P-19 (J1 and the journal).** J1 ("Start here" → "Saved, just for you.") is device-only (`attune.flow.<uid>.first_action`, wiped at sign-out), and reads whether a first note or quiz exists from today's providers; the journal opens through today's `RouteNames.reflectionJournal`.
- **P-20 (reminder hours).** §3.10 J3 allows "any time"; §2.4's quiet hours (22:00–08:00) would move a 22:30 reminder to 08:00 the next day. The server and the sheet allow only 08:00–21:59 local and explain the refusal. *Changes spec intent.*
- **P-21 (how reminders are queued).** `enqueue_journal_reminders(p_now timestamptz DEFAULT now())` runs every 15 minutes and queues one `scheduled_notifications` row per person per local day (`source_key journal_reminder:{user}:{local date}`) for reminders due within ±15 minutes, in the person's own zone from `notification_recipient_tz` (never guessed: no zone, no reminder), skipping open holds, only when the reminder was set before it was due; a change cancels the queued row and frees its key (`last_error 'journal_reminder_changed'`). The push is neutral ("Attune" / "A few quiet minutes for you, if you'd like.", type `journal_reminder`). `p_now` exists for the contract; the cron passes nothing.
- **P-22 (J3 in Settings too).** Settings → Notifications gains "Remind me to write" next to J3 on Me and R3, so the reminder can be found and turned off from one place. *Owner decision 4.*
- **P-23 (contract fixtures).** The reminder contract's fixture phones are `+15558800NN` and ids `3e3c0000-…-0000000000NN`, outside every other contract's ranges.
- **P-24 (automatic push ask).** Today `attach()` prompts for notifications after sign-in. Under `flow_paths_v2` (§2.4) it no longer prompts on Just for me, Dating or a pending invite (the in-context asks replace it), but a **linked or paused couple keeps today's automatic ask while `flow_couples_v2` is off**, because R4 (the in-context ask after joining) is W4b's; once `flow_couples_v2` is on, no automatic ask remains (`automaticPushAskAllowed`). *Changes spec intent* (§2.4 says no automatic ask).
- **P-25 (the in-context ask).** `PushAskRow` shows one reason line and "Turn on"; it asks the OS once, and after a refusal opens the app's settings page instead (`permission_handler`'s `openAppSettings`), and rechecks on resume. R2 says "your partner" (W4b adds the first name). It counts `push_ask_shown:<ctx>` / `push_ask_granted:<ctx>` (W1's metric prefixes).
- **P-26 (`journal_reminder` tap).** The worker forwards `type`; `notification_config.dart` routes `journal_reminder` to `homeLocation(tab: AppTab.me)` and then pushes the journal after the frame, so Back returns to Me.
- **P-27 (manual gating).** The "How you use Attune" manual topic declares `flow_signin_v2` and `flow_paths_v2` in `DocumentationRegistry`'s `_requiredFlags`, and the flags guard test's allow-list gains it; the journal topic gains "No reminders unless you set one."
- **P-28 (copy ASCII).** The only non-ASCII character the plan writes is "→" in the manual topic's Dart source (as a literal, the file's own style); every ARB fragment is ASCII.
- **P-29 (R3 before W4b).** R3 shows today's `PendingInviteGuide` (v1) and today's `InviteCard`; W4b's countdown, day-7 "Send a new invite" and share sheet replace them. R3's "Cancel this invite" lives in the invite's overflow menu, asks first, and withdraws through `HomeStateService.withdrawPendingInvites(returnToPreviousPath: true)` (W1's RPC: the path goes back to what it was before the invite); day 7's "Use Attune on my own for now" withdraws through today's `RelationshipInviteService` and keeps Relationship (Q7).
- **P-30 (`InviteCard`'s code).** `InviteCard`'s `SelectableText` fails `androidTapTargetGuideline`; it isn't changed (W4b replaces it). R3's guideline test runs with no invite (the create call refused), so the guideline covers everything R3 itself adds.
- **P-31 (tests and `ProviderScope`).** `pumpWidget` with a new `ProviderScope` reuses the old element; each helper pumps a `SizedBox` first. Long screens are tested in a 390 × 2400 view so `ListView` builds every row.
- **P-32 (Games guard, R2).** `test/architecture/games_untouched_test.dart` pins every Games route's name and path (39 routes), the SHA-256 of each Games route's source block in `app_router.dart`, the chat screen's Games imports, and `auth_exports.dart`'s hash, so any task that edits `app_router.dart` (3, 5, 12) proves it left Games alone.
- **P-33 (flags on the phone).** The signed-in flags are read from `public.feature_flags` (rows `flow_paths_v2`, `flow_couples_v2`; `flow_signin_v2` stays W2's public-config reader) and cached under `attune.flags.signed_in.v1`; effective values apply §2.5's dependencies; an unreadable flag keeps the cached value, else is off; no SharedPreferences (a bare host) is off, never a throw. `FeatureFlagRefresher` refreshes them with the others.
- **P-34 (W2 dependency).** The gate imports `returningSignUpLocation` from W2's `lib/app/routing/sign_in_redirect.dart` (W2 final fix wave: the under-18 block covers account creation and guest Opinions only; "I already have an account" stays reachable). WhatsApp codes for numbers with no account are W2's and untouched here.
- **P-35 (the Dating lock).** The lock is stored in the Safety PIN's own storage (`SafetyPinStore.defaultStorage`) under `me_section_lock` = `on`, a neutral key (D20), registered as a protective sign-out-only wipe (it survives quick exit, §5.8). Turning it on with no PIN opens the PIN setup first and stays off; turning it off asks for the PIN.
- **P-36 (no discreet icon here).** W3 adds no app-icon or discreet-mode work; quick exit and the Safety PIN are today's.

## Owner questions (open; the plan builds the recommendation)

1. **Reminder hours (P-20).** *Recommendation:* accept 08:00–21:59 local; any later time would land at 08:00 the next day under quiet hours, which reads as a bug.
2. **Linked couples keep the automatic push ask until `flow_couples_v2` (P-24).** *Recommendation:* accept; without it a linked couple would get no ask at all until W4b's R4.
3. **Trimming the Dating sentences on `ChatCouplesLockedScreen` (P-15).** *Recommendation:* accept; leaving them would point at a button that no longer exists.
4. **"Remind me to write" in Settings → Notifications (P-22).** *Recommendation:* keep it; it is the place people look to turn a reminder off.
5. **G3 (P-1).** *Recommendation:* defer it to the Opinions redesign (Q9); Opinions stays untouched.

## W1/W2 interfaces this plan consumes (adjust here if a later fix renames one)

Create `test/helpers/fake_home_state.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W3: scripted home-state and flag gateways for the W3 tests.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/home_state_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/flow_flags.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

/// get_my_home_state()'s shape (spec §4.3 W1b), with every field settable.
Map<String, dynamic> homeJson({
  bool hasAccount = true,
  String? path = 'just_me',
  String? tab,
  String? pathPrevious,
  List<String> needs = const [],
  bool hold = false,
  bool datingEnabled = false,
  bool datingEnrolled = false,
  String link = 'none',
  String? relationshipId,
  String? partnerFirstName,
  String? inviteExpiresAt,
  String? windowEndsAt,
}) => {
  'has_account': hasAccount,
  'path': path,
  'tab': tab ?? (path == 'relationship' ? 'chat' : 'me'),
  'path_previous': pathPrevious,
  'needs': needs,
  'hold': hold,
  'eula_version': hasAccount ? '2' : null,
  'dating': {'enabled': datingEnabled, 'enrolled': datingEnrolled},
  'link': {
    'state': link,
    'relationship_id': relationshipId,
    'partner_first_name': partnerFirstName,
    'invite_expires_at': inviteExpiresAt,
    'window_ends_at': windowEndsAt,
  },
};

class FakeHomeStateGateway implements HomeStateGateway {
  FakeHomeStateGateway({Map<String, dynamic>? state})
    : state = state ?? homeJson();

  /// What get_my_home_state (and set_my_path) answer.
  Map<String, dynamic> state;

  /// Thrown by the next call, if set (once).
  Object? failNext;

  /// Thrown by every call while set.
  Object? failAlways;

  /// While set and not completed, every call waits for it.
  Completer<void>? hold;

  /// set_my_birth_date's answer.
  String birthDateStatus = 'ok';

  /// set_my_path: what the call does to [state] before it answers.
  void Function(Map<String, dynamic> params)? onSetPath;

  final calls = <(String, Map<String, dynamic>?)>[];
  final savedNames = <(String, String)>[];

  int count(String fn) => calls.where((c) => c.$1 == fn).length;

  @override
  Future<Object?> rpc(String fn, {Map<String, dynamic>? params}) async {
    calls.add((fn, params));
    final wait = hold;
    if (wait != null) await wait.future;
    final once = failNext;
    if (once != null) {
      failNext = null;
      throw once;
    }
    if (failAlways != null) throw failAlways!;
    switch (fn) {
      case 'get_my_home_state':
        return state;
      case 'set_my_path':
        onSetPath?.call(params ?? const {});
        return state;
      case 'set_my_birth_date':
        return {'status': birthDateStatus, 'age': 30};
      case 'withdraw_my_pending_invites':
        return 1;
      default:
        return null;
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> saveDisplayName(String userId, String name) async {
    if (failAlways != null) throw failAlways!;
    savedNames.add((userId, name));
  }

  static PostgrestException refusal(String message) =>
      PostgrestException(message: message, code: 'P0001');
}

class FakeFlowFlagsGateway implements FlowFlagsGateway {
  FakeFlowFlagsGateway({this.pathsV2 = true, this.couplesV2 = false});

  bool pathsV2;
  bool couplesV2;
  Object? failWith;
  int fetches = 0;

  @override
  Future<SignedInFlowFlags> fetch() async {
    fetches++;
    if (failWith != null) throw failWith!;
    return SignedInFlowFlags(pathsV2: pathsV2, couplesV2: couplesV2);
  }
}
```

Create `test/features/onboarding/flow_flags_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W3a (§2.5 A-6, §5.3): flow_paths_v2 and flow_couples_v2 as the app
// reads them: effective (each needs the one before it), the cached copy on
// the first frame, off when nothing can be read.
import 'package:attune/core/providers/shared_prefs_provider.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/sign_up_flags.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/flow_flags.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

import '../../helpers/fake_home_state.dart';

Future<ProviderContainer> _container({
  bool signIn = true,
  FakeFlowFlagsGateway? gateway,
  Map<String, Object> prefsValues = const {},
}) async {
  SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues(prefsValues);
  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  final container = ProviderContainer(
    overrides: [
      sharedPreferencesProvider.overrideWithValue(prefs),
      signInFlowV2Provider.overrideWithValue(signIn),
      flowFlagsGatewayProvider.overrideWithValue(
        gateway ?? FakeFlowFlagsGateway(),
      ),
    ],
  );
  addTearDown(container.dispose);
  return container;
}

void main() {
  test('effective values follow the dependencies', () async {
    for (final (signIn, paths, couples, wantPaths, wantCouples) in [
      (true, true, true, true, true),
      (true, true, false, true, false),
      (true, false, true, false, false),
      (false, true, true, false, false),
    ]) {
      final c = await _container(
        signIn: signIn,
        gateway: FakeFlowFlagsGateway(pathsV2: paths, couplesV2: couples),
      );
      await c.read(flowFlagsProvider.future);
      expect(c.read(flowPathsV2Provider), wantPaths, reason: '$signIn $paths');
      expect(c.read(flowCouplesV2Provider), wantCouples, reason: '$couples');
    }
  });

  test(
    'the first frame uses the cached copy; a fresh read replaces it',
    () async {
      final gateway = FakeFlowFlagsGateway(pathsV2: false);
      final c = await _container(
        gateway: gateway,
        prefsValues: {FlowFlagsCache.key: '{"paths":true,"couples":false}'},
      );
      expect(c.read(flowPathsV2Provider), isTrue, reason: 'cached');
      await c.read(flowFlagsProvider.future);
      expect(c.read(flowPathsV2Provider), isFalse, reason: 'live');
      await Future<void>.delayed(Duration.zero);
      final prefs = c.read(sharedPreferencesProvider);
      expect(
        prefs.getString(FlowFlagsCache.key),
        '{"paths":false,"couples":false}',
      );
    },
  );

  test('an unreadable flag keeps the cache, else is off', () async {
    final failing = FakeFlowFlagsGateway()..failWith = StateError('offline');
    final cached = await _container(
      gateway: failing,
      prefsValues: {FlowFlagsCache.key: '{"paths":true,"couples":true}'},
    );
    await cached.read(flowFlagsProvider.future);
    expect(cached.read(flowPathsV2Provider), isTrue);

    final none = await _container(
      gateway: FakeFlowFlagsGateway()..failWith = StateError('offline'),
    );
    await none.read(flowFlagsProvider.future);
    expect(none.read(flowPathsV2Provider), isFalse);
    expect(none.read(flowCouplesV2Provider), isFalse);
  });

  test('no SharedPreferences in a bare host: off, never a throw', () {
    final c = ProviderContainer(
      overrides: [
        signInFlowV2Provider.overrideWithValue(true),
        flowFlagsGatewayProvider.overrideWithValue(
          FakeFlowFlagsGateway()..failWith = StateError('no backend'),
        ),
      ],
    );
    addTearDown(c.dispose);
    expect(c.read(flowPathsV2Provider), isFalse);
  });

  test('the cache key and its values never name Dating (D20)', () async {
    final c = await _container();
    await c.read(flowFlagsProvider.future);
    await Future<void>.delayed(Duration.zero);
    final prefs = c.read(sharedPreferencesProvider);
    for (final key in prefs.getKeys()) {
      expect(key.toLowerCase(), isNot(contains('dating')));
      expect('${prefs.get(key)}'.toLowerCase(), isNot(contains('dating')));
    }
  });
}
```

Create `test/features/onboarding/home_state_service_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W3a (§4.3 W1b, §3.15): the home's server calls, each refusal by
// its code, and every transient failure as "offline".
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/home_state_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

import '../../helpers/fake_home_state.dart';

void main() {
  test('fetch reads get_my_home_state', () async {
    final gateway = FakeHomeStateGateway(
      state: homeJson(path: 'relationship', link: 'pending'),
    );
    final state = await HomeStateService(gateway).fetch();
    expect(gateway.calls.single.$1, 'get_my_home_state');
    expect(state.path, AppPath.relationship);
    expect(state.tab, AppTab.chat);
    expect(state.link.state, LinkState.pending);
  });

  test('setPath sends the wire value and the confirmation', () async {
    final gateway = FakeHomeStateGateway();
    await HomeStateService(gateway).setPath(AppPath.justMe);
    await HomeStateService(
      gateway,
    ).setPath(AppPath.relationship, confirmed: true);
    expect(gateway.calls.map((c) => c.$1), ['set_my_path', 'set_my_path']);
    expect(gateway.calls[0].$2, {'p_path': 'just_me', 'p_confirmed': false});
    expect(gateway.calls[1].$2, {
      'p_path': 'relationship',
      'p_confirmed': true,
    });
  });

  test('each set_my_path refusal is named', () async {
    for (final (message, reason) in [
      ('path:relationship_open', PathRefusal.relationshipOpen),
      ('path:confirm_withdraw', PathRefusal.confirmWithdraw),
      ('path:confirm_pause_dating', PathRefusal.confirmPauseDating),
      ('path:needs_signup', PathRefusal.needsSignUp),
      ('path:invalid', PathRefusal.invalid),
    ]) {
      final gateway =
          FakeHomeStateGateway()
            ..failNext = FakeHomeStateGateway.refusal(message);
      await expectLater(
        HomeStateService(gateway).setPath(AppPath.dating),
        throwsA(isA<PathRefused>().having((e) => e.reason, 'reason', reason)),
      );
    }
  });

  test('a transient failure is offline', () async {
    for (final failure in <Object>[
      TimeoutException('slow'),
      FakeHomeStateGateway.refusal(
        'canceling statement due to statement timeout',
      ),
    ]) {
      final gateway = FakeHomeStateGateway()..failNext = failure;
      await expectLater(
        HomeStateService(gateway).fetch(),
        throwsA(isA<HomeOffline>()),
      );
    }
  });

  test('the date of birth catch-up: ok, under 18, already set', () async {
    final gateway = FakeHomeStateGateway();
    final service = HomeStateService(gateway);
    expect(
      await service.setBirthDate(DateTime.utc(1991, 11, 8)),
      BirthDateOutcome.ok,
    );
    expect(gateway.calls.last.$2, {'p_birth_date': '1991-11-08'});
    gateway.birthDateStatus = 'not_eligible';
    expect(
      await service.setBirthDate(DateTime.utc(2012, 1, 1)),
      BirthDateOutcome.notEligible,
    );
    gateway.failNext = FakeHomeStateGateway.refusal('birth_date:already_set');
    expect(
      await service.setBirthDate(DateTime.utc(1991, 1, 1)),
      BirthDateOutcome.alreadySet,
    );
  });

  test('updated terms, the name and the withdrawal', () async {
    final gateway = FakeHomeStateGateway();
    final service = HomeStateService(gateway);
    await service.acceptUpdatedTerms();
    expect(gateway.calls.last.$1, 'record_eula_acceptance');
    expect(gateway.calls.last.$2, {'p_version': '2'});
    await service.saveDisplayName('u1', 'Ama');
    expect(gateway.savedNames.single, ('u1', 'Ama'));
    expect(await service.withdrawPendingInvites(returnToPreviousPath: true), 1);
    expect(gateway.calls.last.$1, 'withdraw_my_pending_invites');
    expect(gateway.calls.last.$2, {'p_return_to_previous_path': true});
  });
}
```

```dart
// Spec 1 W3a (§5.3, §5.5, D20, §3.14): the home state as the app holds it:
// the cache on the first frame, the server's answer once it comes, the
// queued A5 and A6 counted as done, and nothing about the path or Dating
// ever written to the phone.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/core/providers/shared_prefs_provider.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/home_state_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/home_state_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/onboarding_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

import '../../helpers/fake_home_state.dart';

const _uid = 'u-w3a';

Future<(ProviderContainer, FakeHomeStateGateway, SharedPreferences)> _build({
  Map<String, Object> prefsValues = const {},
  Map<String, dynamic>? server,
}) async {
  SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues(prefsValues);
  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  final gateway = FakeHomeStateGateway(state: server);
  final container = ProviderContainer(
    overrides: [
      sharedPreferencesProvider.overrideWithValue(prefs),
      homeStateServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(HomeStateService(gateway)),
    ],
  );
  addTearDown(container.dispose);
  return (container, gateway, prefs);
}

String _cache({
  String tab = 'me',
  String link = 'none',
  List<String> needs = const [],
  bool hold = false,
}) =>
    '{"tab":"$tab","link":"$link","needs":[${needs.map((n) => '"$n"').join(',')}],'
    '"hold":$hold,"at":"2026-10-26T08:00:00.000Z"}';

void main() {
  test('nothing known: not complete, no layout', () async {
    final (c, _, _) = await _build();
    final snap = c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid));
    expect(snap.known, isFalse);
    expect(snap.isComplete, isFalse);
  });

  test(
    'the first frame reads the cache; path and Dating stay unknown',
    () async {
      final (c, _, _) = await _build(
        prefsValues: {
          HomeStateStore.keyFor(_uid): _cache(tab: 'chat', link: 'linked'),
        },
      );
      final snap = c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid));
      expect(snap.tab, AppTab.chat);
      expect(snap.link, LinkState.linked);
      expect(snap.isComplete, isTrue);
      expect(snap.path, isNull);
      expect(snap.dating, isNull);
    },
  );

  test(
    'a cached unknown need keeps the home closed (final review M-5)',
    () async {
      final (c, _, _) = await _build(
        prefsValues: {
          HomeStateStore.keyFor(_uid): _cache(needs: ['shoe_size']),
        },
      );
      final snap = c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid));
      expect(snap.needs, [HomeNeed.unknown]);
      expect(snap.isComplete, isFalse);
    },
  );

  test('a hold keeps the home closed', () async {
    final (c, _, _) = await _build(
      prefsValues: {HomeStateStore.keyFor(_uid): _cache(hold: true)},
    );
    expect(c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid)).isComplete, isFalse);
  });

  test(
    'refresh applies the answer and caches only layout, link, needs, hold',
    () async {
      final (c, gateway, prefs) = await _build(
        server: homeJson(
          path: 'dating',
          pathPrevious: 'just_me',
          datingEnabled: true,
          datingEnrolled: true,
          link: 'ended_window',
          windowEndsAt: '2026-11-20T10:00:00Z',
        ),
      );
      final answer = await c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid).notifier).refresh();
      expect(answer, isNotNull);
      final snap = c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid));
      expect(snap.path, AppPath.dating);
      expect(snap.pathPrevious, AppPath.justMe);
      expect(snap.dating?.enrolled, isTrue);
      expect(snap.linkDetail.windowEndsAt, DateTime.utc(2026, 11, 20, 10));
      final raw = prefs.getString(HomeStateStore.keyFor(_uid))!;
      expect(raw, contains('"tab":"me"'));
      expect(raw, contains('"link":"ended_window"'));
      // D20: no path, no Dating, on the phone.
      for (final key in prefs.getKeys()) {
        expect(key.toLowerCase(), isNot(contains('dating')));
        expect('${prefs.get(key)}'.toLowerCase(), isNot(contains('dating')));
        expect('${prefs.get(key)}', isNot(contains('just_me')));
      }
      expect(gateway.count('get_my_home_state'), 1);
    },
  );

  test('two refreshes at once make one call', () async {
    final (c, gateway, _) = await _build();
    gateway.hold = Completer<void>();
    final notifier = c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid).notifier);
    final a = notifier.refresh();
    final b = notifier.refresh();
    gateway.hold!.complete();
    await Future.wait([a, b]);
    expect(gateway.count('get_my_home_state'), 1);
  });

  test('a failed refresh keeps the snapshot', () async {
    final (c, gateway, _) = await _build(
      prefsValues: {
        HomeStateStore.keyFor(_uid): _cache(tab: 'chat', link: 'pending'),
      },
    );
    gateway.failAlways = TimeoutException('offline');
    expect(await c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid).notifier).refresh(), isNull);
    expect(c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid)).tab, AppTab.chat);
  });

  test('a queued A5 counts as consent, birth date and name', () async {
    final pending = PendingSignUp(
      displayName: 'Ama',
      birthDate: DateTime.utc(1990, 1, 1),
    );
    final (c, _, prefs) = await _build();
    final store = OnboardingStore(prefs, scope: 'user.$_uid');
    await store.savePendingSignUp(pending);
    c.invalidate(homeStateProvider(_uid));
    final offline = c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid));
    expect(offline.needs, [
      HomeNeed.path,
    ], reason: 'a new account: only A6 left');
    expect(offline.isComplete, isFalse);

    await c
        .read(homeStateProvider(_uid).notifier)
        .apply(
          HomeState.fromJson(
            homeJson(
              hasAccount: false,
              path: null,
              needs: ['consent', 'birth_date', 'name', 'path'],
            ),
          ),
        );
    expect(c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid)).needs, [HomeNeed.path]);
  });

  test('a queued A6 counts as the path and sets the layout', () async {
    final (c, _, prefs) = await _build(
      prefsValues: {
        HomeStateStore.keyFor(_uid): _cache(needs: ['path']),
      },
    );
    await OnboardingStore(
      prefs,
      scope: 'user.$_uid',
    ).savePendingPath(AppPath.relationship);
    c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid).notifier).reloadLocal();
    final snap = c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid));
    expect(snap.needs, isEmpty);
    expect(snap.tab, AppTab.chat);
    expect(snap.isComplete, isTrue);
  });

  test('a Dating choice is never queued (D20)', () async {
    SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues({});
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final store = OnboardingStore(prefs, scope: 'user.$_uid');
    expect(() => store.savePendingPath(AppPath.dating), throwsArgumentError);
    expect(prefs.getKeys(), isEmpty);
  });

  test("the first v2 answer drops this account's legacy mode once", () async {
    final (c, _, prefs) = await _build(
      prefsValues: {
        'attune.onboarding.user.$_uid.mode': 'couples',
        'attune.onboarding.user.other.mode': 'couples',
      },
    );
    await c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid).notifier).refresh();
    expect(prefs.containsKey('attune.onboarding.user.$_uid.mode'), isFalse);
    expect(prefs.getString('attune.onboarding.user.other.mode'), 'couples');
  });

  test('invalidating forgets the live answer (sign-out)', () async {
    final (c, _, _) = await _build(server: homeJson(path: 'dating'));
    await c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid).notifier).refresh();
    expect(c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid)).path, AppPath.dating);
    c.invalidate(homeStateProvider(_uid));
    expect(c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid)).path, isNull);
    expect(
      c.read(homeStateProvider(_uid)).tab,
      AppTab.me,
      reason: 'cache stays',
    );
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the flag reader**

Create `lib/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/flow_flags.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:attune/core/providers/shared_prefs_provider.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/sign_up_flags.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

/// The two signed-in Spec 1 flags W3 reads (spec §2.5, §5.3), as stored:
/// `flow_paths_v2` and `flow_couples_v2`. Each provider below turns them
/// into the effective value (the flag and everything it needs).
@immutable
class SignedInFlowFlags {
  const SignedInFlowFlags({required this.pathsV2, required this.couplesV2});

  /// What an unreadable flag means: off.
  static const off = SignedInFlowFlags(pathsV2: false, couplesV2: false);

  final bool pathsV2;
  final bool couplesV2;

  @override
  bool operator ==(Object other) =>
      other is SignedInFlowFlags &&
      other.pathsV2 == pathsV2 &&
      other.couplesV2 == couplesV2;

  @override
  int get hashCode => Object.hash(pathsV2, couplesV2);
}

/// The one network read, so tests can replace it.
abstract class FlowFlagsGateway {
  Future<SignedInFlowFlags> fetch();
}

class SupabaseFlowFlagsGateway implements FlowFlagsGateway {
  static const timeout = Duration(seconds: 10);

  @override
  Future<SignedInFlowFlags> fetch() async {
    final rows = await Supabase.instance.client
        .from('feature_flags')
        .select('key, enabled')
        .inFilter('key', const ['flow_paths_v2', 'flow_couples_v2'])
        .timeout(timeout);
    bool on(String key) =>
        rows.any((row) => row['key'] == key && row['enabled'] == true);
    return SignedInFlowFlags(
      pathsV2: on('flow_paths_v2'),
      couplesV2: on('flow_couples_v2'),
    );
  }
}

/// The last answer, for the first frame of the next launch. Neutral key
/// names (D20).
class FlowFlagsCache {
  const FlowFlagsCache(this._prefs);

  final SharedPreferences _prefs;

  static const key = 'attune.flags.signed_in.v1';

  SignedInFlowFlags? read() {
    final raw = _prefs.getString(key);
    if (raw == null || raw.isEmpty) return null;
    try {
      final json = jsonDecode(raw);
      if (json is! Map) return null;
      return SignedInFlowFlags(
        pathsV2: json['paths'] == true,
        couplesV2: json['couples'] == true,
      );
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  Future<void> write(SignedInFlowFlags flags) => _prefs.setString(
    key,
    jsonEncode({'paths': flags.pathsV2, 'couples': flags.couplesV2}),
  );
}

final flowFlagsGatewayProvider = Provider<FlowFlagsGateway>(
  (ref) => SupabaseFlowFlagsGateway(),
);

SignedInFlowFlags? _cached(Ref ref) {
  try {
    return FlowFlagsCache(ref.read(sharedPreferencesProvider)).read();
  } catch (_) {
    // No SharedPreferences in a bare test host.
    return null;
  }
}

/// The live flags. A failed read keeps the cached copy, else both off.
/// Re-read on resume and on a change of user (FeatureFlagRefresher).
final flowFlagsProvider = FutureProvider<SignedInFlowFlags>((ref) async {
  final SignedInFlowFlags flags;
  try {
    flags = await ref.read(flowFlagsGatewayProvider).fetch();
  } catch (error) {
    debugPrint('[flow-flags] read failed: ${error.runtimeType}');
    return _cached(ref) ?? SignedInFlowFlags.off;
  }
  unawaited(() async {
    try {
      await FlowFlagsCache(ref.read(sharedPreferencesProvider)).write(flags);
    } catch (error) {
      debugPrint('[flow-flags] cache write failed: ${error.runtimeType}');
    }
  }());
  return flags;
});

SignedInFlowFlags _current(Ref ref) =>
    ref.watch(flowFlagsProvider).valueOrNull ??
    _cached(ref) ??
    SignedInFlowFlags.off;

/// flow_paths_v2, effective: the flag and flow_signin_v2 (spec §2.5 A-6).
/// Before the first answer: the cached copy, else off.
final flowPathsV2Provider = Provider<bool>((ref) {
  if (!ref.watch(signInFlowV2Provider)) return false;
  return _current(ref).pathsV2;
});

/// flow_couples_v2, effective: the flag and flow_paths_v2.
final flowCouplesV2Provider = Provider<bool>((ref) {
  if (!ref.watch(flowPathsV2Provider)) return false;
  return _current(ref).couplesV2;
});

/// The live flow_paths_v2, once read (bounded): for code that decides
/// once, such as the automatic push ask, and must not act on a stale
/// first-frame value.
Future<bool> readFlowPathsV2(
  Ref ref, {
  Duration bound = const Duration(seconds: 3),
}) async {
  try {
    if (!ref.read(signInFlowV2Provider)) return false;
    final flags = await ref.read(flowFlagsProvider.future).timeout(bound);
    return flags.pathsV2;
  } catch (_) {
    return ref.read(flowPathsV2Provider);
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `HomeStateService`**

Create `lib/features/onboarding/data/home_state_service.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

/// The server calls behind the home, the catch-up steps and switching
/// (Spec 1 §3.7, §3.12, §3.15, §4.3 W1b), behind one seam so they can be
/// tested without a backend.
abstract class HomeStateGateway {
  Future<Object?> rpc(String fn, {Map<String, dynamic>? params});

  /// A1's catch-up for an account without a name: the users row (granted
  /// column) and profiles.display_name (games read it), as today's
  /// onboarding writes them.
  Future<void> saveDisplayName(String userId, String name);
}

class SupabaseHomeStateGateway implements HomeStateGateway {
  SupabaseHomeStateGateway([SupabaseClient? client]) : _client = client;

  final SupabaseClient? _client;

  SupabaseClient get _db => _client ?? Supabase.instance.client;

  @override
  Future<Object?> rpc(String fn, {Map<String, dynamic>? params}) =>
      _db.rpc(fn, params: params);

  @override
  Future<void> saveDisplayName(String userId, String name) async {
    await _db.from('users').update({'display_name': name}).eq('id', userId);
    await _db.from('profiles').upsert({
      'id': userId,
      'display_name': name,
      'updated_at': DateTime.now().toUtc().toIso8601String(),
    }, onConflict: 'id');
  }
}

/// set_my_path's refusals (spec §4.3 W1b), each answered by a screen.
enum PathRefusal {
  /// `path:relationship_open`: a shared space is open or paused.
  relationshipOpen,

  /// `path:confirm_withdraw`: own pending invites would be withdrawn.
  confirmWithdraw,

  /// `path:confirm_pause_dating`: Dating would be paused.
  confirmPauseDating,

  /// `path:needs_signup`: no account, no date of birth or an older EULA.
  needsSignUp,

  /// Any other `path:*` refusal.
  invalid,
}

class PathRefused implements Exception {
  const PathRefused(this.reason);

  final PathRefusal reason;
}

/// set_my_birth_date's answer (spec §3.15 A2).
enum BirthDateOutcome { ok, notEligible, alreadySet }

/// The server couldn't be reached or answered with a transient error.
class HomeOffline implements Exception {
  const HomeOffline();
}

class HomeStateService {
  HomeStateService(this._gateway);

  final HomeStateGateway _gateway;

  static const timeout = Duration(seconds: 15);

  /// get_my_home_state().
  Future<HomeState> fetch() async => _state(await _call('get_my_home_state'));

  /// set_my_path (A6, switching); returns the new home state.
  Future<HomeState> setPath(AppPath path, {bool confirmed = false}) async =>
      _state(
        await _call(
          'set_my_path',
          params: {'p_path': path.wire, 'p_confirmed': confirmed},
        ),
      );

  /// A2's catch-up for a legacy account (spec §3.15).
  Future<BirthDateOutcome> setBirthDate(DateTime birthDate) async {
    final Object? data;
    try {
      data = await _call(
        'set_my_birth_date',
        params: {'p_birth_date': _dateOnly(birthDate)},
      );
    } on _Refused catch (refused) {
      if (refused.message.startsWith('birth_date:already_set')) {
        return BirthDateOutcome.alreadySet;
      }
      throw const HomeOffline();
    }
    final status = data is Map ? data['status'] : null;
    return status == 'not_eligible'
        ? BirthDateOutcome.notEligible
        : BirthDateOutcome.ok;
  }

  /// A5c for an account on an older EULA (spec §3.15).
  Future<void> acceptUpdatedTerms() async {
    await _call('record_eula_acceptance', params: {'p_version': '2'});
  }

  /// A1's catch-up (spec §3.15).
  Future<void> saveDisplayName(String userId, String name) async {
    try {
      await _gateway.saveDisplayName(userId, name).timeout(timeout);
    } on PostgrestException {
      throw const HomeOffline();
    } on TimeoutException {
      throw const HomeOffline();
    } on IOException {
      throw const HomeOffline();
    } on http.ClientException {
      throw const HomeOffline();
    }
  }

  /// R3's "Cancel this invite" (spec §3.8): withdraws this person's pending
  /// invites and, with [returnToPreviousPath], restores the path they came
  /// from. Returns how many were withdrawn.
  Future<int> withdrawPendingInvites({
    required bool returnToPreviousPath,
  }) async {
    final data = await _call(
      'withdraw_my_pending_invites',
      params: {'p_return_to_previous_path': returnToPreviousPath},
    );
    return data is int ? data : 0;
  }

  static String _dateOnly(DateTime d) =>
      '${d.year.toString().padLeft(4, '0')}-'
      '${d.month.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}-'
      '${d.day.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';

  HomeState _state(Object? data) {
    if (data is! Map) throw const HomeOffline();
    return HomeState.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(data));
  }

  Future<Object?> _call(String fn, {Map<String, dynamic>? params}) async {
    try {
      return await _gateway.rpc(fn, params: params).timeout(timeout);
    } on PostgrestException catch (error) {
      final message = error.message;
      if (message.startsWith('path:')) throw PathRefused(_pathRefusal(message));
      if (message.startsWith('birth_date:')) throw _Refused(message);
      throw const HomeOffline();
    } on TimeoutException {
      throw const HomeOffline();
    } on IOException {
      throw const HomeOffline();
    } on http.ClientException {
      throw const HomeOffline();
    } on AuthRetryableFetchException {
      throw const HomeOffline();
    }
  }

  static PathRefusal _pathRefusal(String message) {
    if (message.startsWith('path:relationship_open')) {
      return PathRefusal.relationshipOpen;
    }
    if (message.startsWith('path:confirm_withdraw')) {
      return PathRefusal.confirmWithdraw;
    }
    if (message.startsWith('path:confirm_pause_dating')) {
      return PathRefusal.confirmPauseDating;
    }
    if (message.startsWith('path:needs_signup')) return PathRefusal.needsSignUp;
    return PathRefusal.invalid;
  }
}

class _Refused implements Exception {
  const _Refused(this.message);

  final String message;
}

final homeStateServiceProvider = Provider<HomeStateService>(
  (ref) => HomeStateService(SupabaseHomeStateGateway()),
);
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `homeStateProvider`**

Create `lib/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/core/providers/shared_prefs_provider.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/home_state_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/home_state_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/onboarding_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

/// What the home knows about the signed-in account right now (Spec 1
/// §5.3): the tab layout, link state, needs and hold, from the server's
/// last answer this run, else from the phone's cache; plus what the phone
/// still owes the server (a queued A5 or A6, §3.14). The path, the path
/// before it, the link details and the Dating summary come only from a
/// live answer and are never kept on the phone (D20).
@immutable
class HomeSnapshot {
  const HomeSnapshot({
    this.tab,
    this.link = LinkState.none,
    this.needs = const [],
    this.hold = false,
    this.server,
  });

  /// Nothing known: no cache, no answer, nothing queued.
  static const unknown = HomeSnapshot();

  /// Null until something says which layout this account has.
  final AppTab? tab;
  final LinkState link;
  final List<HomeNeed> needs;
  final bool hold;

  /// This run's live answer, in memory only.
  final HomeState? server;

  bool get known => tab != null;

  /// The v2 home may open: the layout is known, nothing is missing, no
  /// hold. An unrecognised need keeps it closed (HomeNeed.unknown).
  bool get isComplete => known && needs.isEmpty && !hold;

  AppPath? get path => server?.path;
  AppPath? get pathPrevious => server?.pathPrevious;
  DatingSummary? get dating => server?.dating;
  HomeLink get linkDetail => server?.link ?? HomeLink(state: link);

  factory HomeSnapshot.fromCache(CachedHomeState cache) => HomeSnapshot(
    tab: cache.tab,
    link: cache.link,
    needs: cache.needs,
    hold: cache.hold,
  );

  factory HomeSnapshot.fromServer(HomeState state) => HomeSnapshot(
    tab: state.tab,
    link: state.link.state,
    needs: state.needs,
    hold: state.hold,
    server: state,
  );

  /// The queued writes count as done (§3.14): a queued A5 carries the
  /// consent, the date of birth and the name (and makes a brand-new
  /// account, so only A6 is left when nothing else is known); a queued A6
  /// carries the path and decides the layout.
  HomeSnapshot withQueued({required bool signUp, AppPath? path}) {
    var needs = this.needs;
    var tab = this.tab;
    if (signUp) {
      needs =
          known
              ? [
                for (final need in needs)
                  if (need != HomeNeed.consent &&
                      need != HomeNeed.birthDate &&
                      need != HomeNeed.name)
                    need,
              ]
              : const [HomeNeed.path];
    }
    if (path != null) {
      needs = [
        for (final need in needs)
          if (need != HomeNeed.path) need,
      ];
      tab = AppTab.forPath(path);
    }
    return HomeSnapshot(
      tab: tab,
      link: link,
      needs: needs,
      hold: hold,
      server: server,
    );
  }
}

/// One per signed-in account (keyed by user id). Synchronous, so the first
/// frame never waits: it opens on the cache and changes when [refresh] or
/// [apply] brings a server answer. HomeScreen refreshes it on launch,
/// resume, sign-in and relationshipModeResyncSignal; the gate and the
/// switch screens apply the answers their own calls return.
class HomeStateNotifier extends FamilyNotifier<HomeSnapshot, String> {
  HomeState? _server;
  Future<HomeState?>? _inFlight;

  SharedPreferences get _prefs => ref.read(sharedPreferencesProvider);

  OnboardingStore get _store =>
      OnboardingStore(_prefs, scope: '${OnboardingStore.userScopePrefix}.$arg');

  @override
  HomeSnapshot build(String userId) {
    // Invalidating this provider forgets the live answer (sign-out).
    _server = null;
    _inFlight = null;
    return _compose();
  }

  HomeSnapshot _compose() {
    final server = _server;
    final HomeSnapshot base;
    if (server != null) {
      base = HomeSnapshot.fromServer(server);
    } else {
      final cache = HomeStateStore(_prefs, userId: arg).read();
      base =
          cache == null ? HomeSnapshot.unknown : HomeSnapshot.fromCache(cache);
    }
    final store = _store;
    return base.withQueued(
      signUp: store.pendingSignUp != null,
      path: store.pendingPath,
    );
  }

  /// get_my_home_state(), once at a time. Null when it couldn't be read;
  /// the snapshot then stays as it was.
  Future<HomeState?> refresh() =>
      _inFlight ??= _refresh().whenComplete(() => _inFlight = null);

  Future<HomeState?> _refresh() async {
    try {
      final answer = await ref.read(homeStateServiceProvider).fetch();
      await apply(answer);
      return answer;
    } catch (error) {
      debugPrint('[home-state] refresh failed: ${error.runtimeType}');
      return null;
    }
  }

  /// A server answer (from [refresh], set_my_path or the gate): kept in
  /// memory, and its tab layout, link, needs and hold cached for the next
  /// launch's first frame.
  Future<void> apply(HomeState answer) async {
    _server = answer;
    state = _compose();
    try {
      await HomeStateStore(_prefs, userId: arg).write(answer);
      // Spec §5.5: the v2 home replaces OnboardingMode for this account;
      // the legacy sync rebuilds it if flow_paths_v2 is switched off.
      final legacyMode =
          'attune.onboarding.${OnboardingStore.userScopePrefix}.$arg.mode';
      if (_prefs.containsKey(legacyMode)) await _prefs.remove(legacyMode);
    } catch (error) {
      debugPrint('[home-state] cache write failed: ${error.runtimeType}');
    }
  }

  /// After a local change the snapshot reads (a queued A6 choice, a
  /// replayed or dropped queue).
  void reloadLocal() => state = _compose();
}

final homeStateProvider =
    NotifierProvider.family<HomeStateNotifier, HomeSnapshot, String>(
      HomeStateNotifier.new,
    );
```

```dart
  /// Replays A6's queued Relationship or Just for me (Spec 1 §3.7, §3.14;
  /// never Dating, D20). A refusal that a replay can't fix drops it, and
  /// the gate asks again; offline, or an account not ready yet, keeps it.
  Future<void> _flushPath(
    OnboardingStore store,
    void Function(HomeState state)? onPathSynced,
  ) async {
    final path = store.pendingPath;
    if (path == null) return;
    try {
      final service =
          _homeStateService ?? HomeStateService(SupabaseHomeStateGateway());
      final state = await service.setPath(path);
      onPathSynced?.call(state);
      await store.clearPendingPath();
      debugPrint('[onboarding] pending path synced');
    } on PathRefused catch (refused) {
      debugPrint('[onboarding] pending path refused: ${refused.reason.name}');
      if (refused.reason != PathRefusal.needsSignUp) {
        await store.clearPendingPath();
      }
    } catch (error) {
      debugPrint('[onboarding] pending path kept: ${error.runtimeType}');
    }
  }
```

Create `test/core/device/device_wipe_registry_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W3a (§5.8, D19): the device wipe registry runs in order, each
// entry in its own try and bound, never throws, and refuses a protective
// entry on quick exit.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/core/device/device_wipe_registry.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

DeviceWipeEntry _entry(
  String id,
  List<String> log, {
  int order = 0,
  Set<WipeMoment> moments = const {WipeMoment.signOut},
  Future<void> Function()? run,
  Duration bound = const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
  bool protective = false,
}) => DeviceWipeEntry(
  id: id,
  order: order,
  moments: moments,
  bound: bound,
  protective: protective,
  run: run ?? () async => log.add(id),
);

void main() {
  test('runs only the moment asked for, lower orders first', () async {
    final log = <String>[];
    final registry =
        DeviceWipeRegistry()
          ..register(_entry('late', log, order: 2))
          ..register(_entry('early', log, order: 1))
          ..register(_entry('exit only', log, moments: {WipeMoment.quickExit}))
          ..register(
            _entry(
              'both',
              log,
              order: 3,
              moments: {WipeMoment.signOut, WipeMoment.quickExit},
            ),
          );
    await registry.run(WipeMoment.signOut);
    expect(log, ['early', 'late', 'both']);
    log.clear();
    await registry.run(WipeMoment.quickExit);
    expect(log, ['exit only', 'both']);
  });

  test('one order runs side by side; a failing entry skips no other', () async {
    final log = <String>[];
    final gate = Completer<void>();
    final registry =
        DeviceWipeRegistry()
          ..register(
            _entry(
              'waits',
              log,
              run: () async {
                await gate.future;
                log.add('waits');
              },
            ),
          )
          ..register(
            _entry(
              'opens',
              log,
              run: () async {
                log.add('opens');
                gate.complete();
              },
            ),
          )
          ..register(
            _entry('throws', log, run: () async => throw StateError('disk')),
          )
          ..register(_entry('after', log, order: 1));
    await registry.run(WipeMoment.signOut);
    expect(log, ['opens', 'waits', 'after']);
  });

  test(
    'a hanging entry is cut at its bound; the next order still runs',
    () async {
      final log = <String>[];
      final registry =
          DeviceWipeRegistry()
            ..register(
              _entry(
                'hangs',
                log,
                bound: const Duration(milliseconds: 50),
                run: () => Completer<void>().future,
              ),
            )
            ..register(_entry('next', log, order: 1));
      final clock = Stopwatch()..start();
      await registry.run(WipeMoment.signOut);
      expect(log, ['next']);
      expect(clock.elapsed, lessThan(const Duration(seconds: 1)));
    },
  );

  test('the whole run is bounded and never throws', () async {
    final registry = DeviceWipeRegistry(
      totalBound: const Duration(milliseconds: 100),
    );
    for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      registry.register(
        _entry(
          'slow$i',
          [],
          order: i,
          bound: const Duration(seconds: 2),
          run: () => Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(seconds: 2)),
        ),
      );
    }
    final clock = Stopwatch()..start();
    await registry.run(WipeMoment.signOut);
    expect(clock.elapsed, lessThan(const Duration(seconds: 1)));
  });

  test(
    'refuses a duplicate id, no moment, a bad bound, a protective quick exit',
    () {
      final registry = DeviceWipeRegistry()..register(_entry('a', []));
      for (final bad in [
        _entry('a', []),
        _entry('none', [], moments: const {}),
        _entry('zero', [], bound: Duration.zero),
        _entry('long', [], bound: const Duration(seconds: 3)),
        _entry(
          'lock',
          [],
          protective: true,
          moments: {WipeMoment.signOut, WipeMoment.quickExit},
        ),
      ]) {
        expect(
          () => registry.register(bad),
          throwsA(isA<AssertionError>()),
          reason: bad.id,
        );
      }
      expect(registry.entries.map((e) => e.id), ['a']);
      expect(
        registry.register(_entry('lock', [], protective: true)),
        isTrue,
        reason: 'a protective entry may run on sign-out',
      );
    },
  );
}
```

Create `test/app/device_wipes_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W3a (§5.8): Spec 1's device wipes. The home-state cache goes on
// sign-out for every account on the phone, and nothing else with it; no
// quick-exit entry touches a protective setting (the Dating lock, the
// Safety PIN).
import 'package:attune/app/device_wipes.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/device/device_wipe_registry.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/home_state_store.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

/// Every device key a protective setting uses (D19). No discreet-icon
/// choice exists in the app yet (W3 plan P-14).
const protectiveKeys = {
  'me_section_lock',
  'safety_pin_hash',
  'safety_pin_failures',
  'safety_pin_retry_at',
  'safety_pin_last_failure_at',
  'safety_pin_every_open',
  'safety_pin_expected',
  'safety_requires_unlock',
};

void main() {
  test('Spec 1 registers its entries once, each valid', () {
    final registry = DeviceWipeRegistry();
    registerDeviceWipes(registry);
    expect(
      registry.entries.map((e) => e.id),
      contains('spec1.home_state_cache'),
    );
    final home = registry.entries.firstWhere(
      (e) => e.id == 'spec1.home_state_cache',
    );
    expect(home.moments, {WipeMoment.signOut});
  });

  test(
    'sign-out removes every account\'s home-state cache and nothing else',
    () async {
      SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues({
        HomeStateStore.keyFor('u1'): '{}',
        HomeStateStore.keyFor('u2'): '{}',
        'attune.home.u1.other': 'kept',
        'attune.age_block.until': 'kept',
        'safety_pin_expected': true,
      });
      final registry = DeviceWipeRegistry();
      registerDeviceWipes(registry);
      await registry.run(WipeMoment.signOut);
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      expect(prefs.getKeys(), {
        'attune.home.u1.other',
        'attune.age_block.until',
        'safety_pin_expected',
      });
    },
  );

  test('no quick-exit entry touches a protective key; none is protective', () {
    final registry = DeviceWipeRegistry();
    registerDeviceWipes(registry);
    for (final entry in registry.entries) {
      if (!entry.moments.contains(WipeMoment.quickExit)) continue;
      expect(entry.protective, isFalse, reason: entry.id);
      expect(
        entry.keys.intersection(protectiveKeys),
        isEmpty,
        reason: entry.id,
      );
    }
    for (final entry in registry.entries.where((e) => e.protective)) {
      expect(
        entry.moments,
        isNot(contains(WipeMoment.quickExit)),
        reason: entry.id,
      );
    }
  });

  test('no Spec 1 device key names Dating (D20)', () {
    final registry = DeviceWipeRegistry();
    registerDeviceWipes(registry);
    for (final entry in registry.entries) {
      for (final key in entry.keys) {
        expect(key.toLowerCase(), isNot(contains('dating')), reason: entry.id);
      }
    }
  });
}
```

Create `test/features/auth/auth_sign_out_device_wipe_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W3a (§5.6 R19, §5.8): sign-out runs the device wipe registry beside
// its four existing wipes and the push release; a hanging entry is cut at
// its bound and the sign-out still lands.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/app/routing/routing_notifier.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/device/device_wipe_registry.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/providers/routing_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/providers/auth_provider.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/services/auth_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/chat/data/cache/chat_cache_service.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

class _FakeCache implements ChatCacheService {
  _FakeCache(this.events);
  final List<String> events;

  @override
  Future<void> clearAll() async => events.add('cache wiped');

  @override
  dynamic noSuchMethod(Invocation invocation) => super.noSuchMethod(invocation);
}

class _FakeAuth implements AuthService {
  _FakeAuth(this.events);
  final List<String> events;

  @override
  Future<void> signOut() async => events.add('auth signed out');

  @override
  User? get currentUser => null;

  @override
  dynamic noSuchMethod(Invocation invocation) => super.noSuchMethod(invocation);
}

({ProviderContainer container, List<String> events}) _build(
  DeviceWipeRegistry registry,
) {
  final events = <String>[];
  final container = ProviderContainer(
    overrides: [
      chatCacheServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(_FakeCache(events)),
      authServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(_FakeAuth(events)),
      routingNotifierProvider.overrideWith((ref) => RoutingNotifier()),
      deviceWipeRegistryProvider.overrideWithValue(registry),
      signOutHooksProvider.overrideWithValue(
        SignOutHooks(beforeSignOut: () async => events.add('push released')),
      ),
    ],
  );
  addTearDown(container.dispose);
  return (container: container, events: events);
}

void main() {
  test(
    'the registry runs beside the existing wipes, before the sign-out',
    () async {
      final registry = DeviceWipeRegistry();
      late List<String> events;
      registry
        ..register(
          DeviceWipeEntry(
            id: 'probe.signout',
            moments: const {WipeMoment.signOut},
            run: () async => events.add('registry wiped'),
          ),
        )
        ..register(
          DeviceWipeEntry(
            id: 'probe.exit',
            moments: const {WipeMoment.quickExit},
            run: () async => events.add('quick exit only'),
          ),
        );
      final t = _build(registry);
      events = t.events;
      await t.container.read(authOperationsProvider).signOut();
      expect(
        events,
        containsAll(['cache wiped', 'push released', 'registry wiped']),
      );
      expect(events, isNot(contains('quick exit only')));
      expect(events.last, 'auth signed out');
    },
  );

  test('a hanging entry does not hold the sign-out past its bound', () async {
    final registry =
        DeviceWipeRegistry()..register(
          DeviceWipeEntry(
            id: 'probe.hang',
            moments: const {WipeMoment.signOut},
            bound: const Duration(milliseconds: 100),
            run: () => Completer<void>().future,
          ),
        );
    final t = _build(registry);
    final clock = Stopwatch()..start();
    await t.container.read(authOperationsProvider).signOut();
    expect(t.events.last, 'auth signed out');
    expect(clock.elapsed, lessThan(const Duration(seconds: 2)));
  });
}
```

```dart
// Spec 1 W3a (§5.6 R18, §5.8, D19): quick exit runs the registry's
// quick-exit entries after the cover is up, still leaves, and never clears
// a protective setting (the Safety PIN, and from W3b the Dating lock).
import 'package:attune/core/device/device_wipe_registry.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/safety/domain/services/quick_exit_service.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

void main() {
  testWidgets(
    'quick-exit entries run, sign-out ones do not; the app still leaves',
    (t) async {
      SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues({'safety_pin_expected': true});
      FlutterSecureStorage.setMockInitialValues({
        'safety_pin_hash': r'v1$a$b$c',
      });
      final events = <String>[];
      QuickExitService.exitOverride = () async => events.add('exit');
      final registry = DeviceWipeRegistry.instance;
      addTearDown(() {
        QuickExitService.exitOverride = null;
        registry.clear();
      });
      registry
        ..clear()
        ..register(
          DeviceWipeEntry(
            id: 'probe.exit',
            moments: const {WipeMoment.quickExit},
            run: () async => events.add('wiped on exit'),
          ),
        )
        ..register(
          DeviceWipeEntry(
            id: 'probe.signout',
            moments: const {WipeMoment.signOut},
            run: () async => events.add('sign-out only'),
          ),
        );
      final router = GoRouter(
        routes: [
          GoRoute(path: '/', builder: (_, __) => const Text('HOME')),
          GoRoute(
            path: '/neutralScreen',
            name: 'neutralScreen',
            builder: (_, __) => const Text('DECOY'),
          ),
        ],
      );
      addTearDown(router.dispose);
      await t.pumpWidget(MaterialApp.router(routerConfig: router));
      await t.runAsync(() => QuickExitService.executeWithRouter(router));
      await t.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.text('DECOY'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(events, ['wiped on exit', 'exit']);

      // The protective settings are where they were.
      final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
      expect(prefs.getBool('safety_pin_expected'), isTrue);
      expect(
        await const FlutterSecureStorage().read(key: 'safety_pin_hash'),
        r'v1$a$b$c',
      );
    },
  );
}
```

```dart
import 'package:attune/app/routing/app_router.dart' show RouteNames;

  Future<void> signOut() async =>

            moments: const {WipeMoment.signOut},
            run: () async => ran.add('wiped'),

    final router = GoRouter(

        GoRoute(path: '/', builder: (_, __) => const SizedBox()),
        GoRoute(path: RouteNames.intro, builder: (_, __) => const SizedBox()),
      ],

      overrides: [

        deviceWipeRegistryProvider.overrideWithValue(registry),
        routingNotifierProvider.overrideWith((ref) => RoutingNotifier()),
        appRouterProvider.overrideWithValue(router),
      ],
    );
    addTearDown(container.dispose);
    await safetySignOut(container.read(_refProvider));
    expect(ran, ['wiped']);
  });
}
```
