- [ ] **Step 3: Write the registry**

Create `lib/core/device/device_wipe_registry.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

/// When something is removed from this phone (Spec 1 §5.8, D19).
enum WipeMoment { signOut, quickExit }

/// One thing to remove from this phone at one or both moments.
@immutable
class DeviceWipeEntry {
  const DeviceWipeEntry({
    required this.id,
    required this.moments,
    required this.run,
    this.order = 0,
    this.bound = const Duration(seconds: 1),
    this.protective = false,
    this.keys = const {},
  });

  /// Unique, e.g. `spec1.home_state_cache`.
  final String id;

  /// Lower runs first; one order's entries run side by side.
  final int order;
  final Set<WipeMoment> moments;
  final Future<void> Function() run;

  /// At most [DeviceWipeRegistry.maxBound].
  final Duration bound;

  /// A protective setting (the Dating lock, the Safety PIN, the discreet
  /// icon): it may never be removed by quick exit, only by sign-out.
  final bool protective;

  /// The device keys (or key patterns, `<uid>` for an account id) this entry
  /// removes, so a test can prove no quick-exit entry touches a protective
  /// one.
  final Set<String> keys;
}

/// One ordered list of "things to remove from this phone", run on sign-out
/// and on quick exit. Spec 1 owns it and its two call sites
/// (quick_exit_service.dart, auth_provider.dart and safety_sign_out.dart's
/// fallback); features only register entries, through
/// lib/app/device_wipes.dart.
class DeviceWipeRegistry {
  DeviceWipeRegistry({this.totalBound = const Duration(seconds: 3)});

  static final instance = DeviceWipeRegistry();

  /// No entry may hold a moment longer than this.
  static const maxBound = Duration(seconds: 2);

  /// The whole run, every entry included.
  final Duration totalBound;

  final _entries = <String, DeviceWipeEntry>{};

  List<DeviceWipeEntry> get entries => List.unmodifiable(_entries.values);

  /// Adds [entry]. A duplicate id, no moment, a bound outside (0, 2 s], or a
  /// protective entry that would run on quick exit is refused: an assert in
  /// debug, ignored (false) in release.
  bool register(DeviceWipeEntry entry) {
    final problem = _problem(entry);
    if (problem != null) {
      assert(false, 'DeviceWipeRegistry: $problem');
      return false;
    }
    _entries[entry.id] = entry;
    return true;
  }

  String? _problem(DeviceWipeEntry entry) {
    if (_entries.containsKey(entry.id)) return 'duplicate id ${entry.id}';
    if (entry.moments.isEmpty) return '${entry.id} names no moment';
    if (entry.bound <= Duration.zero || entry.bound > maxBound) {
      return '${entry.id} bound ${entry.bound} is outside (0, $maxBound]';
    }
    if (entry.protective && entry.moments.contains(WipeMoment.quickExit)) {
      return '${entry.id} is protective and may not run on quick exit';
    }
    return null;
  }

  /// Runs every entry for [moment]: one order after another, each order's
  /// entries side by side, each in its own try and bound, so one failing
  /// or hanging entry never skips another. Bounded by [totalBound]; never
  /// throws.
  Future<void> run(WipeMoment moment) async {
    try {
      await _runAll(moment).timeout(totalBound);
    } catch (error) {
      debugPrint(
        '[device-wipe] ${moment.name} cut short: ${error.runtimeType}',
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> _runAll(WipeMoment moment) async {
    final due = [
      for (final entry in _entries.values)
        if (entry.moments.contains(moment)) entry,
    ];
    final orders = {for (final entry in due) entry.order}.toList()..sort();
    for (final order in orders) {
      await Future.wait([
        for (final entry in due)
          if (entry.order == order) _runOne(entry),
      ]);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _runOne(DeviceWipeEntry entry) async {
    try {
      await Future<void>.sync(entry.run).timeout(entry.bound);
    } catch (error) {
      debugPrint('[device-wipe] ${entry.id}: ${error.runtimeType}');
    }
  }

  /// Empties the list (tests only).
  @visibleForTesting
  void clear() => _entries.clear();
}

/// [DeviceWipeRegistry.instance], replaceable in tests (spec §5.3).
final deviceWipeRegistryProvider = Provider<DeviceWipeRegistry>(
  (ref) => DeviceWipeRegistry.instance,
);
```

Create `lib/app/device_wipes.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:attune/core/device/device_wipe_registry.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

/// Every feature's device wipes, registered once at boot (main.dart; spec
/// §5.8). Spec 1's entries are here; another feature adds one line calling
/// its own register function (Spec 2: `registerDatingDeviceWipes(registry)`).
void registerDeviceWipes([DeviceWipeRegistry? registry]) {
  final target = registry ?? DeviceWipeRegistry.instance;
  for (final entry in spec1DeviceWipes()) {
    target.register(entry);
  }
}

/// Removes every prefs key [matches] accepts (all accounts on the phone:
/// the next person to sign in here inherits none of them).
Future<void> removePrefsKeys(bool Function(String key) matches) async {
  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  for (final key in prefs.getKeys().where(matches).toList()) {
    await prefs.remove(key);
  }
}

/// `attune.home.<uid>.state` (HomeStateStore.keyFor).
bool isHomeStateKey(String key) =>
    key.startsWith('attune.home.') && key.endsWith('.state');

/// Spec 1's entries (spec §5.8 table). W3b adds the Dating lock and the Me
/// keys; W4b the partner name and Ask 2 progress.
List<DeviceWipeEntry> spec1DeviceWipes() => [
  DeviceWipeEntry(
    id: 'spec1.home_state_cache',
    moments: const {WipeMoment.signOut},
    keys: const {'attune.home.<uid>.state'},
    run: () => removePrefsKeys(isHomeStateKey),
  ),
];
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the ARB helper**

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Appends ARB entries to lib/i10n/app_en.arb without reformatting it.

Usage: python3 -I arb_append.py <fragment.txt>
The fragment is the exact text of the entries, two-space indented, no
trailing comma. Refuses a key the file already has (the file itself has two old duplicate
keys, editProfileScreenTitle and introGetStarted, which are left alone) or
repeats inside the fragment, and checks the result is valid JSON first.
"""
import json
import pathlib
import sys

arb = pathlib.Path("lib/i10n/app_en.arb")
fragment = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]).read_text().rstrip("\n")
text = arb.read_text().rstrip()
assert text.endswith("}"), "app_en.arb does not end with }"
body = text[:-1].rstrip()


def pairs(items):
    keys = [k for k, _ in items]
    dupes = {k for k in keys if keys.count(k) > 1}
    if dupes:
        raise SystemExit(f"duplicate keys: {sorted(dupes)}")
    return dict(items)


new_keys = set(json.loads("{" + fragment + "}", object_pairs_hook=pairs))
old_keys = set(json.loads(text))
clash = new_keys & old_keys
if clash:
    raise SystemExit(f"already in app_en.arb: {sorted(clash)}")
result = body + ",\n\n" + fragment + "\n}"
json.loads(result)
arb.write_text(result)
print(f"added {len([k for k in new_keys if not k.startswith('@')])} keys")
```

Save it as `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-26-spec1-w3/arb_append.py`. It appends a fragment to `lib/i10n/app_en.arb` without reformatting the file, and refuses a key the file already has or a key repeated in the fragment.

```dart
// Spec 1 W3: pumps HomeScreen with no Supabase: a scripted signed-in

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/app/theme/app_theme.dart';

import 'package:attune/core/services/user_timezone_reporter.dart';

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

import 'fake_home_state.dart';

User testUser(String id) => User(
  id: id,
  appMetadata: const {},
  userMetadata: const {},
  aud: 'authenticated',
  createdAt: '2026-10-01T00:00:00Z',
);

class FakeHomeAuth implements HomeAuth {
  FakeHomeAuth(this.currentUser);

  @override
  User? currentUser;

  final changes = StreamController<AuthState>.broadcast();

  void signIn(String id) {
    currentUser = testUser(id);
    changes.add(const AuthState(AuthChangeEvent.signedIn, null));
  }

  void signOut() {
    currentUser = null;
    changes.add(const AuthState(AuthChangeEvent.signedOut, null));
  }

  @override
  Stream<AuthState> get authStateChanges => changes.stream;
}

/// Tab 2's stand-in: counts how often it was created (a rebuilt shell
/// creates it again) and shows what it was given.
class TabTwoProbe extends StatefulWidget {
  const TabTwoProbe({super.key, required this.tab, required this.home});

  final AppTab tab;
  final HomeSnapshot home;

  static int created = 0;

  @override
  State<TabTwoProbe> createState() => _TabTwoProbeState();
}

class _TabTwoProbeState extends State<TabTwoProbe> {
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    TabTwoProbe.created++;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) =>
      Text('TAB2 ${widget.tab.wire} ${widget.home.link.wire}');
}

class HomeHarness {
  HomeHarness(
    this.router,
    this.container,
    this.auth,
    this.gateway,
    this.prefs,
    this.onboardingVisits,
  );

  final GoRouter router;
  final ProviderContainer container;
  final FakeHomeAuth auth;
  final FakeHomeStateGateway gateway;
  final SharedPreferences prefs;
  final List<String> onboardingVisits;

  String get location =>
      router.routerDelegate.currentConfiguration.uri.toString();

  StateController<bool> get pathsV2 => container.read(_pathsV2.notifier);

  /// Applies a server answer as get_my_home_state would give it.
  Future<void> answer(String userId, Map<String, dynamic> json) => container
      .read(homeStateProvider(userId).notifier)
      .apply(HomeState.fromJson(json));
}

final _pathsV2 = StateProvider<bool>((_) => true);

Future<HomeHarness> pumpHome(
  WidgetTester tester, {
  String? userId = 'u-home',
  bool pathsV2 = true,
  bool signInV2 = true,
  Map<String, Object> prefsValues = const {},
  Map<String, dynamic>? server,
  String initialLocation = '/home',
}) async {
  tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(390, 844);
  tester.view.devicePixelRatio = 1;
  addTearDown(tester.view.reset);
  TabTwoProbe.created = 0;
  SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues(prefsValues);
  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  final auth = FakeHomeAuth(userId == null ? null : testUser(userId));
  addTearDown(auth.changes.close);
  final gateway = FakeHomeStateGateway(state: server);
  final visits = <String>[];
  final router = GoRouter(
    initialLocation: initialLocation,
    routes: [
      GoRoute(
        path: '/home',
        builder:
            (_, state) => HomeScreen(
              tabRequest:
                  state.uri.queryParameters.containsKey('tab')
                      ? state.uri.query
                      : null,
            ),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/onboarding',
        builder: (_, __) {
          visits.add('onboarding');
          return const Scaffold(body: Text('ONBOARDING'));
        },
      ),
    ],
  );
  addTearDown(router.dispose);
  final container = ProviderContainer(
    overrides: [
      sharedPreferencesProvider.overrideWithValue(prefs),
      homeAuthProvider.overrideWithValue(auth),
      homeStateServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(HomeStateService(gateway)),
      _pathsV2.overrideWith((_) => pathsV2),
      flowPathsV2Provider.overrideWith((ref) => ref.watch(_pathsV2)),
      signInFlowV2Provider.overrideWithValue(signInV2),
      homeOpinionsTabProvider.overrideWithValue(const Text('OPINIONS')),
      homeTabTwoBuilderProvider.overrideWithValue(
        (tab, home, _) => TabTwoProbe(tab: tab, home: home),
      ),
      userTimezoneReporterProvider.overrideWithValue(
        UserTimezoneReporter(
          deviceTimezone: () async => '',
          send: (_) async {},
        ),
      ),
    ],
  );
  addTearDown(container.dispose);
  await tester.pumpWidget(
    UncontrolledProviderScope(
      container: container,
      child: ScreenUtilConfig.builder(
        builder:
            (_) => MaterialApp.router(
              theme: AppTheme.lightTheme,
              localizationsDelegates: AppLocalizations.localizationsDelegates,
              supportedLocales: AppLocalizations.supportedLocales,
              routerConfig: router,
            ),
      ),
    ),
  );
  await tester.pump();
  await tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 50));
  return HomeHarness(router, container, auth, gateway, prefs, visits);
}

/// A snapshot as the server would answer it.
HomeSnapshot snapshotOf(Map<String, dynamic> json) =>
    HomeSnapshot.fromServer(HomeState.fromJson(json));
```

Create `test/home/home_screen_v2_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W3a (§3.11, §5.6 R1, review N-2): the home shell under
// flow_paths_v2. The latch block is not edited: v2 changes only what
// `isComplete` means. Tab 2 is Chat or Me by the home state's layout and
// opens first; a layout change rebuilds the keyed shell, a link change or
// Dating <-> Just for me does not; /home?tab= opens Tab 2; a guest's Tab 2
// is "Sign in" under flow_signin_v2.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/home_state_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/home/home_location.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

import '../helpers/fake_home_state.dart';
import '../helpers/home_harness.dart';

String _cache({
  String tab = 'me',
  String link = 'none',
  List<String> needs = const [],
}) =>
    '{"tab":"$tab","link":"$link","needs":[${needs.map((n) => '"$n"').join(',')}],'
    '"hold":false,"at":"2026-10-26T08:00:00.000Z"}';

bool _onTab2(WidgetTester tester) =>
    !tester
        .widget<Offstage>(
          find
              .ancestor(
                of: find.textContaining('TAB2', skipOffstage: false),
                matching: find.byType(Offstage),
              )
              .first,
        )
        .offstage;

void main() {
  testWidgets(
    'a guest sees today\'s "Chat" with sign-in v2 off, "Sign in" with it on',
    (tester) async {
      await pumpHome(tester, userId: null, signInV2: false);
      expect(find.text('Chat'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('Sign in'), findsNothing);

      await pumpHome(tester, userId: null, signInV2: true);
      expect(find.text('Sign in'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(find.text('Chat'), findsNothing);
    },
  );

  testWidgets("a guest's /home?tab=chat opens Tab 2 (back from Join Attune)", (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final h = await pumpHome(
      tester,
      userId: null,
      initialLocation: homeLocation(tab: AppTab.chat),
    );
    expect(h.location, startsWith('/home?tab=chat'));
    final tab2 = tester.widget<Offstage>(
      find
          .ancestor(
            of: find.text('Join Attune'),
            matching: find.byType(Offstage),
          )
          .first,
    );
    expect(tab2.offstage, isFalse);
  });

  testWidgets('Relationship: Tab 2 is Chat and opens first', (tester) async {
    await pumpHome(
      tester,
      prefsValues: {
        HomeStateStore.keyFor('u-home'): _cache(tab: 'chat', link: 'pending'),
      },
      server: homeJson(path: 'relationship', link: 'pending'),
    );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Chat'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('TAB2 chat pending'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(_onTab2(tester), isTrue);
  });

  testWidgets('Just for me: Tab 2 is Me', (tester) async {
    await pumpHome(
      tester,
      prefsValues: {HomeStateStore.keyFor('u-home'): _cache()},
      server: homeJson(path: 'just_me'),
    );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Me'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Chat'), findsNothing);
    expect(find.text('TAB2 me none'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets(
    'the latch fires once for needs [birth_date], not again after the date (N-2)',
    (tester) async {
      final h = await pumpHome(
        tester,
        prefsValues: {
          HomeStateStore.keyFor('u-home'): _cache(needs: ['birth_date']),
        },
        server: homeJson(needs: ['birth_date']),
      );
      // Rebuilds while still incomplete never add a redirect.
      await h.answer('u-home', homeJson(needs: ['birth_date']));
      await h.answer('u-home', homeJson(needs: ['birth_date']));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(h.onboardingVisits, ['onboarding']);
      expect(find.text('ONBOARDING'), findsOneWidget);

      // The interstitial took the date; the gate goes home.
      h.gateway.state = homeJson();
      await h.answer('u-home', homeJson());
      h.router.go('/home');
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.text('TAB2 me none'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(h.onboardingVisits, ['onboarding']);
    },
  );

  testWidgets('an unknown need keeps the home closed (W2 P-15, review M-5)', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final h = await pumpHome(
      tester,
      prefsValues: {
        HomeStateStore.keyFor('u-home'): _cache(needs: ['from_a_later_wave']),
      },
      server: homeJson(needs: ['from_a_later_wave']),
    );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(h.onboardingVisits, ['onboarding']);
  });

  testWidgets('a layout change rebuilds the shell; a link change does not', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final h = await pumpHome(
      tester,
      prefsValues: {
        HomeStateStore.keyFor('u-home'): _cache(tab: 'chat', link: 'pending'),
      },
      server: homeJson(path: 'relationship', link: 'pending'),
    );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    final created = TabTwoProbe.created;
    await tester.tap(find.text('Opinions'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();

    // pending -> linked: Tab 2's child changes in place; Opinions stays up.
    await h.answer('u-home', homeJson(path: 'relationship', link: 'linked'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('TAB2 chat linked', skipOffstage: false), findsOneWidget);
    expect(TabTwoProbe.created, created);
    expect(_onTab2(tester), isFalse);

    // Relationship -> Just for me: a new layout, so a new shell on Tab 2.
    await h.answer('u-home', homeJson(path: 'just_me'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('TAB2 me none'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(TabTwoProbe.created, created + 1);
    expect(_onTab2(tester), isTrue);
  });

  testWidgets('Dating <-> Just for me keeps the shell (both Me)', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final h = await pumpHome(
      tester,
      prefsValues: {HomeStateStore.keyFor('u-home'): _cache()},
      server: homeJson(path: 'just_me'),
    );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    final created = TabTwoProbe.created;
    await h.answer('u-home', homeJson(path: 'dating', datingEnabled: true));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await h.answer('u-home', homeJson(path: 'just_me'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(TabTwoProbe.created, created);
  });

  testWidgets('/home?tab=me while the shell is up opens Tab 2, every time', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final h = await pumpHome(
      tester,
      prefsValues: {HomeStateStore.keyFor('u-home'): _cache()},
      server: homeJson(path: 'just_me'),
    );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    for (var i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
      await tester.tap(find.text('Opinions'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(_onTab2(tester), isFalse);
      h.router.go(homeLocation(tab: AppTab.me));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(_onTab2(tester), isTrue, reason: 'request ${i + 1}');
    }
  });

  testWidgets('flow_paths_v2 off: the v2 home state is not read', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final h = await pumpHome(
      tester,
      pathsV2: false,
      prefsValues: {
        HomeStateStore.keyFor('u-home'): _cache(),
        'attune.onboarding.user.u-home.completed': true,
        'attune.onboarding.user.u-home.mode': 'personal',
      },
    );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(h.gateway.count('get_my_home_state'), 0);
    expect(find.text('Chat'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Me'), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('signing out forgets the account\'s live answer (D20)', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final h = await pumpHome(
      tester,
      prefsValues: {HomeStateStore.keyFor('u-home'): _cache()},
      server: homeJson(path: 'dating', datingEnabled: true),
    );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(h.gateway.count('get_my_home_state'), greaterThanOrEqualTo(1));
    h.auth.signOut();
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    // A fresh read of the provider has no live answer: path unknown.
    final probe = h.container.read(homeStateProvider('u-home'));
    expect(probe.path, isNull);
    expect(find.text('Sign in'), findsOneWidget, reason: 'the guest shell');
  });
}
```

```dart
/// The chat screen's Games entry points (§1.5: chat_screen.dart:69-78).
const _chatGamesImports = [
  "import 'package:attune/features/games/paint_ball/models/paint_ball_models.dart';",
  "import 'package:attune/features/games/invites/presentation/game_composer_bar.dart';",
  "import 'package:attune/features/games/invites/state/game_invite_provider.dart';",
  "import 'package:attune/features/games/presentation/providers/game_card_provider.dart';",
  "import 'package:attune/features/games/presentation/providers/game_partner_name_provider.dart';",
  "import 'package:attune/features/games/presentation/widgets/chat_games_sheet.dart';",
  "import 'package:attune/features/games/presentation/widgets/game_icon.dart';",
  "import 'package:attune/features/games/presentation/widgets/game_palette.dart';",
];

const _authExportsSha256 =
    'c615dbed9246f7a4582b9e8c072a8258378f769eb62de54b6960f31c356c63f2';

/// Every `GoRoute(...)` call in [src], whole: parentheses matched, string
/// literals and `//` comments skipped.
List<String> _goRouteBlocks(String src) {
  final blocks = <String>[];
  var from = 0;
  while (true) {
    final start = src.indexOf('GoRoute(', from);
    if (start < 0) return blocks;
    var i = start + 'GoRoute'.length;
    var depth = 0;
    while (i < src.length) {
      final c = src[i];
      if (c == '/' && i + 1 < src.length && src[i + 1] == '/') {
        i = src.indexOf('\n', i);
        if (i < 0) i = src.length;
        continue;
      }
      if (c == "'" || c == '"') {
        final quote = c;
        i++;
        while (i < src.length && src[i] != quote) {
          if (src[i] == r'\') i++;
          i++;
        }
        i++;
        continue;
      }
      if (c == '(') depth++;
      if (c == ')') {
        depth--;
        if (depth == 0) break;
      }
      i++;
    }
    blocks.add(src.substring(start, i + 1));
    from = i + 1;
  }
}

Map<String, String> _namedPaths(List<RouteBase> routes) => {
  for (final route in routes) ...{
    if (route is GoRoute && route.name != null) route.name!: route.path,
    ..._namedPaths(route.routes),
  },
};

void main() {
  test('every Games route keeps its name and path', () {
    final router = createAppRouter(RoutingNotifier());
    addTearDown(router.dispose);
    final live = _namedPaths(router.configuration.routes);
    for (final entry in _gamesRoutes.entries) {
      expect(live[entry.key], entry.value, reason: entry.key);
    }
  });

  test('the source of every Games route is unchanged', () {
    final src = File('lib/app/routing/app_router.dart').readAsStringSync();
    final seen = <String, String>{};
    for (final block in _goRouteBlocks(src)) {
      final name = RegExp(r"name:\s*'([^']+)'").firstMatch(block)?.group(1);
      if (name != null && _gamesRouteSource.containsKey(name)) {
        seen[name] = sha256.convert(utf8.encode(block)).toString();
      }
    }
    expect(seen.keys.toSet(), _gamesRouteSource.keys.toSet());
    for (final entry in _gamesRouteSource.entries) {
      expect(seen[entry.key], entry.value, reason: '${entry.key} changed');
    }
  });

  test("the chat's Games entry points are still there", () {
    final chat =
        File(
          'lib/features/chat/presentation/screens/chat_screen.dart',
        ).readAsStringSync();
    for (final line in _chatGamesImports) {
      expect(chat, contains(line));
    }
    expect(chat, contains('ChatGamesSheet'));
  });

  test('the auth_exports.dart barrel is byte-identical', () {
    final bytes =
        File('lib/features/auth/utility/auth_exports.dart').readAsBytesSync();
    expect(sha256.convert(bytes).toString(), _authExportsSha256);
  });
}
```

Create `lib/home/home_location.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:attune/app/routing/app_router.dart' show RouteNames;
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';

int _tabRequests = 0;

/// `/home`, or `/home?tab=chat|me` to open Tab 2 (Spec 1 §3.11): the
/// journal reminder tap, Spec 2's `me_update`, the guest's way back from
/// sign-up. Each request carries its own `n`, so asking again while the
/// shell is up opens Tab 2 again.
String homeLocation({AppTab? tab}) =>
    tab == null
        ? RouteNames.home
        : '${RouteNames.home}?tab=${tab.wire}&n=${++_tabRequests}';
```

Create `lib/home/home_seams.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:attune/features/auth/data/passwordless_auth_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/opinions/presentation/screen/opinions_tab.dart';
import 'package:attune/home/widgets/home_tab_two.dart';
import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

/// The signed-in account as HomeScreen reads it (Spec 1 W3a): the same two
/// members PasswordlessAuthService has, so tests can drive the shell
/// without Supabase.
abstract class HomeAuth {
  User? get currentUser;
  Stream<AuthState> get authStateChanges;
}

class PasswordlessHomeAuth implements HomeAuth {
  PasswordlessHomeAuth([PasswordlessAuthService? service])
    : _service = service ?? PasswordlessAuthService();

  final PasswordlessAuthService _service;

  @override
  User? get currentUser => _service.currentUser;

  @override
  Stream<AuthState> get authStateChanges => _service.authStateChanges;
}

final homeAuthProvider = Provider<HomeAuth>((ref) => PasswordlessHomeAuth());

/// Tab 1, Opinions (unchanged, Q9), replaceable in the shell's tests.
final homeOpinionsTabProvider = Provider<Widget>((ref) => const OpinionsTab());

typedef HomeTabTwoBuilder =
    Widget Function(AppTab tab, HomeSnapshot home, VoidCallback onInviteSent);

/// Tab 2 under flow_paths_v2, replaceable in the shell's tests.
final homeTabTwoBuilderProvider = Provider<HomeTabTwoBuilder>(
  (ref) =>
      (tab, home, onInviteSent) =>
          HomeTabTwo(tab: tab, home: home, onInviteSent: onInviteSent),
);
```

Create `lib/home/widgets/home_tab_two.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:attune/features/chat/presentation/widgets/authenticated_chat_workspace.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart';
import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';

/// Tab 2 under flow_paths_v2 (Spec 1 §3.11). A linked couple gets the chat
/// (ChatHomeScreen, unchanged). Until W3b builds RelationshipSpaceScreen and
/// MeHomeScreen, every other state shows today's locked Chat screen.
class HomeTabTwo extends StatelessWidget {
  const HomeTabTwo({
    super.key,
    required this.tab,
    required this.home,
    required this.onInviteSent,
  });

  final AppTab tab;
  final HomeSnapshot home;
  final VoidCallback onInviteSent;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final linked = tab == AppTab.chat && home.link == LinkState.linked;
    return AuthenticatedChatWorkspace(
      isCouples: linked,
      isPendingCouples: tab == AppTab.chat && home.link == LinkState.pending,
      onInviteSent: onInviteSent,
    );
  }
}
```

with:

```dart
  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant HomeScreen oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    // Spec 1 W3a: a new /home?tab= while the shell is up opens Tab 2.
    if (widget.tabRequest != null && widget.tabRequest != oldWidget.tabRequest) {
      _tabRequests++;
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);
    relationshipModeResyncSignal.removeListener(_syncRelationshipMode);
    _authSubscription?.cancel();
    _homeSubscription?.close();
    _pathsSubscription?.close();
    super.dispose();
  }

  /// Spec 1 W3a (§5.3): follows the signed-in account's home state; the
  /// account that just signed out forgets its live answer (D20: in memory
  /// only).
  void _bindHomeState(String? previousUserId) {
    _homeSubscription?.close();
    _homeSubscription = null;
    if (previousUserId != null && previousUserId.isNotEmpty) {
      ref.invalidate(homeStateProvider(previousUserId));
    }
    final userId = _scopeUserId;
    if (userId == null || userId.isEmpty) return;
    _homeSubscription = ref.listenManual<HomeSnapshot>(
      homeStateProvider(userId),
      (_, next) {
        if (!mounted || !_pathsV2 || _scopeUserId != userId) return;
        setState(() => _gateData = _v2GateData(_gateData.store, next));
      },
    );
  }

  /// get_my_home_state(), shared with every other reader (deduplicated).
  Future<void> _refreshHomeState() async {
    final userId = _scopeUserId;
    if (userId == null || userId.isEmpty) return;
    await ref.read(homeStateProvider(userId).notifier).refresh();
  }
```

```dart
    final isOnboarded = isAuthenticated && _gateData.isComplete;
    final mode = store.mode;
    final isActiveCouples = mode == OnboardingMode.couples;
    final isRelationshipTrack = mode?.isRelationshipTrack ?? false;

    // Spec 1 W3a (§3.11): under flow_paths_v2, Tab 2 is Chat or Me by the
    // home state's layout (the legacy mode stands in until it is known).
    final homeState = _gateData.home;
    final AppTab? layout =
        homeState == null || !isOnboarded
            ? null
            : homeState.tab ??
                (isRelationshipTrack ? AppTab.chat : AppTab.me);
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;

    // Default tab: Opinions (0) for single/personal, Chat (1) for active
    // couples. Spec 1 W3a: Tab 2 for everyone signed in under
    // flow_paths_v2, and whenever /home?tab= asked for it.
    final initialTabIndex =
        layout != null ||
                widget.tabRequest != null ||
                (isOnboarded && isActiveCouples)
            ? 1
            : 0;

    final tabs = [
      HomeTab(
        id: 'opinions',
        label: 'Opinions',
        icon: Icons.forum_outlined,
        activeIcon: Icons.forum,
        screen: ref.watch(homeOpinionsTabProvider),
      ),
      HomeTab(
        id: layout == AppTab.me ? 'me' : 'chat',
        // Spec 1 §3.2 G2: a guest's Tab 2 is "Sign in" under flow_signin_v2.
        label:
            layout == AppTab.me
                ? loc.flow_tabMe
                : !isOnboarded && ref.watch(signInFlowV2Provider)
                ? loc.flow_tabSignIn
                : 'Chat',
        icon:
            layout == AppTab.me
                ? Icons.person_outline
                : Icons.chat_bubble_outline,
        activeIcon: layout == AppTab.me ? Icons.person : Icons.chat_bubble,
        screen:
            isOnboarded
                ? layout != null
                    ? ref.watch(homeTabTwoBuilderProvider)(
                      layout,
                      homeState!,
                      _onInviteSent,
                    )
                    : AuthenticatedChatWorkspace(
                      isCouples: isActiveCouples,
                      isPendingCouples: isRelationshipTrack && !isActiveCouples,
                      onInviteSent: _onInviteSent,
                    )
                : const LoginProfile(),
      ),
    ];

    final home = KeyedSubtree(
      // Spec 1 W3a (§3.11): HomeWidget reads initialTabIndex only once, so a
      // new layout (Chat <-> Me) or a /home?tab= request rebuilds the shell;
      // a link change, or Dating <-> Just for me (both Me), keeps it.
      key: ValueKey('home-${layout?.wire ?? 'legacy'}-$_tabRequests'),
      child: HomeWidgetResponsive.adaptive(
        context: context,
        tabs: tabs,
        initialTabIndex: initialTabIndex,
      ),
    );
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write A6**

Create `lib/features/onboarding/presentation/widgets/path_step.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/app/theme/design_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/ui/motion/motion_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/ui/motion/reduce_motion.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/domain/country_names.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_buttons.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_colors.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_frame.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_text_field.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/submit_once.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/semantics.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';

/// A6 · What brings you to Attune? (Spec 1 §3.7): three equal cards,
/// nothing preselected, a radio group; Continue once one is chosen.
class PathStep extends StatefulWidget {
  const PathStep({
    super.key,
    required this.onContinue,
    this.ownCountry,
    this.openCountries = const [],
    this.onAlreadyInvited,
    this.errorText,
  });

  /// The chosen path. The step stays busy until it has finished.
  final FutureOr<void> Function(AppPath path) onContinue;

  /// ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 of the person's own number, when known.
  final String? ownCountry;

  /// config.dating_open_countries (the D15 seam); empty when never read.
  final List<String> openCountries;

  /// "My partner already invited me" (RI-2).
  final VoidCallback? onAlreadyInvited;

  /// Shown under the cards and read out (e.g. Dating chosen offline).
  final String? errorText;

  @override
  State<PathStep> createState() => _PathStepState();
}

class _PathStepState extends State<PathStep> with SubmitOnce {
  AppPath? _selected;

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant PathStep oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    final error = widget.errorText;
    if (error != null && error != oldWidget.errorText) {
      SemanticsService.announce(error, Directionality.of(context));
    }
  }

  void _select(AppPath path) {
    if (_selected == path) return;
    HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
    setState(() => _selected = path);
  }

  void _continue() {
    final path = _selected;
    if (path == null || submitting) return;
    submitOnce(() => widget.onContinue(path));
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final own = widget.ownCountry;
    final notOpenHere =
        own != null &&
        widget.openCountries.isNotEmpty &&
        !widget.openCountries.contains(own);
    final cards = [
      (
        AppPath.relationship,
        loc.flow_pathRelationshipTitle,
        loc.flow_pathRelationshipLine,
        null,
      ),
      (
        AppPath.dating,
        loc.flow_pathDatingTitle,
        loc.flow_pathDatingLine,
        notOpenHere
            ? loc.flow_pathNotOpen(
              countryNameFor(
                own,
                special: {'GB': loc.flow_countryUk, 'US': loc.flow_countryUs},
              ),
            )
            : null,
      ),
      (
        AppPath.justMe,
        loc.flow_pathJustMeTitle,
        loc.flow_pathJustMeLineFull,
        null,
      ),
    ];
    final error = widget.errorText;

    return FlowFrame(
      title: loc.flow_pathTitle,
      body: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Semantics(
            container: true,
            label: loc.flow_pathGroupSemantics,
            explicitChildNodes: true,
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                for (final (path, title, line, note) in cards) ...[
                  PathChoiceCard(
                    title: title,
                    line: line,
                    note: note,
                    selected: _selected == path,
                    onTap: () => _select(path),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: Spacing.smMd),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
          if (error != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
            FlowErrorLine(
              text: error,
              style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
              ),
            ),
          ],
          const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
          Text(
            loc.flow_pathFooter,
            style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
              color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
            ),
          ),
          if (widget.onAlreadyInvited != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
            FlowTextLink(
              label: loc.flow_pathAlreadyInvited,
              alignment: AlignmentDirectional.centerStart,
              onPressed: widget.onAlreadyInvited!,
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
      actions: [
        FlowPrimaryButton(
          label: loc.flow_continue,
          busy: submitting,
          onPressed: _selected == null ? null : _continue,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// One path as a radio card (A6, and Settings -> How you use Attune).
class PathChoiceCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const PathChoiceCard({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.line,
    required this.selected,
    required this.onTap,
    this.note,
  });

  final String title;
  final String line;
  final String? note;
  final bool selected;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final scheme = theme.colorScheme;
    final accent = flowAccent(context);
    final duration =
        reduceMotionOf(context) ? Duration.zero : kSelectionDuration;
    return Semantics(
      container: true,
      button: true,
      inMutuallyExclusiveGroup: true,
      checked: selected,
      label: [title, line, if (note != null) note!].join('. '),
      child: ExcludeSemantics(
        child: Material(
          type: MaterialType.transparency,
          child: InkWell(
            onTap: onTap,
            borderRadius: BorderRadiusTokens.lgAll,
            child: AnimatedContainer(
              duration: duration,
              constraints: const BoxConstraints(minHeight: 48),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: selected ? accent.withValues(alpha: 0.08) : null,
                borderRadius: BorderRadiusTokens.lgAll,
                border: Border.all(
                  color: selected ? accent : scheme.outlineVariant,
                  width: selected ? 2 : 1,
                ),
              ),
              child: Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          title,
                          style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                            color: scheme.onSurface,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: Spacing.xs),
                        Text(
                          line,
                          style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                            color: scheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                          ),
                        ),
                        if (note != null) ...[
                          const SizedBox(height: Spacing.xs),
                          Text(
                            note!,
                            style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                              color: scheme.onSurface,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: Spacing.sm),
                  Icon(
                    selected
                        ? Icons.radio_button_checked
                        : Icons.radio_button_unchecked,
                    color: selected ? accent : scheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the path chooser (every `set_my_path` refusal mapped to its confirmation)**

Create `lib/features/onboarding/presentation/path_chooser.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:attune/app/theme/design_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/providers/shared_prefs_provider.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/ui/text/allow_large_text.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_buttons.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/home_state_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/onboarding_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

/// What [choosePath] did.
enum PathChoiceOutcome {
  /// The server took it (or, with queueOffline, it was queued).
  done,

  /// The person said no to a confirmation; nothing changed.
  cancelled,

  /// No connection; nothing changed.
  offline,

  /// Dating chosen with no connection: never queued (D20).
  datingOffline,

  /// A shared space is open or paused (`path:relationship_open`); the
  /// caller shows how to end or pause it (§3.12).
  relationshipOpen,

  /// Anything else; nothing changed.
  failed,
}

/// The one way any screen changes the path (A6, J4, the Dating slot,
/// Settings -> How you use Attune; Spec 1 §3.7, §3.12): set_my_path, and on
/// its refusals the confirmation it asks for, then the call again,
/// confirmed. The answer is applied to the home state, so the shell
/// follows. [queueOffline] keeps a Relationship or Just for me choice for
/// the replay (A6 only, §3.14); Dating is never queued (D20).
Future<PathChoiceOutcome> choosePath(
  BuildContext context,
  WidgetRef ref, {
  required String userId,
  required AppPath path,
  bool confirmed = false,
  bool queueOffline = false,
  String? partnerName,
}) async {
  final service = ref.read(homeStateServiceProvider);
  final notifier = ref.read(homeStateProvider(userId).notifier);
  var isConfirmed = confirmed;
  for (var attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
    try {
      final state = await service.setPath(path, confirmed: isConfirmed);
      await notifier.apply(state);
      return PathChoiceOutcome.done;
    } on PathRefused catch (refused) {
      if (!context.mounted) return PathChoiceOutcome.cancelled;
      switch (refused.reason) {
        case PathRefusal.confirmWithdraw:
          if (!await showCancelInviteSheet(context, partnerName: partnerName)) {
            return PathChoiceOutcome.cancelled;
          }
        case PathRefusal.confirmPauseDating:
          if (!await showPauseDatingSheet(context)) {
            return PathChoiceOutcome.cancelled;
          }
        case PathRefusal.relationshipOpen:
          return PathChoiceOutcome.relationshipOpen;
        case PathRefusal.needsSignUp:
        case PathRefusal.invalid:
          return PathChoiceOutcome.failed;
      }
      isConfirmed = true;
    } on HomeOffline {
      if (path == AppPath.dating) return PathChoiceOutcome.datingOffline;
      if (!queueOffline) return PathChoiceOutcome.offline;
      await OnboardingStore(
        ref.read(sharedPreferencesProvider),
        scope: '${OnboardingStore.userScopePrefix}.$userId',
      ).savePendingPath(path);
      notifier.reloadLocal();
      return PathChoiceOutcome.done;
    }
  }
  return PathChoiceOutcome.failed;
}

/// §3.12 "Cancel your invite?" (Relationship, pending -> another path).
Future<bool> showCancelInviteSheet(
  BuildContext context, {
  String? partnerName,
}) {
  final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
  final name = partnerName?.trim();
  return showFlowConfirm(
    context,
    title: loc.flow_cancelInviteTitle,
    body:
        name == null || name.isEmpty
            ? loc.flow_cancelInviteBody
            : loc.flow_cancelInviteBodyNamed(name),
    confirm: loc.flow_cancelInviteConfirm,
    cancel: loc.flow_cancelInviteKeep,
  );
}

/// §3.12 "Inviting a partner pauses Dating" (Dating -> Relationship).
Future<bool> showPauseDatingSheet(BuildContext context) {
  final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
  return showFlowConfirm(
    context,
    title: loc.flow_pauseDatingTitle,
    body: loc.flow_pauseDatingBody,
    confirm: loc.flow_continue,
    cancel: loc.flow_cancel,
  );
}

/// A two-button confirmation sheet in the flow's style (§2.3: large text,
/// 48 dp targets, a heading). True only for [confirm].
Future<bool> showFlowConfirm(
  BuildContext context, {
  required String title,
  required String body,
  required String confirm,
  required String cancel,
}) async {
  final result = await showModalBottomSheet<bool>(
    context: context,
    isScrollControlled: true,
    showDragHandle: true,
    builder:
        (sheet) => FlowConfirmSheet(
          title: title,
          body: body,
          confirm: confirm,
          cancel: cancel,
        ),
  );
  return result ?? false;
}

class FlowConfirmSheet extends StatelessWidget {
  const FlowConfirmSheet({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.body,
    required this.confirm,
    required this.cancel,
  });

  final String title;
  final String body;
  final String confirm;
  final String cancel;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => FlowConfirmSheetBody(
    title: title,
    body: body,
    actions: [
      (confirm, () => Navigator.of(context).pop(true)),
      (cancel, () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false)),
    ],
  );
}

/// A sheet with a heading, a body and its buttons: the first is the
/// primary, the rest secondary (§2.3: large text, 48 dp targets).
class FlowConfirmSheetBody extends StatelessWidget {
  const FlowConfirmSheetBody({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.body,
    required this.actions,
  });

  final String title;
  final String body;
  final List<(String, VoidCallback)> actions;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return AllowLargeText(
      child: SafeArea(
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
            Spacing.lg,
            0,
            Spacing.lg,
            Spacing.md,
          ),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              Semantics(
                header: true,
                child: Text(
                  title,
                  style: theme.textTheme.titleLarge?.copyWith(
                    color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
              Text(
                body,
                style: theme.textTheme.bodyLarge?.copyWith(
                  color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: Spacing.lg),
              for (var i = 0; i < actions.length; i++) ...[
                if (i > 0) const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
                i == 0
                    ? FlowPrimaryButton(
                      label: actions[i].$1,
                      onPressed: actions[i].$2,
                    )
                    : FlowSecondaryButton(
                      label: actions[i].$1,
                      onPressed: actions[i].$2,
                    ),
              ],
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

Create `test/helpers/fake_invites.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W3: today's invite service, scripted.
import 'package:attune/features/relationships/data/relationship_invite_service.dart';

class FakeInvites extends RelationshipInviteService {
  FakeInvites({this.acceptError, this.createError});

  RelationshipInviteException? acceptError;
  RelationshipInviteException? createError;
  final accepted = <String>[];
  int creates = 0;
  int withdrawals = 0;
  Duration? age;

  @override
  bool get isConfigured => true;

  @override
  Future<RelationshipInvite> createInvite({bool forceNew = false}) async {
    creates++;
    if (createError != null) throw createError!;
    return RelationshipInvite(
      relationshipId: 'r-new',
      code: 'K7P2QM',
      expiresAt: DateTime.utc(2026, 11, 2),
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<InviteAcceptance> acceptInvite(String inviteCode) async {
    accepted.add(inviteCode);
    if (acceptError != null) throw acceptError!;
    return const InviteAcceptance(
      relationshipId: 'r-joined',
      status: 'active',
      idempotent: false,
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<Duration?> inviteAge() async => age;

  @override
  Future<void> withdrawPendingInvites() async => withdrawals++;
}
```

Create `test/features/onboarding/w3/onboarding_gate_v2_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W3a (§3.7, §3.14, §3.15; W2 final review M-3, M-5; W2 P-15): the
// v2 gate shows only what the account lacks, in order, then A6 and R1;
// a hold opens H1; an unknown need asks for an update; no account goes back
// to sign-up; a queued A5 or A6 counts as done; offline A6 queues
// Relationship and Just for me, never Dating.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/app/routing/app_router.dart';
import 'package:attune/app/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/flags/public_app_config.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/providers/shared_prefs_provider.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/utils/screen_util_config.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/home_state_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/onboarding_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/onboarding_sync_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/screens/invite_partner_screen.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/screens/onboarding_gate_v2.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/widgets/catch_up_steps.dart';
import 'package:attune/home/home_seams.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

import '../../../helpers/fake_home_state.dart';
import '../../../helpers/fake_invites.dart';
import '../../../helpers/flow_harness.dart';
import '../../../helpers/home_harness.dart';

const _uid = 'u-gate';

class _Gate {
  _Gate(this.router, this.gateway, this.prefs, this.invites, this.signOuts);
  final GoRouter router;
  final FakeHomeStateGateway gateway;
  final SharedPreferences prefs;
  final FakeInvites invites;
  final List<String> signOuts;

  String get location =>
      router.routerDelegate.currentConfiguration.uri.toString();

  OnboardingStore get store => OnboardingStore(prefs, scope: 'user.$_uid');
}

Future<_Gate> _pumpGate(
  WidgetTester tester, {
  Map<String, dynamic>? server,
  Map<String, Object> prefsValues = const {},
  bool signedIn = true,
  String? phone = '+233244123456',
  FakeInvites? invites,
  void Function(FakeHomeStateGateway)? setUp,
}) async {
  tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(390, 844);
  tester.view.devicePixelRatio = 1;
  addTearDown(tester.view.reset);
  SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues(prefsValues);
  FlutterSecureStorage.setMockInitialValues({});
  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  final gateway = FakeHomeStateGateway(state: server);
  setUp?.call(gateway);
  final service = HomeStateService(gateway);
  final fakeInvites = invites ?? FakeInvites();
  final signOuts = <String>[];
  final user = signedIn ? testUser(_uid) : null;
  final auth = FakeHomeAuth(
    user == null
        ? null
        : User(
          id: user.id,
          appMetadata: const {},
          userMetadata: const {},
          aud: 'authenticated',
          createdAt: '2026-10-01T00:00:00Z',
          phone: phone,
        ),
  );
  addTearDown(auth.changes.close);
  final router = GoRouter(
    navigatorKey: appNavigatorKey,
    initialLocation: '/onboarding',
    routes: [
      GoRoute(
        path: '/onboarding',
        builder: (_, __) => const OnboardingGateV2(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/home',
        builder: (_, __) => const Scaffold(body: Text('HOME')),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/login',
        builder: (_, __) => const Scaffold(body: Text('LOGIN')),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/signup',
        builder: (_, s) => Scaffold(body: Text('SIGNUP ${s.uri.query}')),
      ),
    ],
  );
  addTearDown(router.dispose);
  await tester.pumpWidget(
    ProviderScope(
      overrides: [
        sharedPreferencesProvider.overrideWithValue(prefs),
        homeAuthProvider.overrideWithValue(auth),
        homeStateServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(service),
        onboardingSyncServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(
          OnboardingSyncService(homeStateService: service),
        ),
        invitePartnerServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(fakeInvites),
        catchUpSignOutProvider.overrideWithValue(
          () async => signOuts.add('out'),
        ),
        publicAppConfigGatewayProvider.overrideWithValue(
          FakePublicConfigGateway(openCountries: const ['GB', 'GH']),
        ),
      ],
      child: ScreenUtilConfig.builder(
        builder:
            (_) => MaterialApp.router(
              theme: AppTheme.lightTheme,
              localizationsDelegates: AppLocalizations.localizationsDelegates,
              supportedLocales: AppLocalizations.supportedLocales,
              routerConfig: router,
            ),
      ),
    ),
  );
  await _settle(tester);
  return _Gate(router, gateway, prefs, fakeInvites, signOuts);
}

/// Lets the gate's calls and waitForRouterSettled's polls run. No
/// pumpAndSettle: the loading step's spinner never settles.
Future<void> _settle(WidgetTester tester) async {
  for (var i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
    await tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 150));
  }
}

void main() {
  testWidgets(
    'needs [consent]: A5c before home, every launch until accepted (M-3)',
    (tester) async {
      var g = await _pumpGate(tester, server: homeJson(needs: ['consent']));
      expect(find.text("We've updated our terms"), findsOneWidget);

      // Killed here and opened again: A5c again, not home.
      g = await _pumpGate(tester, server: homeJson(needs: ['consent']));
      expect(find.text("We've updated our terms"), findsOneWidget);

      // Set before the tap: awaiting the tap already runs the calls.
      g.gateway.state = homeJson();
      await tester.tap(find.text('Agree and continue'));
      await _settle(tester);
      expect(g.gateway.count('record_eula_acceptance'), 1);
      expect(find.text('HOME'), findsOneWidget);
    },
  );

  testWidgets('A5c "Not now" signs out', (tester) async {
    final g = await _pumpGate(tester, server: homeJson(needs: ['consent']));
    await tester.tap(find.text('Not now'));
    await _settle(tester);
    expect(g.signOuts, ['out']);
  });

  testWidgets('no account and nothing queued: back to sign-up (M-5)', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final g = await _pumpGate(
      tester,
      server: homeJson(
        hasAccount: false,
        path: null,
        needs: ['consent', 'birth_date', 'name', 'path'],
      ),
    );
    expect(g.location, '/signup?entry=returning');
  });

  testWidgets('a queued A5 counts as done: only A6 is asked', (tester) async {
    final g = await _pumpGate(
      tester,
      server: homeJson(
        hasAccount: false,
        path: null,
        needs: ['consent', 'birth_date', 'name', 'path'],
      ),
      prefsValues: {
        'attune.onboarding.user.$_uid.pending_sign_up':
            '{"display_name":"Ama","birth_date":"1990-01-01"}',
      },
      setUp: (gw) => gw.failNext = null,
    );
    // The replay ran (complete_sign_up refused offline here, so it stays
    // queued) and the server still has no account: A6.
    expect(find.text('What brings you to Attune?'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(g.store.pendingSignUp, isNotNull);
  });

  testWidgets('needs [birth_date]: "Add your date of birth", then home', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final g = await _pumpGate(tester, server: homeJson(needs: ['birth_date']));
    expect(find.text('Add your date of birth'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.byTooltip('Back'), findsNothing);
    await tester.enterText(find.bySemanticsLabel('Day of birth'), '8');
    await tester.enterText(find.bySemanticsLabel('Month of birth'), '11');
    await tester.enterText(find.bySemanticsLabel('Year of birth'), '1991');
    await tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
    g.gateway.state = homeJson();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Continue'));
    await _settle(tester);
    expect(g.gateway.calls.firstWhere((c) => c.$1 == 'set_my_birth_date').$2, {
      'p_birth_date': '1991-11-08',
    });
    expect(find.text('HOME'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('an under-18 date of birth opens H1', (tester) async {
    final g = await _pumpGate(tester, server: homeJson(needs: ['birth_date']));
    g.gateway.birthDateStatus = 'not_eligible';
    await tester.enterText(find.bySemanticsLabel('Day of birth'), '1');
    await tester.enterText(find.bySemanticsLabel('Month of birth'), '1');
    await tester.enterText(find.bySemanticsLabel('Year of birth'), '2012');
    await tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
    g.gateway.state = homeJson(needs: ['birth_date'], hold: true);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Continue'));
    await _settle(tester);
    expect(find.text('We need to check your account'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('a hold opens H1; Sign out signs out', (tester) async {
    final g = await _pumpGate(tester, server: homeJson(hold: true));
    expect(find.text('We need to check your account'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Contact support'), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Sign out'));
    await _settle(tester);
    expect(g.signOuts, ['out']);
  });

  testWidgets('an unknown need asks for an update, never home (P-15)', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    await _pumpGate(tester, server: homeJson(needs: ['from_a_later_wave']));
    expect(find.text('Update Attune to continue'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('needs [name]: the name, then home', (tester) async {
    final g = await _pumpGate(tester, server: homeJson(needs: ['name']));
    await tester.enterText(find.byType(TextField), '  Akosua  ');
    g.gateway.state = homeJson();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Continue'));
    await _settle(tester);
    expect(g.gateway.savedNames.single, (_uid, 'Akosua'));
    expect(find.text('HOME'), findsOneWidget);

  testWidgets('A6 Just for me: set_my_path, then home', (tester) async {
    final g = await _pumpGate(
      tester,
      server: homeJson(path: null, needs: ['path']),

    g.gateway.onSetPath =

    await tester.tap(find.text('Just for me'));
    await tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
    await tester.tap(find.text('Continue'));

      'p_path': 'just_me',

  testWidgets('A6 Relationship: R1 follows', (tester) async {

    );

    await tester.tap(find.text('In a relationship'));
    await tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
    await tester.tap(find.text('Continue'));
    await _settle(tester);
    expect(find.byType(InvitePartnerScreen), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Create my invite'));

    expect(find.text('K7P2QM'), findsOneWidget);

  testWidgets('A6 offline: Just for me is queued; Dating is not (D20)', (

    await tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));

    expect(g.gateway.calls.firstWhere((c) => c.$1 == 'set_my_path').$2, {
      'p_path': 'relationship',
      'p_confirmed': true,
    });
    expect(find.text('HOME'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets(

  testWidgets('A6 "My partner already invited me" takes a code', (

  ) async {

  testWidgets('nothing known and offline: try again or sign out', (
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The copy**

Create `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-26-spec1-w3/arb_task5.txt` with exactly (the entries to append, no trailing comma):

```json
  "flow_dobAddTitle": "Add your date of birth",
  "flow_holdTitle": "We need to check your account",
  "flow_holdBody": "You can't use Attune while we do this. If you think this is a mistake, contact support.",
  "flow_holdContact": "Contact support",
  "flow_signOut": "Sign out",
  "flow_updateTitle": "Update Attune to continue",
  "flow_updateBody": "This version of Attune can't finish setting up your account. Update the app from your app store, then open it again.",
  "flow_checkOfflineTitle": "We couldn't check your account",
  "flow_checkOfflineBody": "Check your connection, then try again.",
  "flow_invitePartnerTitle": "Invite your partner",
  "flow_invitePartnerBody": "Send a link. When your partner joins, your private space opens.",
  "flow_invitePartnerCreate": "Create my invite",
  "flow_invitePartnerLater": "Invite later",
  "flow_invitePartnerDone": "Done"
```

Then append them, regenerate, and restore the one doc comment the generator rewrites:

```bash
cd /Users/user/attune
grep -cP '[^\x00-\x7F]' .superpowers/sdd/2026-10-26-spec1-w3/arb_task5.txt
python3 -I .superpowers/sdd/2026-10-26-spec1-w3/arb_append.py .superpowers/sdd/2026-10-26-spec1-w3/arb_task5.txt
flutter gen-l10n --arb-dir=lib/i10n --template-arb-file=app_en.arb --output-dir=lib/i10n/generated --output-localization-file=app_localizations.dart --no-synthetic-package > /dev/null
perl -0pi -e "s/\*\*'You\\\\'re browsing/**'You're browsing/" lib/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart
git diff --numstat lib/i10n/generated | awk '{s+=$2} END {print "deleted lines:", s+0}'
```

Expected: `0` (no character outside ASCII); `added 14 keys`; `deleted lines: 0` (the generator adds and never rewrites).

Create `lib/features/onboarding/presentation/screens/invite_partner_screen.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:attune/app/theme/design_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_buttons.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_frame.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_text_field.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/path_chooser.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/widgets/invite_card.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/relationships/data/relationship_invite_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/home/home_seams.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/semantics.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

/// The invite service, replaceable in tests.
final invitePartnerServiceProvider = Provider<RelationshipInviteService>(
  (ref) => RelationshipInviteService(),
);

/// R1 until W4b (Spec 1 §3.8; W3 plan P-8): "Invite your partner" with
/// today's invite (InviteCard: QR, code, Share, Copy), created only when the
/// person taps "Create my invite". From another path ([switchPath]) that
/// tap is also the switch to Relationship (§3.12: "path set when the
/// invite is sent"); [pauseConfirmed] says the Dating pause was already
/// confirmed. W4b's InvitePartnerStep replaces it under flow_couples_v2.
class InvitePartnerScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const InvitePartnerScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.onDone,
    this.switchPath = false,
    this.pauseConfirmed = false,
  });

  final VoidCallback onDone;
  final bool switchPath;
  final bool pauseConfirmed;

  @override
  ConsumerState<InvitePartnerScreen> createState() =>
      _InvitePartnerScreenState();
}

class _InvitePartnerScreenState extends ConsumerState<InvitePartnerScreen> {
  RelationshipInvite? _invite;
  bool _busy = false;
  String? _error;
  int _errorCount = 0;

  void _fail(String message) {
    setState(() {
      _error = message;
      _errorCount++;
    });
    SemanticsService.announce(message, Directionality.of(context));
  }

  Future<void> _create() async {
    if (_busy) return;
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final userId = ref.read(homeAuthProvider).currentUser?.id;
    if (userId == null) return;
    setState(() {
      _busy = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      if (widget.switchPath) {
        final outcome = await choosePath(
          context,
          ref,
          userId: userId,
          path: AppPath.relationship,
          confirmed: widget.pauseConfirmed,
        );
        if (!mounted) return;
        if (outcome == PathChoiceOutcome.cancelled) return;
        if (outcome != PathChoiceOutcome.done) {
          _fail(
            outcome == PathChoiceOutcome.offline
                ? loc.flow_switchOffline
                : loc.flow_saveFailed,
          );
          return;
        }
      }
      final invite =
          await ref.read(invitePartnerServiceProvider).createInvite();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _invite = invite);
      SemanticsService.announce(invite.code, Directionality.of(context));
    } on RelationshipInviteException catch (error) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      if (error.alreadyActiveRelationship) {
        widget.onDone();
        return;
      }
      _fail(error.message);
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) _fail(loc.flow_saveFailed);
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
      // The link state changed (pending): every reader catches up.
      await ref.read(homeStateProvider(userId).notifier).refresh();
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final invite = _invite;
    final error = _error;
    return FlowFrame(
      title: loc.flow_invitePartnerTitle,
      hint: loc.flow_invitePartnerBody,
      body: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          if (invite != null) InviteCard(invite: invite),
          if (error != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
            FlowErrorLine(
              key: ValueKey(_errorCount),
              text: error,
              style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
      actions: [
        if (invite == null) ...[
          FlowPrimaryButton(
            label: loc.flow_invitePartnerCreate,
            busy: _busy,
            onPressed: _create,
          ),
          FlowTextLink(
            label: loc.flow_invitePartnerLater,
            onPressed: widget.onDone,
          ),
        ] else
          FlowPrimaryButton(
            label: loc.flow_invitePartnerDone,
            onPressed: widget.onDone,
          ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
/// H1 · Account check (Spec 1 §3.15): any hold. No reason, no motion.

class AccountHoldStep extends StatefulWidget {

class _AccountHoldStepState extends State<AccountHoldStep> with SubmitOnce {

          onPressed: () => submitOnce(widget.onSignOut),
        ),
      ],

}

/// A need this build doesn't know (HomeNeed.unknown, a later wave added

/// The account couldn't be read and nothing on the phone says where it

class CheckOfflineStep extends StatefulWidget {
  const CheckOfflineStep({
    super.key,
    required this.onRetry,
    required this.onSignOut,
  });

  final FutureOr<void> Function() onRetry;
  final FutureOr<void> Function() onSignOut;

  @override
  State<CheckOfflineStep> createState() => _CheckOfflineStepState();
}

class _CheckOfflineStepState extends State<CheckOfflineStep> with SubmitOnce {

      title: loc.flow_checkOfflineTitle,

        ),

      actions: [

          onPressed: () => submitOnce(widget.onRetry, tag: #retry),
        ),
        FlowSecondaryButton(
          label: loc.flow_signOut,
          onPressed: () => submitOnce(widget.onSignOut, tag: #signOut),
        ),
      ],

  }
}
```
