M-5: a signed-in person whose answer has `has_account` false and nothing queued goes to `returningSignUpLocation`, never home.**

Create `lib/features/onboarding/presentation/screens/onboarding_gate_v2.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:attune/app/routing/app_router.dart';
import 'package:attune/app/routing/sign_in_redirect.dart'
    show returningSignUpLocation;
import 'package:attune/core/flags/public_app_config.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/providers/shared_prefs_provider.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/domain/birth_date_validator.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/domain/invite_code.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/domain/phone_entry.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/steps/birth_date_step.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/steps/consent_step.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/steps/name_step.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_buttons.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_frame.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_text_field.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/home_state_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/onboarding_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/onboarding_sync_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/path_chooser.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/flow_flags.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/screens/invite_partner_screen.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/screens/onboarding_gate.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/widgets/catch_up_steps.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/widgets/couples_joined_step.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/widgets/onboarding_deck_card.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/widgets/onboarding_deck_scope.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/widgets/path_step.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/relationships/data/relationship_invite_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/safety/data/safety_sign_out.dart';
import 'package:attune/home/home_seams.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/semantics.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

/// `/onboarding` (Spec 1 §5.2): the v2 gate under flow_paths_v2, today's
/// OnboardingGate otherwise (and for the debug preview).
class OnboardingEntry extends ConsumerWidget {
  const OnboardingEntry({super.key, this.previewOnboarding = false});

  final bool previewOnboarding;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final preview = kDebugMode && previewOnboarding;
    if (!preview && ref.watch(flowPathsV2Provider)) {
      return const OnboardingGateV2();
    }
    return OnboardingGate(previewOnboarding: previewOnboarding);
  }
}

/// The full app sign-out (the safety sign-out's sequence: wipes, push
/// release, Welcome), replaceable in tests.
final catchUpSignOutProvider = Provider<Future<void> Function()>(
  (ref) => () => safetySignOut(ref),
);

/// The OnboardingSyncService the gate replays the queue with.
final onboardingSyncServiceProvider = Provider<OnboardingSyncService>(
  (ref) => OnboardingSyncService(
    homeStateService: ref.read(homeStateServiceProvider),
  ),
);

enum _Step {
  loading,
  offline,
  hold,
  update,
  consent,
  birthDate,
  name,
  path,
  inviteCode,
  invitePartner,
  joined,
}

/// The v2 gate (Spec 1 §3.7, §3.15): shows only what the account lacks, in
/// order (A5c "We've updated our terms", A2 "Add your date of birth", A1,
/// A6), then R1 for a new Relationship. A hold opens H1; a need this build
/// doesn't know asks for an update; an account that doesn't exist goes
/// back to sign-up (W2 final review M-5). Every launch with `consent` in
/// `needs` comes here before home (M-3): HomeScreen's latch sends it.
class OnboardingGateV2 extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const OnboardingGateV2({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<OnboardingGateV2> createState() => _OnboardingGateV2State();
}

class _OnboardingGateV2State extends ConsumerState<OnboardingGateV2> {
  _Step _step = _Step.loading;
  String? _error;
  int _errorCount = 0;

  /// A6 chose Relationship in this visit: R1 follows.
  bool _relationshipChosen = false;

  /// A stored invite was accepted in this visit: "You are connected".
  bool _joined = false;

  /// The stored invite was tried once in this visit.
  bool _inviteTried = false;

  /// An open space was set as Relationship once in this visit (a server
  /// that still asks for the path then gets A6, never a loop).
  bool _quietTried = false;

  String? get _userId => ref.read(homeAuthProvider).currentUser?.id;

  OnboardingStore _store(String userId) => OnboardingStore(
    ref.read(sharedPreferencesProvider),
    scope: '${OnboardingStore.userScopePrefix}.$userId',
  );

  HomeStateService get _service => ref.read(homeStateServiceProvider);

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    unawaited(_resolve());
  }

  void _show(_Step step, {String? error}) {
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _step = step;
      _error = error;
      if (error != null) _errorCount++;
    });
    if (error != null) {
      SemanticsService.announce(error, Directionality.of(context));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _go(String location) async {
    final settled = await waitForRouterSettled();
    if (!mounted) return;
    (settled != null && settled.mounted ? settled : context).go(location);
  }

  /// Replays what the phone owes, reads the server, then shows the next
  /// step (or goes home).
  Future<void> _resolve() async {
    final userId = _userId;
    if (userId == null) {
      await _go(RouteNames.login);
      return;
    }
    final store = _store(userId);
    final home = ref.read(homeStateProvider(userId).notifier);
    try {
      await ref
          .read(onboardingSyncServiceProvider)
          .flush(store, onPathSynced: home.apply)
          .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 20));
    } catch (_) {}
    final answer = await home.refresh();
    if (!mounted) return;
    await _next(userId, answer: answer);
  }

  Future<void> _next(String userId, {HomeState? answer}) async {
    final snapshot = ref.read(homeStateProvider(userId));
    final store = _store(userId);
    final live = answer ?? snapshot.server;
    final queuedSignUp = store.pendingSignUp != null;
    if (snapshot.hold) return _show(_Step.hold);
    if (live == null && !snapshot.known && !queuedSignUp) {
      return _show(_Step.offline);
    }
    // M-5: signed in with no account (a refused or lost A5): sign-up again.
    if (live != null && !live.hasAccount && !queuedSignUp) {
      await _go(returningSignUpLocation);
      return;
    }
    final needs = snapshot.needs;
    if (needs.contains(HomeNeed.unknown)) return _show(_Step.update);
    if (needs.isEmpty) {
      if (_relationshipChosen) return _show(_Step.invitePartner);
      if (_joined) return _show(_Step.joined);
      await _go(RouteNames.home);
      return;
    }
    switch (needs.first) {
      case HomeNeed.consent:
        return _show(_Step.consent);
      case HomeNeed.birthDate:
        return _show(_Step.birthDate);
      case HomeNeed.name:
        return _show(_Step.name);
      case HomeNeed.path:
        // An open or paused shared space is the Relationship path (§3.11):
        // no question whose answer is already known.
        final link = snapshot.link;
        if (!_quietTried &&
            (link == LinkState.linked || link == LinkState.paused)) {
          return _setRelationshipQuietly(userId);
        }
        // A stored invite is accepted only now, after the name (§5.2).
        if (!_inviteTried && store.pendingInviteCode != null) {
          return _acceptStoredInvite(userId);
        }
        return _show(_Step.path);
      case HomeNeed.unknown:
        return _show(_Step.update);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _setRelationshipQuietly(String userId) async {
    _quietTried = true;
    final outcome = await choosePath(
      context,
      ref,
      userId: userId,
      path: AppPath.relationship,
      confirmed: true,
      queueOffline: true,
    );
    if (!mounted) return;
    if (outcome != PathChoiceOutcome.done) {
      return _show(_Step.offline);
    }
    await _next(userId);
  }

  Future<void> _acceptStoredInvite(String userId) async {
    _inviteTried = true;
    final store = _store(userId);
    final code = store.pendingInviteCode;
    if (code == null) return _next(userId);
    try {
      await ref.read(invitePartnerServiceProvider).acceptInvite(code);
      await store.clearPendingInviteCode();
      _joined = true;
      // Joining is the Relationship path; A6 is skipped (§3.8 RI-3).
      await _setRelationshipQuietly(userId);
    } on RelationshipInviteException catch (error) {
      if (!error.retryable) await store.clearPendingInviteCode();
      _show(_Step.path, error: error.message);
    } catch (_) {
      _show(_Step.path);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _signOut() => ref.read(catchUpSignOutProvider)();

  Future<void> _acceptTerms() async {
    final userId = _userId;
    if (userId == null) return;
    try {
      await _service.acceptUpdatedTerms();
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) {
        _show(
          _Step.consent,
          error: AppLocalizations.of(context)!.flow_saveFailed,
        );
      }
      return;
    }
    await _resolve();
  }

  Future<void> _saveBirthDate(DateTime date) async {
    final userId = _userId;
    if (userId == null) return;
    final BirthDateOutcome outcome;
    try {
      outcome = await _service.setBirthDate(date);
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) {
        _show(
          _Step.birthDate,
          error: AppLocalizations.of(context)!.flow_saveFailed,
        );
      }
      return;
    }
    // Under 18: the server placed a hold (§3.15); H1, nothing else.
    if (outcome == BirthDateOutcome.notEligible) {
      await ref.read(homeStateProvider(userId).notifier).refresh();
      return _show(_Step.hold);
    }
    await _resolve();
  }

  Future<void> _saveName(String name) async {
    final userId = _userId;
    if (userId == null) return;
    try {
      await _service.saveDisplayName(userId, name);
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) {
        _show(_Step.name, error: AppLocalizations.of(context)!.flow_saveFailed);
      }
      return;
    }
    await _resolve();
  }

  Future<void> _choosePath(AppPath path) async {
    final userId = _userId;
    if (userId == null) return;
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final outcome = await choosePath(
      context,
      ref,
      userId: userId,
      path: path,
      queueOffline: true,
    );
    if (!mounted) return;
    switch (outcome) {
      case PathChoiceOutcome.done:
        _relationshipChosen = path == AppPath.relationship;
        await _next(userId);
      case PathChoiceOutcome.cancelled:
        return;
      case PathChoiceOutcome.datingOffline:
        _show(_Step.path, error: loc.flow_pathDatingOffline);
      case PathChoiceOutcome.offline:
      case PathChoiceOutcome.relationshipOpen:
      case PathChoiceOutcome.failed:
        _show(_Step.path, error: loc.flow_saveFailed);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _submitInviteCode(String raw) async {
    final userId = _userId;
    if (userId == null) return;
    final code = normalizeInviteCode(raw);
    if (code == null) {
      return _show(
        _Step.inviteCode,
        error: AppLocalizations.of(context)!.flow_inviteCodeError,
      );
    }
    await _store(userId).storePendingInviteCode(code);
    _inviteTried = false;
    await _acceptStoredInvite(userId);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final user = ref.read(homeAuthProvider).currentUser;
    final step = switch (_step) {
      _Step.loading => const Scaffold(
        body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
      ),
      _Step.offline => CheckOfflineStep(onRetry: _resolve, onSignOut: _signOut),
      _Step.hold => AccountHoldStep(onSignOut: _signOut),
      _Step.update => UpdateAppStep(onSignOut: _signOut),
      _Step.consent => ConsentStep(
        updatedTerms: true,
        errorText: _error,
        onAgree: _acceptTerms,
        onNotNow: _signOut,
      ),
      _Step.birthDate => BirthDateStep(
        key: ValueKey('dob-$_errorCount'),
        order: birthDateOrderFor(Localizations.localeOf(context)),
        todayUtc: _todayUtc(),
        title: AppLocalizations.of(context)!.flow_dobAddTitle,
        onSubmit: _saveBirthDate,
      ),
      _Step.name => NameStep(onSubmit: _saveName),
      _Step.path => PathStep(
        key: ValueKey('path-$_errorCount'),
        ownCountry: countryOfPhone(_e164(user?.phone)),
        openCountries: ref.watch(datingOpenCountriesProvider),
        errorText: _error,
        onContinue: _choosePath,
        onAlreadyInvited: () => _show(_Step.inviteCode),
      ),
      _Step.inviteCode => _InviteCodeStep(
        errorText: _error,
        errorCount: _errorCount,
        onSubmit: _submitInviteCode,
        onBack: () => _show(_Step.path),
      ),
      _Step.invitePartner => InvitePartnerScreen(
        onDone: () => unawaited(_go(RouteNames.home)),
      ),
      // Today's "You are connected" card, in the deck it was built for.
      _Step.joined => Scaffold(
        body: SafeArea(
          child: OnboardingDeckScope(
            cardKey: 0,
            accent: OnboardingDeckAccent.couples,
            enableDeck: false,
            child: CouplesJoinedStep(
              onFinish: () => unawaited(_go(RouteNames.home)),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    };
    return step;
  }

  static DateTime _todayUtc() {
    final now = DateTime.now().toUtc();
    return DateTime.utc(now.year, now.month, now.day);
  }

  static String _e164(String? phone) {
    if (phone == null || phone.isEmpty) return '';
    return phone.startsWith('+') ? phone : '+$phone';
  }
}

/// RI-2 for a signed-in person (A6's "My partner already invited me"):
/// the code, then today's silent accept until W4b's RI-3.
class _InviteCodeStep extends StatefulWidget {
  const _InviteCodeStep({
    required this.onSubmit,
    required this.onBack,
    required this.errorCount,
    this.errorText,
  });

  final Future<void> Function(String code) onSubmit;
  final VoidCallback onBack;
  final String? errorText;
  final int errorCount;

  @override
  State<_InviteCodeStep> createState() => _InviteCodeStepState();
}

class _InviteCodeStepState extends State<_InviteCodeStep> {
  final _code = TextEditingController();
  bool _busy = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _code.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _submit() async {
    if (_busy) return;
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      await widget.onSubmit(_code.text);
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    return FlowFrame(
      title: loc.flow_inviteCodeTitle,
      onBack: widget.onBack,
      body: FlowTextField(
        controller: _code,
        label: loc.flow_inviteCodeLabel,
        errorText: widget.errorText,
        errorCount: widget.errorCount,
        textCapitalization: TextCapitalization.characters,
        textInputAction: TextInputAction.done,
        inputFormatters: [LengthLimitingTextInputFormatter(12)],
        autofocus: true,
        onSubmitted: (_) => _submit(),
      ),
      actions: [
        FlowPrimaryButton(
          label: loc.flow_continue,
          busy: _busy,
          onPressed: _submit,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
// Spec 1 W3b (§3.10, §3.12): the Me home. Your space (Journal, Quizzes,
// Healing after a breakup or a loss); J1 and its one celebration; J4's
// More ways to use Attune.
import 'package:attune/features/me/presentation/me_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/me/presentation/screens/me_home_screen.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/home_state_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/relationships/presentation/relationship_lifecycle_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/home/home_seams.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

import '../../helpers/fake_home_state.dart';
import '../../helpers/flow_harness.dart';
import '../../helpers/home_harness.dart';

const _uid = 'u-me';

User _user(String phone) => User(
  id: _uid,
  appMetadata: const {},
  userMetadata: const {},
  aud: 'authenticated',
  createdAt: '2026-10-01T00:00:00Z',
  phone: phone,
);

List<Override> _overrides(
  FakeHomeStateGateway gateway, {
  String phone = '+233244123456',
  ({bool? hasNote, bool? hasQuiz}) first = (hasNote: true, hasQuiz: true),
}) => [
  homeAuthProvider.overrideWithValue(FakeHomeAuth(_user(phone))),
  homeStateServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(HomeStateService(gateway)),
  relationshipLifecycleProvider.overrideWith((ref) async => null),
  firstActionInputsProvider.overrideWithValue(first),
];

Future<(FlowHarness, FakeHomeStateGateway)> _pump(
  WidgetTester tester,
  Map<String, dynamic> json, {
  String phone = '+233244123456',
  List<String> open = const ['GB', 'GH'],
  ({bool? hasNote, bool? hasQuiz}) first = (hasNote: true, hasQuiz: true),
  Map<String, Object> prefsValues = const {},
}) async {
  // A fresh ProviderScope each time (pumpWidget would reuse the last one).
  await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox());
  final gateway = FakeHomeStateGateway(state: json);
  final h = await pumpFlowScreen(
    tester,
    () => MeHomeScreen(home: snapshotOf(json)),
    openCountries: open,
    prefsValues: prefsValues,
    overrides: _overrides(gateway, phone: phone, first: first),
  );
  return (h, gateway);
}

void main() {
  testWidgets('Just for me: your space, no Dating slot', (tester) async {
    await _pump(tester, homeJson(path: 'just_me', datingEnabled: true));
    expect(find.text('Your space'), findsOneWidget);
    for (final row in ['Journal', 'Quizzes', 'Healing']) {
      expect(find.text(row, skipOffstage: false), findsOneWidget);
    }
    expect(
      find.text('After a breakup or a loss', skipOffstage: false),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(find.text('Set up Dating'), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('J1: "Start here" before any note or quiz', (tester) async {
    final (h, _) = await _pump(
      tester,
      homeJson(path: 'just_me'),
      first: (hasNote: false, hasQuiz: false),
    );
    expect(find.text('Start here'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Write your first note'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(
      find.text('Take a 3-minute look at how you connect'),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(h.prefs.getString('attune.flow.$_uid.first_action'), 'started');
  });

  testWidgets('J1: "Saved, just for you." once, after the first note', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final (h, _) = await _pump(
      tester,
      homeJson(path: 'just_me'),
      first: (hasNote: true, hasQuiz: false),
      prefsValues: {'attune.flow.$_uid.first_action': 'started'},
    );
    expect(find.text('Saved, just for you.'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(h.prefs.getString('attune.flow.$_uid.first_action'), 'celebrated');

    await _pump(
      tester,
      homeJson(path: 'just_me'),
      first: (hasNote: true, hasQuiz: false),
      prefsValues: {'attune.flow.$_uid.first_action': 'celebrated'},
    );
    expect(find.text('Saved, just for you.'), findsNothing);
    expect(find.text('Start here'), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('J4: More ways offers the other two paths', (tester) async {
    final (_, gateway) = await _pump(tester, homeJson(path: 'just_me'));
    await tester.scrollUntilVisible(
      find.text('More ways to use Attune'),
      200,
      scrollable: find.byType(Scrollable).first,
    );
    await tester.tap(find.text('More ways to use Attune'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Invite a partner'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Single and open to dating'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Just for me'), findsNothing);
    gateway.state = homeJson(path: 'dating');
    await tester.tap(find.text('Single and open to dating'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(gateway.calls.firstWhere((c) => c.$1 == 'set_my_path').$2, {
      'p_path': 'dating',
      'p_confirmed': false,
    });
  });

  testWidgets('meets the tap-target, label and contrast guidelines', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final semantics = tester.ensureSemantics();
    await _pump(
      tester,
      homeJson(path: 'just_me'),
      first: (hasNote: false, hasQuiz: false),
    );
    await expectFlowGuidelines(tester);
    semantics.dispose();
  });

  testWidgets('Me goldens', (tester) async {
    await expectFlowGoldens(
      tester,
      'me_just_me',
      () => MeHomeScreen(home: snapshotOf(homeJson(path: 'just_me'))),
      overrides: _overrides(
        FakeHomeStateGateway(state: homeJson(path: 'just_me')),
        first: (hasNote: false, hasQuiz: false),
      ),
    );
  });
}
```

```dart
// Spec 1 W3b (§5.8, D20): the Me home's device keys (the country the Dating
// slot uses, J1's progress) go on sign-out, for every account on the phone.
import 'package:attune/app/device_wipes.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/device/device_wipe_registry.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

void main() {
  test("sign-out clears every account's Me keys and nothing else", () async {
    SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues({
      'attune.flow.u1.country_override': 'GH',
      'attune.flow.u2.first_action': 'started',
      'attune.flow.u1.other': 'kept',
    });
    final registry = DeviceWipeRegistry();
    registerDeviceWipes(registry);
    expect(registry.entries.map((e) => e.id), contains('spec1.me_device'));
    await registry.run(WipeMoment.signOut);
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    expect(prefs.getKeys(), {'attune.flow.u1.other'});
  });
}
```

Create `lib/features/me/presentation/widgets/your_space_section.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:attune/app/theme/design_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/utils/bottom_sheet_utils.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/utils/exports/export_screens.dart' show RouteNames;
import 'package:attune/core/widgets/info_row_widget.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/healing/presentation/providers/healing_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/healing/presentation/widgets/healing_self_report_sheet.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/profile/widgets/know_yourself_section.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

/// The space's heading with a way to Settings (Spec 1 §3.10, §3.8 R3).
class SpaceHeader extends StatelessWidget {
  const SpaceHeader({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return Row(
      children: [
        Expanded(
          child: Semantics(
            header: true,
            child: Text(
              loc.flow_spaceTitle,
              style: theme.textTheme.headlineSmall?.copyWith(
                color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        IconButton(
          tooltip: loc.flow_settingsTooltip,
          icon: const Icon(Icons.settings_outlined),
          onPressed: () => context.pushNamed('settings'),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// "Your space" (Spec 1 §3.10 rows; §3.8 R3): Journal, Quizzes, Healing,
/// on Me and on the Relationship space alike. [extraRows] go last.
class YourSpaceSection extends ConsumerWidget {
  const YourSpaceSection({super.key, this.extraRows = const []});

  final List<Widget> extraRows;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    return Card(
      margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
      elevation: 0,
      color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surfaceContainerLow,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
          horizontal: Spacing.md,
          vertical: Spacing.sm,
        ),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            SpaceRow(
              title: loc.flow_spaceJournal,
              subtitle: loc.flow_spaceJournalLine,
              icon: Icons.menu_book_outlined,
              onTap: () => context.pushNamed('reflectionJournal'),
            ),
            SpaceRow(
              title: loc.flow_spaceQuizzes,
              subtitle: loc.flow_spaceQuizzesLine,
              icon: Icons.psychology_outlined,
              onTap: () => openQuizzes(context),
            ),
            SpaceRow(
              title: loc.flow_spaceHealing,
              subtitle: loc.flow_spaceHealingLine,
              icon: Icons.healing_outlined,
              onTap: () => openHealing(context, ref),
            ),
            ...extraRows,
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// One row of the space: InfoRowWidget, labelled as one button, lines that
/// wrap at large text.
class SpaceRow extends StatelessWidget {
  const SpaceRow({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
    required this.icon,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  final String title;
  final String subtitle;
  final IconData icon;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ConstrainedBox(
      constraints: const BoxConstraints(minHeight: 48),
      child: InfoRowWidget(
        title: title,
        subtitle: subtitle,
        icon: icon,
        onTap: onTap,
        showDivider: false,
        showTrailingArrow: true,
        titleMaxLines: 3,
        subTitleMaxLines: 4,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: Spacing.sm),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// The quizzes, in a sheet: "Know yourself" (today's list, same gates).
Future<void> openQuizzes(BuildContext context) =>
    BottomSheetUtils.showDocumentationBottomSheet<void>(
      context: context,
      widget: const SingleChildScrollView(child: KnowYourselfSection()),
    );

/// Healing, as today's personal-mode entry does it (ChatCouplesLockedScreen):
/// the journey if one is under way, else the self-report sheet first.
Future<void> openHealing(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) async {
  final colorScheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
  final active = await ref.read(hasActiveSoloHealingJourneyProvider.future);
  if (!context.mounted) return;
  if (active) {
    await context.push(RouteNames.healingJourney);
    return;
  }
  final started = await BottomSheetUtils.showDocumentationBottomSheet<bool>(
    context: context,
    backgroundColor: colorScheme.surface,
    widget: const HealingSelfReportSheet(),
  );
  if (started == true && context.mounted) {
    await context.push(RouteNames.healingJourney);
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The switch helper (P-18)**

Create `lib/features/me/presentation/path_switch.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:attune/app/routing/app_router.dart' show RouteNames;
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/path_chooser.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/home/home_seams.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

/// A switch to another path from inside the app (Spec 1 §3.12): J4's
/// "More ways to use Attune", the Dating slot's "Not ready?", and Settings
/// -> How you use Attune. Every switch is silent to everyone else.
///   * to Relationship: R1, where creating the invite is the switch
///     (`/invite-partner?switch=1`; a Dating pause is confirmed there if
///     the server asks for it);
///   * Dating -> Just for me: "Use Attune just for you?" first, unless
///     [oneTap] and nothing is enrolled (a wrong first choice, §3.12);
///   * a refusal that needs a confirmation (a pending invite) asks it.
/// [home] is the state the screen shows (else the provider's). Errors are
/// shown in a snackbar; the home follows the new state.
Future<PathChoiceOutcome> switchPath(
  BuildContext context,
  WidgetRef ref, {
  required AppPath to,
  bool oneTap = false,
  HomeSnapshot? home,
}) async {
  final userId = ref.read(homeAuthProvider).currentUser?.id;
  if (userId == null) return PathChoiceOutcome.failed;
  final HomeSnapshot state = home ?? ref.read(homeStateProvider(userId));
  if (to == AppPath.relationship) {
    await context.push('${RouteNames.invitePartner}?switch=1');
    return PathChoiceOutcome.done;
  }
  final enrolled = state.dating?.enrolled ?? false;
  if (state.path == AppPath.dating &&
      to == AppPath.justMe &&
      (!oneTap || enrolled)) {
    if (!await showJustForMeSheet(context)) return PathChoiceOutcome.cancelled;
    if (!context.mounted) return PathChoiceOutcome.cancelled;
  }
  final outcome = await choosePath(
    context,
    ref,
    userId: userId,
    path: to,
    partnerName: state.linkDetail.partnerFirstName,
  );
  if (!context.mounted) return outcome;
  final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
  final message = switch (outcome) {
    PathChoiceOutcome.datingOffline => loc.flow_pathDatingOffline,
    PathChoiceOutcome.offline => loc.flow_switchOffline,
    PathChoiceOutcome.failed => loc.flow_saveFailed,
    _ => null,
  };
  if (message != null) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.maybeOf(
      context,
    )?.showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(message)));
  }
  return outcome;
}

/// §3.12 "Use Attune just for you?" (Dating -> Just for me).
Future<bool> showJustForMeSheet(BuildContext context) {
  final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
  return showFlowConfirm(
    context,
    title: loc.flow_justMeSheetTitle,
    body: loc.flow_justMeSheetBody,
    confirm: loc.flow_justMeSheetSwitch,
    cancel: loc.flow_cancel,
  );
}
```

```dart
import 'package:attune/app/theme/design_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/ui/text/allow_large_text.dart';

import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

/// J4 · "More ways to use Attune" (Spec 1 §3.10, research 09 P19): one
/// neutral row; the sheet offers the two other paths.
class MoreWaysRow extends ConsumerWidget {
  const MoreWaysRow({super.key, required this.home});

  /// The home the screen shows; the row waits for its live path.
  final HomeSnapshot home;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final path = home.path;
    if (path == null) return const SizedBox.shrink();
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    return SpaceRow(
      title: loc.flow_moreWays,
      subtitle: '',
      icon: Icons.alt_route_outlined,
      onTap: () async {
        final to = await showModalBottomSheet<AppPath>(
          context: context,
          showDragHandle: true,
          isScrollControlled: true,
          builder: (_) => MoreWaysSheet(current: path),
        );
        if (to != null && context.mounted) {
          await switchPath(context, ref, to: to, home: home);
        }
      },
    );
  }
}

/// The two paths the person isn't on (§3.10 J4). Pops the chosen one.
class MoreWaysSheet extends StatelessWidget {
  const MoreWaysSheet({super.key, required this.current});

  final AppPath current;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final options = [
      if (current != AppPath.relationship)
        (
          AppPath.relationship,
          loc.flow_moreInvite,
          loc.flow_pathRelationshipLine,
        ),
      if (current != AppPath.dating)
        (AppPath.dating, loc.flow_pathDatingTitle, loc.flow_pathDatingLine),
      if (current != AppPath.justMe)
        (AppPath.justMe, loc.flow_pathJustMeTitle, loc.flow_pathJustMeLine),
    ];
    return AllowLargeText(
      child: SafeArea(
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
            Spacing.lg,
            0,
            Spacing.lg,
            Spacing.md,
          ),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              Semantics(
                header: true,
                child: Text(
                  loc.flow_moreWays,
                  style: theme.textTheme.titleLarge?.copyWith(
                    color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
              for (final (path, title, line) in options)
                SpaceRow(
                  title: title,
                  subtitle: line,
                  icon: switch (path) {
                    AppPath.relationship => Icons.favorite_outline,
                    AppPath.dating => Icons.people_outline,
                    AppPath.justMe => Icons.person_outline,
                  },
                  onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(path),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: The Me device store (neutral keys, D20) and J1's inputs**

Create `lib/features/me/data/me_device_store.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

/// Where J1 stands on this phone (Spec 1 §3.10).
enum FirstActionStage { none, started, celebrated }

/// The Me home's device keys, per account (spec §5.5): the country the
/// Dating slot uses instead of the phone number's (`country_override`, a
/// neutral name, D20) and how far J1 got. Both go on sign-out (§5.8).
class MeDeviceStore {
  MeDeviceStore(this._prefs, {required String userId}) : _userId = userId;

  final SharedPreferences _prefs;
  final String _userId;

  static String countryKey(String userId) =>
      'attune.flow.$userId.country_override';
  static String firstActionKey(String userId) =>
      'attune.flow.$userId.first_action';

  /// An ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 code, or null.
  String? get countryOverride {
    final value = _prefs.getString(countryKey(_userId));
    return value != null && RegExp(r'^[A-Z]{2}$').hasMatch(value)
        ? value
        : null;
  }

  Future<void> setCountryOverride(String iso) =>
      _prefs.setString(countryKey(_userId), iso.toUpperCase());

  FirstActionStage get firstAction => switch (_prefs.getString(
    firstActionKey(_userId),
  )) {
    'started' => FirstActionStage.started,
    'celebrated' => FirstActionStage.celebrated,
    _ => FirstActionStage.none,
  };

  Future<void> setFirstAction(FirstActionStage stage) =>
      _prefs.setString(firstActionKey(_userId), stage.name);
}
```

```dart
import 'package:attune/features/quiz/presentation/providers/quiz_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/reflection_journal/presentation/providers/reflection_journal_providers.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

/// What J1 needs to know (Spec 1 §3.10): whether this person has a journal
/// note and a finished quiz. Null while unknown.
final firstActionInputsProvider = Provider<({bool? hasNote, bool? hasQuiz})>((
  ref,
) {
  final notes = ref.watch(journalEntriesProvider).valueOrNull;
  final quizzes = ref.watch(userQuizzesStatusProvider).valueOrNull;
  return (
    hasNote: notes?.isNotEmpty,
    hasQuiz: quizzes?.values.any((status) => status.completed),
  );
});
```

- [ ] **Step 8: J1**

Create `lib/features/me/presentation/widgets/first_action_card.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:attune/app/theme/design_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/providers/shared_prefs_provider.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/ui/motion/motion_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/ui/motion/reduce_motion.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/me/data/me_device_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/me/presentation/me_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/me/presentation/widgets/your_space_section.dart';
import 'package:attune/home/home_seams.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

/// J1 (Spec 1 §3.10): "Start here" until the first note or first quiz; once,
/// after the first note, "Saved, just for you." (the Just-for-me
/// celebration: the check fades in over 150 ms, text only).
class FirstActionCard extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const FirstActionCard({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<FirstActionCard> createState() => _FirstActionCardState();
}

class _FirstActionCardState extends ConsumerState<FirstActionCard> {
  /// The celebration was decided this visit: it stays until the person
  /// leaves, though the phone already counts it as shown.
  bool _celebrating = false;

  MeDeviceStore? _store() {
    final userId = ref.read(homeAuthProvider).currentUser?.id;
    if (userId == null) return null;
    return MeDeviceStore(ref.read(sharedPreferencesProvider), userId: userId);
  }

  void _record(FirstActionStage stage) {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      _store()?.setFirstAction(stage);
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final inputs = ref.watch(firstActionInputsProvider);
    final store = _store();
    final hasNote = inputs.hasNote;
    final hasQuiz = inputs.hasQuiz;
    if (store == null || hasNote == null || hasQuiz == null) {
      return const SizedBox.shrink();
    }
    final stage = store.firstAction;
    if (!hasNote && !hasQuiz) {
      if (stage == FirstActionStage.none) _record(FirstActionStage.started);
      return const _StartHere();
    }
    if (_celebrating || (hasNote && stage == FirstActionStage.started)) {
      if (!_celebrating) {
        _celebrating = true;
        _record(FirstActionStage.celebrated);
      }
      return const _SavedForYou();
    }
    if (stage != FirstActionStage.celebrated) {
      _record(FirstActionStage.celebrated);
    }
    return const SizedBox.shrink();
  }
}

class _StartHere extends StatelessWidget {
  const _StartHere();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: Spacing.md),
      child: Card(
        margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
        elevation: 0,
        color: theme.colorScheme.secondaryContainer,
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              Semantics(
                header: true,
                child: Text(
                  loc.flow_firstStartHere,
                  style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                    color: theme.colorScheme.onSecondaryContainer,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              SpaceRow(
                title: loc.flow_firstWriteNote,
                subtitle: '',
                icon: Icons.edit_note_outlined,
                onTap: () => context.pushNamed('reflectionJournal'),
              ),
              SpaceRow(
                title: loc.flow_firstQuiz,
                subtitle: '',
                icon: Icons.psychology_outlined,
                onTap:
                    () => context.pushNamed('quizEntry', extra: 'attachment'),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SavedForYou extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SavedForYou();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final duration =
        reduceMotionOf(context) ? Duration.zero : kSelectionDuration;
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: Spacing.md),
      child: Semantics(
        liveRegion: true,
        child: Row(
          children: [
            TweenAnimationBuilder<double>(
              tween: Tween(begin: 0, end: 1),
              duration: duration,
              builder:
                  (_, opacity, child) =>
                      Opacity(opacity: opacity, child: child),
              child: Icon(
                Icons.check_circle_outline,
                color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: Spacing.sm),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                loc.flow_firstSaved,
                style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                  color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 11: Wipe the Me device keys at sign-out**

Replace the whole of `lib/app/device_wipes.dart` (a file this plan created) with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:attune/core/device/device_wipe_registry.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

/// Every feature's device wipes, registered once at boot (main.dart; spec
/// §5.8). Spec 1's entries are here; another feature adds one line calling
/// its own register function (Spec 2: `registerDatingDeviceWipes(registry)`).
void registerDeviceWipes([DeviceWipeRegistry? registry]) {
  final target = registry ?? DeviceWipeRegistry.instance;
  for (final entry in spec1DeviceWipes()) {
    target.register(entry);
  }
}

/// Removes every prefs key [matches] accepts (all accounts on the phone:
/// the next person to sign in here inherits none of them).
Future<void> removePrefsKeys(bool Function(String key) matches) async {
  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  for (final key in prefs.getKeys().where(matches).toList()) {
    await prefs.remove(key);
  }
}

/// `attune.home.<uid>.state` (HomeStateStore.keyFor).
bool isHomeStateKey(String key) =>
    key.startsWith('attune.home.') && key.endsWith('.state');

/// Spec 1's entries (spec §5.8 table). W4b adds the partner name and Ask 2
/// progress.
List<DeviceWipeEntry> spec1DeviceWipes() => [
  DeviceWipeEntry(
    id: 'spec1.home_state_cache',
    moments: const {WipeMoment.signOut},
    keys: const {'attune.home.<uid>.state'},
    run: () => removePrefsKeys(isHomeStateKey),
  ),
  // W3b: the Me home's country and J1 progress.
  DeviceWipeEntry(
    id: 'spec1.me_device',
    moments: const {WipeMoment.signOut},
    keys: const {
      'attune.flow.<uid>.country_override',
      'attune.flow.<uid>.first_action',
    },
    run:
        () => removePrefsKeys(
          (key) =>
              key.startsWith('attune.flow.') &&
              (key.endsWith('.country_override') ||
                  key.endsWith('.first_action')),
        ),
  ),
];
```

```dart
import '../../helpers/fake_home_state.dart';
import '../../helpers/fake_invites.dart';
import '../../helpers/flow_harness.dart';
import '../../helpers/home_harness.dart';

Future<(FlowHarness, FakeHomeStateGateway, FakeInvites)> _pump(
  WidgetTester tester,
  HomeSnapshot home, {
  double textScale = 1.0,
  FakeInvites? fakeInvites,
}) async {
  await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox());
  final gateway = FakeHomeStateGateway(state: homeJson(path: 'relationship'));
  final invites = fakeInvites ?? FakeInvites();
  final h = await pumpFlowScreen(
    tester,
    () => RelationshipSpaceScreen(home: home),
    textScale: textScale,
    // Tall enough that the whole list is built (it is lazy).
    size: const Size(390, 2400),
    overrides: [
      homeAuthProvider.overrideWithValue(FakeHomeAuth(testUser('u-r3'))),
      homeStateServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(HomeStateService(gateway)),
      invitePartnerServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(invites),
      relationshipLifecycleProvider.overrideWith((ref) async => null),
    ],
  );
  return (h, gateway, invites);
}

final _states = {
  'pending': snapshotOf(homeJson(path: 'relationship', link: 'pending')),
  'none': snapshotOf(homeJson(path: 'relationship')),
  'paused': snapshotOf(homeJson(path: 'relationship', link: 'paused')),
  'ended_window': snapshotOf(
    homeJson(
      path: 'relationship',
      link: 'ended_window',
      windowEndsAt: '2026-11-20T10:00:00Z',
    ),
  ),
  'after_ending': snapshotOf(
    homeJson(path: 'relationship', link: 'after_ending'),
  ),
};

void main() {
  for (final entry in _states.entries) {
    testWidgets('${entry.key}: never anything about Dating', (tester) async {
      await _pump(tester, entry.value);
      expect(
        find.textContaining(
          RegExp('dating', caseSensitive: false),
          skipOffstage: false,
        ),
        findsNothing,
      );
      expect(find.text('Journal', skipOffstage: false), findsOneWidget);
    });
  }

  testWidgets('pending: the invite, the guide, a first note, the receipts line', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final (_, _, invites) = await _pump(tester, _states['pending']!);
    expect(invites.creates, 1, reason: 'the live invite, fetched');
    expect(find.text('K7P2QM'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(
      find.text(
        "We'll suggest a private reflection space if this takes a little while.",
      ),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(
      find.text('Write a first note', skipOffstage: false),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(
      find.text('A quiz about you, for you', skipOffstage: false),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(
      find.text(
        'When your partner joins, read receipts start off. You can both turn them on.',
        skipOffstage: false,
      ),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(
      find.text('More ways to use Attune', skipOffstage: false),
      findsNothing,
    );
  });

  testWidgets(
    'pending: "Cancel this invite" asks, then returns to the previous path',
    (tester) async {
      final (_, gateway, _) = await _pump(tester, _states['pending']!);
      await tester.tap(find.byTooltip('Invite options'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      await tester.tap(find.text('Cancel this invite'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.text('Cancel this invite?'), findsOneWidget);
      expect(
        find.text(
          "Your invite code will stop working. Your partner won't be told.",
        ),
        findsOneWidget,
      );
      await tester.tap(find.text('Cancel invite'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(
        gateway.calls
            .firstWhere((c) => c.$1 == 'withdraw_my_pending_invites')
            .$2,
        {'p_return_to_previous_path': true},
      );
      expect(
        gateway.count('get_my_home_state'),
        1,
        reason: 'the home caught up',
      );
    },
  );

  testWidgets('none: "Invite your partner" makes the invite', (tester) async {
    final (_, gateway, invites) = await _pump(tester, _states['none']!);
    expect(invites.creates, 0);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Invite your partner'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(invites.creates, 1);
    expect(gateway.count('get_my_home_state'), 1);
  });

  testWidgets('inside the ending window: no invite, no "More ways"', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    await _pump(tester, _states['ended_window']!);
    expect(find.text('Invite your partner'), findsNothing);
    expect(
      find.text('More ways to use Attune', skipOffstage: false),
      findsNothing,
    );
  });

  testWidgets('after the window: one "More ways" row', (tester) async {
    await _pump(tester, _states['after_ending']!);
    expect(
      find.text('More ways to use Attune', skipOffstage: false),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(find.text('Invite your partner'), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('meets the tap-target, label and contrast guidelines', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final semantics = tester.ensureSemantics();
    // Today's InviteCard (W4b replaces it) shows the code as SelectableText,
    // which the tap-target guideline flags; the rest of the pending space
    // is checked with the code unavailable.
    for (final state in ['pending', 'none']) {
      await _pump(
        tester,
        _states[state]!,
        fakeInvites: FakeInvites(
          createError: const RelationshipInviteException(
            'Could not create invite.',
          ),
        ),
      );
      await expectFlowGuidelines(tester);
    }
    semantics.dispose();
  });

  for (final state in ['pending', 'none']) {
    testWidgets('R3 $state goldens', (tester) async {
      await expectFlowGoldens(
        tester,
        'r3_$state',
        () => RelationshipSpaceScreen(home: _states[state]!),
        overrides: [
          homeAuthProvider.overrideWithValue(FakeHomeAuth(testUser('u-r3'))),
          homeStateServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(
            HomeStateService(FakeHomeStateGateway()),
          ),
          invitePartnerServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(FakeInvites()),
          relationshipLifecycleProvider.overrideWith((ref) async => null),
        ],
      );
    });
  }
}
```

```dart
// Spec 1 W3b (§3.8 R3, §5.6 R17, review R-5): the legacy Chat-tab screen
// (what flow_paths_v2 off shows) never offers Dating, pending or not; the
// invite area and the Healing entry around the removed button stay.
import 'package:attune/core/utils/screen_util_config.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/chat/presentation/screens/chat_couples_locked_screen.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/relationships/presentation/relationship_lifecycle_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

Future<void> _pump(WidgetTester tester, {required bool pending}) async {
  await tester.pumpWidget(
    ProviderScope(
      overrides: [
        relationshipLifecycleProvider.overrideWith((ref) async => null),
      ],
      child: ScreenUtilConfig.builder(
        builder:
            (_) => MaterialApp(
              localizationsDelegates: AppLocalizations.localizationsDelegates,
              supportedLocales: AppLocalizations.supportedLocales,
              home: ChatCouplesLockedScreen(
                isPendingCouples: pending,
                onInviteSent: () {},
              ),
            ),
      ),
    ),
  );
  await tester.pumpAndSettle();
}

void main() {
  for (final pending in [false, true]) {
    testWidgets('pending: $pending: no way into Dating', (tester) async {
      await _pump(tester, pending: pending);
      await tester.scrollUntilVisible(
        find.text('Personal reflections'),
        300,
        scrollable: find.byType(Scrollable).first,
      );
      expect(
        find.text('Meet someone in Dating mode', skipOffstage: false),
        findsNothing,
      );
      expect(
        find.textContaining(
          RegExp('dating', caseSensitive: false),
          skipOffstage: false,
        ),
        findsNothing,
      );
    });
  }

  testWidgets('the invite area and Healing stay', (tester) async {
    await _pump(tester, pending: false);
    // No Supabase in a test: the invite button's place says so.
    expect(
      find.text(
        'Invites are unavailable right now. Please try again later.',
        skipOffstage: false,
      ),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    await tester.scrollUntilVisible(
      find.text('Healing from a breakup?'),
      300,
      scrollable: find.byType(Scrollable).first,
    );
    expect(find.text('Healing from a breakup?'), findsOneWidget);
  });
}
```

Create `lib/features/relationships/presentation/screens/relationship_space_screen.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:attune/app/theme/design_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/ui/text/allow_large_text.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_buttons.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_text_field.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/me/presentation/widgets/more_ways_sheet.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/me/presentation/widgets/your_space_section.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/home_state_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/path_chooser.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/screens/invite_partner_screen.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/widgets/invite_card.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/widgets/pending_invite_guide.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/relationships/data/relationship_invite_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/relationships/presentation/widgets/relationship_lifecycle_card.dart';
import 'package:attune/home/home_seams.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

/// R3 · Your space while you wait (Spec 1 §3.8): Tab 2 on the Relationship
/// path when not linked. Pending: the invite (Send again, Cancel this
/// invite), today's waiting guidance, a first note and a quiz. None: "Invite
/// your partner". Paused or inside the ending window: the lifecycle card.
/// After the window: one "More ways" row. Never anything about Dating, in
/// any state (§3.8, review R-5).
class RelationshipSpaceScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const RelationshipSpaceScreen({super.key, required this.home});

  final HomeSnapshot home;

  @override
  ConsumerState<RelationshipSpaceScreen> createState() =>
      _RelationshipSpaceScreenState();
}

class _RelationshipSpaceScreenState
    extends ConsumerState<RelationshipSpaceScreen> {
  RelationshipInvite? _invite;
  Duration? _age;
  bool _busy = false;
  String? _error;

  LinkState get _link => widget.home.link;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    if (_link == LinkState.pending) _loadInvite();
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant RelationshipSpaceScreen oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (oldWidget.home.link != _link) {
      _invite = null;
      _error = null;
      if (_link == LinkState.pending) _loadInvite();
    }
  }

  RelationshipInviteService get _invites =>
      ref.read(invitePartnerServiceProvider);

  Future<void> _refreshHome() async {
    final userId = ref.read(homeAuthProvider).currentUser?.id;
    if (userId == null) return;
    await ref.read(homeStateProvider(userId).notifier).refresh();
  }

  /// The live code: create-relationship-invite returns the open invite (or
  /// mints one); [forceNew] is day 7's new code.
  Future<void> _loadInvite({bool forceNew = false}) async {
    setState(() {
      _busy = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final invite = await _invites.createInvite(forceNew: forceNew);
      final age = await _invites.inviteAge();
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _invite = invite;
        _age = age;
      });
    } on RelationshipInviteException catch (error) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      if (error.alreadyActiveRelationship) {
        await _refreshHome();
        return;
      }
      setState(() => _error = error.message);
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _error = AppLocalizations.of(context)!.flow_saveFailed);
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _inviteNow() async {
    await _loadInvite();
    await _refreshHome();
  }

  /// "Cancel this invite" (§3.8): back to the path they came from, if any.
  Future<void> _cancelInvite() async {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final name = widget.home.linkDetail.partnerFirstName;
    final ok = await showFlowConfirm(
      context,
      title: loc.flow_cancelThisInviteTitle,
      body:
          name == null || name.isEmpty
              ? loc.flow_cancelInviteBody
              : loc.flow_cancelInviteBodyNamed(name),
      confirm: loc.flow_cancelThisInviteConfirm,
      cancel: loc.flow_cancelInviteKeep,
    );
    if (!ok || !mounted) return;
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      await ref
          .read(homeStateServiceProvider)
          .withdrawPendingInvites(returnToPreviousPath: true);
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _error = loc.flow_saveFailed);
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
    await _refreshHome();
  }

  /// Day 7's "Use Attune on my own for now" (Q7): the invites go, the path
  /// stays Relationship.
  Future<void> _onMyOwn() async {
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      await _invites.withdrawPendingInvites();
    } catch (_) {}
    if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    await _refreshHome();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final link = _link;
    final children = <Widget>[
      const SpaceHeader(),
      const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
      if (link == LinkState.paused || link == LinkState.endedWindow)
        const RelationshipLifecycleCard(),
      if (link == LinkState.pending) ...[
        _InviteSection(
          invite: _invite,
          busy: _busy,
          onCancel: _invite == null ? null : _cancelInvite,
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
        PendingInviteGuide(
          age: _age,
          busy: _busy,
          onReflect: () => context.pushNamed('reflectionJournal'),
          onKeepWaiting: () => _loadInvite(),
          onUseAlone: _onMyOwn,
          onInviteSomeoneElse: () => _loadInvite(forceNew: true),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
        _WhileYouWait(),
        const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
        Text(
          loc.flow_waitReceipts,
          style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
            color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
          ),
        ),
      ],
      if (link == LinkState.none) ...[
        FlowPrimaryButton(
          label: loc.flow_invitePartnerTitle,
          busy: _busy,
          onPressed: _inviteNow,
        ),
      ],
      if (_error != null) ...[
        const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
        FlowErrorLine(text: _error!, liveRegion: true),
      ],
      const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
      YourSpaceSection(
        extraRows: [
          // After the window only (§3.8; never inside it, 09 P15).
          if (link == LinkState.afterEnding) MoreWaysRow(home: widget.home),
        ],
      ),
      const SizedBox(height: Spacing.xxl * 2),
    ];
    return AllowLargeText(
      child: Scaffold(
        body: SafeArea(
          child: ListView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
            children: children,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _InviteSection extends StatelessWidget {
  const _InviteSection({
    required this.invite,
    required this.busy,
    required this.onCancel,
  });

  final RelationshipInvite? invite;
  final bool busy;
  final VoidCallback? onCancel;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final current = invite;
    return Card(
      margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
      elevation: 0,
      color: theme.colorScheme.surfaceContainerLow,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: Semantics(
                    header: true,
                    child: Text(
                      loc.flow_invitePartnerTitle,
                      style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                        color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                if (onCancel != null)
                  PopupMenuButton<void>(
                    tooltip: loc.flow_inviteMenu,
                    itemBuilder:
                        (_) => [
                          PopupMenuItem<void>(
                            onTap: onCancel,
                            child: Text(loc.flow_cancelThisInvite),
                          ),
                        ],
                  ),
              ],
            ),
            if (current != null)
              InviteCard(invite: current)
            else if (busy)
              const Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
                child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _WhileYouWait extends StatelessWidget {
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    return Card(
      margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
      elevation: 0,
      color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surfaceContainerLow,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
          horizontal: Spacing.md,
          vertical: Spacing.sm,
        ),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            SpaceRow(
              title: loc.flow_waitWriteNote,
              subtitle: '',
              icon: Icons.edit_note_outlined,
              onTap: () => context.pushNamed('reflectionJournal'),
            ),
            SpaceRow(
              title: loc.flow_waitQuiz,
              subtitle: '',
              icon: Icons.psychology_outlined,
              onTap: () => context.pushNamed('quizEntry', extra: 'attachment'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
import 'package:attune/features/chat/presentation/widgets/authenticated_chat_workspace.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/me/presentation/screens/me_home_screen.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/relationships/presentation/screens/relationship_space_screen.dart';
import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';

/// Tab 2 under flow_paths_v2 (Spec 1 §3.11): a linked couple gets the chat
/// (ChatHomeScreen, unchanged); the rest of the Relationship path gets its
/// space (R3, never Dating); Dating and Just for me get Me.
class HomeTabTwo extends StatelessWidget {
  const HomeTabTwo({
    super.key,
    required this.tab,
    required this.home,
    required this.onInviteSent,
  });

  final AppTab tab;
  final HomeSnapshot home;
  final VoidCallback onInviteSent;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (tab == AppTab.me) return MeHomeScreen(home: home);
    if (home.link == LinkState.linked) {
      return AuthenticatedChatWorkspace(
        isCouples: true,
        isPendingCouples: false,
        onInviteSent: onInviteSent,
      );
    }
    return RelationshipSpaceScreen(home: home);
  }
}
```

```dart
// Spec 1 W3b (§3.9, §3.12, D20): the Dating slot at the top of Me, Dating

// closed and gentle-wait states; the Dating lock; "Not ready?"; nothing

import 'package:attune/features/me/presentation/me_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/me/presentation/screens/me_home_screen.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/home_state_service.dart';

const _uid = 'u-me';

User _user(String phone) => User(
  id: _uid,
  appMetadata: const {},
  userMetadata: const {},
  aud: 'authenticated',
  createdAt: '2026-10-01T00:00:00Z',
  phone: phone,
);

class _Lock extends DatingLockStore {
  _Lock(this.on);
  bool on;
  @override
  Future<bool> isOn() async => on;
  @override
  Future<void> setOn(bool value) async => on = value;
}

List<Override> _overrides(
  FakeHomeStateGateway gateway, {
  String phone = '+233244123456',
  bool locked = false,
  ({bool? hasNote, bool? hasQuiz}) first = (hasNote: true, hasQuiz: true),
}) => [
  homeAuthProvider.overrideWithValue(FakeHomeAuth(_user(phone))),
  homeStateServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(HomeStateService(gateway)),
  relationshipLifecycleProvider.overrideWith((ref) async => null),
  datingLockStoreProvider.overrideWithValue(_Lock(locked)),
  firstActionInputsProvider.overrideWithValue(first),
];

Future<(FlowHarness, FakeHomeStateGateway)> _pump(
  WidgetTester tester,
  Map<String, dynamic> json, {
  String phone = '+233244123456',
  bool locked = false,
  List<String> open = const ['GB', 'GH'],
  ({bool? hasNote, bool? hasQuiz}) first = (hasNote: true, hasQuiz: true),
  Map<String, Object> prefsValues = const {},
}) async {
  // A fresh ProviderScope each time (pumpWidget would reuse the last one).
  await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox());
  final gateway = FakeHomeStateGateway(state: json);
  final h = await pumpFlowScreen(
    tester,
    () => MeHomeScreen(home: snapshotOf(json)),
    openCountries: open,
    prefsValues: prefsValues,
    overrides: _overrides(gateway, phone: phone, locked: locked, first: first),
  );
  return (h, gateway);
}
```

Create `test/features/me/dating_lock_wipe_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W3b (§5.8, D19): the Dating lock is a protective, sign-out-only
// wipe: sign-out clears it, quick exit keeps it, the Safety PIN stays.
import 'package:attune/app/device_wipes.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/device/device_wipe_registry.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/safety/data/dating_lock_store.dart';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

void main() {
  test('the lock entry is protective and sign-out only', () {
    final registry = DeviceWipeRegistry();
    registerDeviceWipes(registry);
    final lock = registry.entries.firstWhere(
      (e) => e.id == 'spec1.dating_lock',
    );
    expect(lock.protective, isTrue);
    expect(lock.moments, {WipeMoment.signOut});
    expect(lock.keys, {'me_section_lock'});
  });

  test('sign-out clears the lock; the Safety PIN stays', () async {
    SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues({});
    FlutterSecureStorage.setMockInitialValues({
      'me_section_lock': 'on',
      'safety_pin_hash': 'kept',
    });
    final registry = DeviceWipeRegistry();
    registerDeviceWipes(registry);
    await registry.run(WipeMoment.signOut);
    expect(await DatingLockStore().isOn(), isFalse);
    expect(
      await const FlutterSecureStorage().read(key: 'safety_pin_hash'),
      'kept',
    );
  });

  test('quick exit keeps the Dating lock (D19)', () async {
    SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues({});
    FlutterSecureStorage.setMockInitialValues({'me_section_lock': 'on'});
    final registry = DeviceWipeRegistry();
    registerDeviceWipes(registry);
    await registry.run(WipeMoment.quickExit);
    expect(await DatingLockStore().isOn(), isTrue);
  });
}
```

```dart
// Spec 1 W3b (§3.9): Settings -> Safety -> "Dating lock" shows only for the
// Dating path or an enrollment (from the live home state); turning it on
// with no Safety PIN opens the PIN setup first and stays off without one.
import 'package:attune/core/providers/shared_prefs_provider.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/me/presentation/widgets/dating_lock_setting.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/flow_flags.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/safety/data/dating_lock_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/settings/models/settings_config.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

import '../../helpers/fake_home_state.dart';

Future<(ProviderContainer, GoRouter, List<SettingsConfig?>)> _pump(
  WidgetTester tester,
  Map<String, dynamic> json,
) async {
  SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues({});
  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  final rows = <SettingsConfig?>[];
  final container = ProviderContainer(
    overrides: [
      sharedPreferencesProvider.overrideWithValue(prefs),
      flowPathsV2Provider.overrideWithValue(true),
    ],
  );
  addTearDown(container.dispose);
  await container
      .read(homeStateProvider('u1').notifier)
      .apply(HomeState.fromJson(json));
  final router = GoRouter(
    routes: [
      GoRoute(
        path: '/',
        builder:
            (_, __) => Consumer(
              builder: (context, ref, _) {
                final row = datingLockSetting(context, ref, 'u1');
                rows.add(row);
                return row == null
                    ? const Text('NO ROW')
                    : TextButton(
                      onPressed: () => row.onToggle!(true),
                      child: Text(row.title),
                    );
              },
            ),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/settings/safety-pin',
        builder: (_, __) => const Text('PIN SETUP'),
      ),
    ],
  );
  addTearDown(router.dispose);
  await tester.pumpWidget(
    UncontrolledProviderScope(
      container: container,
      child: MaterialApp.router(
        localizationsDelegates: AppLocalizations.localizationsDelegates,
        supportedLocales: AppLocalizations.supportedLocales,
        routerConfig: router,
      ),
    ),
  );
  await tester.pumpAndSettle();
  return (container, router, rows);
}

void main() {
  testWidgets('hidden on Just for me; shown on Dating and while enrolled', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    await _pump(tester, homeJson(path: 'just_me'));
    expect(find.text('NO ROW'), findsOneWidget);
    await _pump(tester, homeJson(path: 'dating'));
    expect(find.text('Dating lock'), findsOneWidget);
    await _pump(tester, homeJson(path: 'relationship', datingEnrolled: true));
    expect(find.text('Dating lock'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('on with no PIN: the PIN setup first, and still off', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    FlutterSecureStorage.setMockInitialValues({});
    final (container, router, _) = await _pump(
      tester,
      homeJson(path: 'dating'),
    );
    await tester.tap(find.text('Dating lock'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('PIN SETUP'), findsOneWidget);
    router.pop();
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(await DatingLockStore().isOn(), isFalse);
  });

  testWidgets('on with a PIN: on, under the neutral key', (tester) async {
    FlutterSecureStorage.setMockInitialValues({'safety_pin_hash': r'v1$a$b$c'});
    await _pump(tester, homeJson(path: 'dating'));
    await tester.tap(find.text('Dating lock'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(
      await const FlutterSecureStorage().read(key: 'me_section_lock'),
      'on',
    );
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The Dating lock's store (the Safety PIN's storage, a neutral key)**

Create `lib/features/safety/data/dating_lock_store.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:attune/features/safety/data/safety_pin_store.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';

/// The Dating lock (Spec 1 §3.9, Q6): "Ask for your Safety PIN before
/// showing Dating." A device setting in the safety keystore (this phone
/// only, excluded from backups), under the neutral key `me_section_lock`
/// (D20: no "dating" in a device key or value). Protective: quick exit
/// never clears it; sign-out does (D19, §5.8 `spec1.dating_lock`).
class DatingLockStore {
  DatingLockStore({FlutterSecureStorage? storage})
    : _storage = storage ?? SafetyPinStore.defaultStorage;

  final FlutterSecureStorage _storage;

  static const key = 'me_section_lock';

  Future<bool> isOn() async => (await _storage.read(key: key)) == 'on';

  Future<void> setOn(bool on) =>
      on ? _storage.write(key: key, value: 'on') : _storage.delete(key: key);

  Future<void> clear() => _storage.delete(key: key);
}

final datingLockStoreProvider = Provider<DatingLockStore>(
  (ref) => DatingLockStore(),
);

/// Whether the Dating lock is on, on this phone.
class DatingLockNotifier extends AsyncNotifier<bool> {
  @override
  Future<bool> build() => ref.read(datingLockStoreProvider).isOn();

  Future<void> set(bool on) async {
    await ref.read(datingLockStoreProvider).setOn(on);
    state = AsyncData(on);
  }
}

final datingLockProvider = AsyncNotifierProvider<DatingLockNotifier, bool>(
  DatingLockNotifier.new,
);
```
