```dart
/// The Dating slot at the top of Me, Dating path only (Spec 1 §3.9).
/// It calls no Dating RPC (review I-4): it reads the home state's
/// `dating {enabled, enrolled}` and `link`, and the D15 open-country list.
/// Spec 2 W3 replaces it with DatingSection. While the Dating lock is on it
/// is one row until the Safety PIN is entered.
class DatingSlot extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const DatingSlot({super.key, required this.home});

  final HomeSnapshot home;

  @override
  ConsumerState<DatingSlot> createState() => _DatingSlotState();
}

class _DatingSlotState extends ConsumerState<DatingSlot> {
  /// Unlocked for this visit only; never stored.
  bool _unlocked = false;

  String? get _userId => ref.read(homeAuthProvider).currentUser?.id;

  String? _country() {
    final userId = _userId;
    if (userId == null) return null;
    final override =
        MeDeviceStore(
          ref.read(sharedPreferencesProvider),
          userId: userId,
        ).countryOverride;
    if (override != null) return override;
    final phone = ref.read(homeAuthProvider).currentUser?.phone;
    if (phone == null || phone.isEmpty) return null;
    return countryOfPhone(phone.startsWith('+') ? phone : '+$phone');
  }

  Future<void> _pickCountry(String? current) async {
    final userId = _userId;
    if (userId == null) return;
    final selected =
        current == null
            ? PhoneCountries.defaultCountry
            : PhoneCountries.all.firstWhere(
              (c) => c.isoCode == current,
              orElse: () => PhoneCountries.defaultCountry,
            );
    await BottomSheetUtils.showDocumentationBottomSheet<void>(
      context: context,
      padding: 0,
      widget: PhoneCountryPickerSheet(
        selectedCountry: selected,
        onCountrySelected: (country) async {
          await MeDeviceStore(
            ref.read(sharedPreferencesProvider),
            userId: userId,
          ).setCountryOverride(country.isoCode);
          if (mounted) setState(() {});
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _unlock() async {
    if (await confirmSafetyPin(context)) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _unlocked = true);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final locked = ref.watch(datingLockProvider);
    // Unknown yet: show nothing rather than a flash of Dating.
    if (!locked.hasValue) return const SizedBox.shrink();
    if (locked.value! && !_unlocked) {
      return SpaceRow(
        title: loc.flow_slotLocked,
        subtitle: loc.flow_slotLockedLine,
        icon: Icons.lock_outline,
        onTap: _unlock,
      );
    }

    final home = widget.home;
    final windowEnds = home.linkDetail.windowEndsAt;
    if (home.link == LinkState.endedWindow && windowEnds != null) {
      return _SlotCard(
        title: loc.flow_slotGentleWait(
          MyDateFormat.toDate(windowEnds.toLocal()),
        ),
        lines: [loc.flow_slotGentleWaitWhy],
        actions: [
          FlowTextLink(
            label: loc.flow_slotHealing,
            alignment: AlignmentDirectional.centerStart,
            onPressed: () => openHealing(context, ref),
          ),
        ],
      );
    }

    final open = ref.watch(datingOpenCountriesProvider);
    final enabled = home.dating?.enabled ?? false;
    final notReady = FlowTextLink(
      label: loc.flow_slotNotReady,
      alignment: AlignmentDirectional.centerStart,
      onPressed:
          () => switchPath(
            context,
            ref,
            to: AppPath.justMe,
            oneTap: true,
            home: widget.home,
          ),
    );
    if (!enabled || open.isEmpty) {
      return _SlotCard(
        title: loc.flow_slotClosedTitle,
        lines: [loc.flow_slotClosedBody],
        actions: [notReady],
      );
    }

    String name(String iso) => countryNameFor(
      iso,
      special: {'GB': loc.flow_countryUk, 'US': loc.flow_countryUs},
    );
    final openNames = formatCountryList(
      open,
      nameOf: name,
      pair: loc.flow_listTwo,
    );
    final country = _country();
    if (country == null) {
      return _SlotCard(
        title: loc.flow_slotDatingTitle,
        lines: [loc.flow_slotNotOpenBody(openNames)],
        actions: [
          FlowTextLink(
            label: loc.flow_slotChooseCountry,
            alignment: AlignmentDirectional.centerStart,
            onPressed: () => _pickCountry(null),
          ),
          notReady,
        ],
      );
    }
    if (!open.contains(country)) {
      return _SlotCard(
        title: loc.flow_slotNotOpenTitle(name(country)),
        lines: [loc.flow_slotNotOpenBody(openNames)],
        actions: [
          FlowTextLink(
            label: loc.flow_slotChangeCountry(name(country)),
            alignment: AlignmentDirectional.centerStart,
            onPressed: () => _pickCountry(country),
          ),
          notReady,
        ],
      );
    }
    return _SlotCard(
      title: loc.flow_slotDatingTitle,
      lines: [loc.flow_slotDatingBody],
      actions: [
        FlowPrimaryButton(
          label: loc.flow_slotSetUp,
          onPressed: () => context.push(RouteNames.datingMode),
        ),
        notReady,
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _SlotCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SlotCard({
    required this.title,
    required this.lines,
    required this.actions,
  });

  final String title;
  final List<String> lines;
  final List<Widget> actions;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: Spacing.md),
      child: Card(
        margin: EdgeInsets.zero,
        elevation: 0,
        color: theme.colorScheme.surfaceContainerLow,
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(Spacing.md),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              Semantics(
                header: true,
                child: Text(
                  title,
                  style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                    color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              for (final line in lines) ...[
                const SizedBox(height: Spacing.xs),
                Text(
                  line,
                  style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                    color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
              const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
              ...actions,
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
import 'package:attune/app/routing/app_router.dart' show RouteNames;
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/flow_flags.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/safety/data/dating_lock_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/safety/data/safety_pin_store.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/safety/presentation/widgets/confirm_safety_pin.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/settings/models/settings_config.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

/// Settings -> Safety -> "Dating lock" (Spec 1 §3.9): shown under
/// flow_paths_v2 when the live home state says the path is Dating or
/// something is enrolled (never from the phone; review A-10). Null: hidden.
SettingsConfig? datingLockSetting(
  BuildContext context,
  WidgetRef ref,
  String userId,
) {
  if (userId.isEmpty || !ref.watch(flowPathsV2Provider)) return null;
  final home = ref.watch(homeStateProvider(userId));
  final show = home.path == AppPath.dating || (home.dating?.enrolled ?? false);
  if (!show) return null;
  final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
  return SettingsConfig(
    id: 'dating_lock',
    rowKey: const ValueKey('dating_lock_switch'),
    title: loc.flow_lockTitle,
    subtitle: loc.flow_lockSubtitle,
    icon: Icons.lock_outline,
    type: SettingsItemType.toggle,
    value: ref.watch(datingLockProvider).valueOrNull ?? false,
    onToggle: (on) => setDatingLock(context, ref, on),
    iconColor: Colors.red,
  );
}

/// On: with no Safety PIN, its setup comes first, and the lock is set only
/// once a PIN exists. Off: the PIN is asked first (true when none is set),
/// so someone holding the phone can't turn it off.
Future<void> setDatingLock(
  BuildContext context,
  WidgetRef ref,
  bool on, {
  SafetyPinStore? pins,
}) async {
  final store = pins ?? SafetyPinStore.instance;
  if (on) {
    if (!await store.hasPin()) {
      if (!context.mounted) return;
      await context.push(RouteNames.safetyPin);
      if (!await store.hasPin()) return;
    }
  } else {
    if (!context.mounted) return;
    if (!await confirmSafetyPin(context, store: store)) return;
  }
  await ref.read(datingLockProvider.notifier).set(on);
}
```

```dart
/// The Me home (Spec 1 §3.10): Tab 2 on Dating and Just for me. "Your
/// space": the lifecycle card while an ending's window is open (§3.12), the

}
```

Replace the whole of `lib/app/device_wipes.dart` (a file this plan created) with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:attune/core/device/device_wipe_registry.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/safety/data/dating_lock_store.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

/// Every feature's device wipes, registered once at boot (main.dart; spec
/// §5.8). Spec 1's entries are here; another feature adds one line calling
/// its own register function (Spec 2: `registerDatingDeviceWipes(registry)`).
void registerDeviceWipes([DeviceWipeRegistry? registry]) {
  final target = registry ?? DeviceWipeRegistry.instance;
  for (final entry in spec1DeviceWipes()) {
    target.register(entry);
  }
}

/// Removes every prefs key [matches] accepts (all accounts on the phone:
/// the next person to sign in here inherits none of them).
Future<void> removePrefsKeys(bool Function(String key) matches) async {
  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  for (final key in prefs.getKeys().where(matches).toList()) {
    await prefs.remove(key);
  }
}

/// `attune.home.<uid>.state` (HomeStateStore.keyFor).
bool isHomeStateKey(String key) =>
    key.startsWith('attune.home.') && key.endsWith('.state');

/// Spec 1's entries (spec §5.8 table). W4b adds the partner name and Ask 2
/// progress.
List<DeviceWipeEntry> spec1DeviceWipes() => [
  DeviceWipeEntry(
    id: 'spec1.home_state_cache',
    moments: const {WipeMoment.signOut},
    keys: const {'attune.home.<uid>.state'},
    run: () => removePrefsKeys(isHomeStateKey),
  ),
  // W3b: the Dating lock is protective: sign-out only, never quick exit
  // (D19).
  DeviceWipeEntry(
    id: 'spec1.dating_lock',
    moments: const {WipeMoment.signOut},
    protective: true,
    keys: const {DatingLockStore.key},
    run: () => DatingLockStore().clear(),
  ),
  // W3b: the Me home's country and J1 progress.
  DeviceWipeEntry(
    id: 'spec1.me_device',
    moments: const {WipeMoment.signOut},
    keys: const {
      'attune.flow.<uid>.country_override',
      'attune.flow.<uid>.first_action',
    },
    run:
        () => removePrefsKeys(
          (key) =>
              key.startsWith('attune.flow.') &&
              (key.endsWith('.country_override') ||
                  key.endsWith('.first_action')),
        ),
  ),
];
```

**Files:**

- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261026100010_journal_reminders_grants.sql`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `public.users` (`timezone`), `public.notification_recipient_tz(uuid)`, `public.scheduled_notifications` (`user_id`, `notification_type`, `scheduled_for`, `status`, `metadata`, `source_key`, `delivery_channel`, `last_error`), the account-hold table W1 reads for `hold`, pg_cron.
- Produces: table `public.journal_reminder_settings (user_id uuid PK → auth.users ON DELETE CASCADE, enabled boolean, iso_weekday smallint 1–7, local_time time 08:00–<22:00, last_enqueued_for date, updated_at timestamptz)` with RLS (own row SELECT); `public.set_my_journal_reminder(p_enabled boolean, p_iso_weekday smallint, p_local_time time) → jsonb` (refuses `journal_reminder:not_signed_in`, `journal_reminder:no_account`, `journal_reminder:invalid`, `journal_reminder:quiet_hours`); `public.get_my_journal_reminder() → jsonb` (`{enabled, iso_weekday, local_time}`, `{enabled: false}` with no row); `public.enqueue_journal_reminders(p_now timestamptz DEFAULT now()) → integer` (service role and cron only); cron job `enqueue-journal-reminders` at `*/15 * * * *`; push metadata `{type: 'journal_reminder', title: 'Attune', body: "A few quiet minutes for you, if you'd like."}`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing contract**

Create `supabase/tests/journal_reminder_contracts.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- supabase/tests/journal_reminder_contracts.sql
-- Spec 1 W3c (§3.10 J3, §4.2, §4.3 W3c, §4.6, §7.1): the weekly journal
-- reminder. Own row only (read through RLS, written through the RPC);
-- 08:00-21:59 local only; one neutral push per local day at the chosen
-- minute in the person's own zone (never guessed); a change cancels what
-- was queued; no client runs the enqueue. One BEGIN ... ROLLBACK.
-- Run only on attune_spec3a:
--   psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f supabase/tests/journal_reminder_contracts.sql
-- Fixture ids 3e3c0000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000NN; phones +15558800NN, set
-- on auth.users and public.users. Every refusal is checked by its message.
BEGIN;

CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.as_user(p uuid) RETURNS void LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
  EXECUTE 'SET LOCAL ROLE authenticated';
  PERFORM set_config('request.jwt.claim.sub', coalesce(p::text, ''), true);
  PERFORM set_config('request.jwt.claims',
    json_build_object('sub', p, 'role', 'authenticated')::text, true);
END $$;

CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.error_of(p_sql text) RETURNS text LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
  EXECUTE p_sql;
  RETURN NULL;
EXCEPTION WHEN raise_exception OR insufficient_privilege OR check_violation THEN
  RETURN SQLERRM;
END $$;

CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.id(p_n text) RETURNS uuid LANGUAGE sql AS $$
  SELECT ('3e3c0000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000' || p_n)::uuid;
$$;

-- A person in [p_zone] (NULL: unknown) with a reminder, its settings
-- dated well before the test's clock.
CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.person(p_n text, p_zone text, p_weekday int, p_time time) RETURNS void
LANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO auth.users (id, phone, phone_confirmed_at)
  VALUES (pg_temp.id(p_n), '+155588000' || p_n, now());
  INSERT INTO public.users (id, phone, display_name, timezone)
  VALUES (pg_temp.id(p_n), '+155588000' || p_n, 'Ama ' || p_n, p_zone);
  IF p_weekday IS NOT NULL THEN
    INSERT INTO public.journal_reminder_settings (user_id, enabled, iso_weekday, local_time, updated_at)
    VALUES (pg_temp.id(p_n), true, p_weekday, p_time, TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-10-01 00:00Z');
  END IF;
END $$;

CREATE FUNCTION pg_temp.queued(p_n text) RETURNS int LANGUAGE sql AS $$
  SELECT count(*)::int FROM public.scheduled_notifications
   WHERE user_id = pg_temp.id(p_n) AND metadata->>'type' = 'journal_reminder'
     AND status = 'pending';
$$;

-- =====================================================================
-- A. Objects and grants.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
BEGIN
  IF to_regclass('public.journal_reminder_settings') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('public.set_my_journal_reminder(boolean,smallint,time)') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('public.get_my_journal_reminder()') IS NULL
     OR to_regprocedure('public.enqueue_journal_reminders(timestamptz)') IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: W3c objects are missing (journal_reminder_settings, set/get_my_journal_reminder, enqueue_journal_reminders)';
  END IF;
  IF NOT (SELECT relrowsecurity FROM pg_class WHERE oid = 'public.journal_reminder_settings'::regclass) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: journal_reminder_settings has no RLS';
  END IF;
  IF NOT has_table_privilege('authenticated', 'public.journal_reminder_settings', 'SELECT')
     OR has_table_privilege('authenticated', 'public.journal_reminder_settings', 'INSERT')
     OR has_table_privilege('authenticated', 'public.journal_reminder_settings', 'UPDATE')
     OR has_table_privilege('authenticated', 'public.journal_reminder_settings', 'DELETE')
     OR has_table_privilege('anon', 'public.journal_reminder_settings', 'SELECT') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: client table grants are wrong (authenticated SELECT only, anon nothing)';
  END IF;
  IF NOT has_table_privilege('service_role', 'public.journal_reminder_settings', 'SELECT')
     OR NOT has_table_privilege('service_role', 'public.journal_reminder_settings', 'UPDATE') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: service_role lost DML on journal_reminder_settings';
  END IF;
  IF NOT has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.set_my_journal_reminder(boolean,smallint,time)', 'EXECUTE')
     OR NOT has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.get_my_journal_reminder()', 'EXECUTE')
     OR has_function_privilege('anon', 'public.set_my_journal_reminder(boolean,smallint,time)', 'EXECUTE')
     OR has_function_privilege('anon', 'public.get_my_journal_reminder()', 'EXECUTE')
     OR has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.enqueue_journal_reminders(timestamptz)', 'EXECUTE')
     OR has_function_privilege('anon', 'public.enqueue_journal_reminders(timestamptz)', 'EXECUTE') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: reminder function grants are wrong';
  END IF;
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM cron.job
                  WHERE jobname = 'enqueue-journal-reminders' AND schedule = '*/15 * * * *') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: enqueue-journal-reminders is not scheduled every 15 minutes';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- B. Own row only; written through the RPC; checked values.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  v_msg text;
  v_n int;
  v jsonb;
BEGIN
  PERFORM pg_temp.person('01', 'Africa/Accra', NULL, NULL);
  PERFORM pg_temp.person('02', 'Africa/Accra', NULL, NULL);

  PERFORM pg_temp.as_user(pg_temp.id('01'));
  PERFORM public.set_my_journal_reminder(true, 1::smallint, time '20:00');
  v := public.get_my_journal_reminder();
  RESET ROLE;
  IF v <> '{"enabled": true, "iso_weekday": 1, "local_time": "20:00"}'::jsonb THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: get_my_journal_reminder read back %', v;
  END IF;

  -- Someone else sees nothing of it.
  PERFORM pg_temp.as_user(pg_temp.id('02'));
  SELECT count(*) INTO v_n FROM public.journal_reminder_settings;
  v := public.get_my_journal_reminder();
  RESET ROLE;
  IF v_n <> 0 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: another person can read % reminder rows', v_n;
  END IF;
  IF v <> '{"enabled": false, "iso_weekday": null, "local_time": null}'::jsonb THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: no row did not read as off: %', v;
  END IF;

  -- No direct writes.
  PERFORM pg_temp.as_user(pg_temp.id('02'));
  v_msg := pg_temp.error_of(format(
    'INSERT INTO public.journal_reminder_settings (user_id, enabled) VALUES (%L, false)', pg_temp.id('02')));
  RESET ROLE;
  IF v_msg IS DISTINCT FROM 'permission denied for table journal_reminder_settings' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a client wrote its row directly (got %)', coalesce(v_msg, 'no error');
  END IF;

  -- Refusals.
  PERFORM pg_temp.as_user(pg_temp.id('02'));
  v_msg := pg_temp.error_of('SELECT public.set_my_journal_reminder(true, NULL, time ''20:00'')');
  IF v_msg IS DISTINCT FROM 'journal_reminder:invalid' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: on without a day (got %)', coalesce(v_msg, 'no error');
  END IF;
  v_msg := pg_temp.error_of('SELECT public.set_my_journal_reminder(true, 8::smallint, time ''20:00'')');
  IF v_msg IS DISTINCT FROM 'journal_reminder:invalid' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: weekday 8 (got %)', coalesce(v_msg, 'no error');
  END IF;
  v_msg := pg_temp.error_of('SELECT public.set_my_journal_reminder(true, 3::smallint, time ''22:30'')');
  IF v_msg IS DISTINCT FROM 'journal_reminder:quiet_hours' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: 22:30 was accepted (got %)', coalesce(v_msg, 'no error');
  END IF;
  v_msg := pg_temp.error_of('SELECT public.set_my_journal_reminder(true, 3::smallint, time ''07:59'')');
  IF v_msg IS DISTINCT FROM 'journal_reminder:quiet_hours' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: 07:59 was accepted (got %)', coalesce(v_msg, 'no error');
  END IF;
  v_msg := pg_temp.error_of('SELECT public.set_my_journal_reminder(true, 3::smallint, time ''08:00'')');
  RESET ROLE;
  IF v_msg IS NOT NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: 08:00 was refused (%)', v_msg;
  END IF;

  PERFORM pg_temp.as_user(NULL);
  v_msg := pg_temp.error_of('SELECT public.set_my_journal_reminder(false, NULL, NULL)');
  RESET ROLE;
  IF v_msg IS DISTINCT FROM 'journal_reminder:not_signed_in' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: no session (got %)', coalesce(v_msg, 'no error');
  END IF;

  -- Off keeps the day and time.
  PERFORM pg_temp.as_user(pg_temp.id('01'));
  PERFORM public.set_my_journal_reminder(false, NULL, NULL);
  v := public.get_my_journal_reminder();
  RESET ROLE;
  IF v <> '{"enabled": false, "iso_weekday": 1, "local_time": "20:00"}'::jsonb THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: turning off forgot the day and time: %', v;
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- C. Enqueue: the local minute, once per local day, neutral.
--    Monday 2026-11-02. Accra is UTC; Chicago is UTC-6 (CST from 1 Nov).
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
DECLARE
  r record;
BEGIN
  PERFORM pg_temp.person('11', 'Africa/Accra', 1, time '20:00');
  PERFORM pg_temp.person('12', 'America/Chicago', 1, time '20:00');
  PERFORM pg_temp.person('13', NULL, 1, time '20:00');
  PERFORM pg_temp.person('14', 'Africa/Accra', 2, time '20:00');
  PERFORM pg_temp.person('15', 'Africa/Accra', 1, time '20:00');
  UPDATE public.journal_reminder_settings SET enabled = false WHERE user_id = pg_temp.id('15');
  PERFORM pg_temp.person('16', 'Africa/Accra', 1, time '20:00');
  INSERT INTO public.account_holds (user_id, kind) VALUES (pg_temp.id('16'), 'age_review');

  -- 19:44Z: Accra's 20:00 is 16 minutes away: not yet.
  PERFORM public.enqueue_journal_reminders(TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-11-02 19:44Z');
  IF pg_temp.queued('11') <> 0 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: queued more than 15 minutes early';
  END IF;
  -- 19:46Z: queued for 20:00 itself; the runs after add nothing.
  PERFORM public.enqueue_journal_reminders(TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-11-02 19:46Z');
  PERFORM public.enqueue_journal_reminders(TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-11-02 20:00Z');
  PERFORM public.enqueue_journal_reminders(TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-11-02 20:14Z');
  IF pg_temp.queued('11') <> 1 OR NOT EXISTS (
       SELECT 1 FROM public.scheduled_notifications
        WHERE user_id = pg_temp.id('11') AND scheduled_for = TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-11-02 20:00Z'
          AND source_key = 'journal_reminder:' || pg_temp.id('11') || ':2026-11-02') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: Accra got % reminders, or not for 20:00 Monday', pg_temp.queued('11');
  END IF;
  IF (SELECT last_enqueued_for FROM public.journal_reminder_settings WHERE user_id = pg_temp.id('11'))
     IS DISTINCT FROM DATE '2026-11-02' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: last_enqueued_for was not the local Monday';
  END IF;
  -- Not Chicago (14:00 there), not Tuesday's, not off, not held, never a
  -- guessed zone.
  IF pg_temp.queued('12') + pg_temp.queued('13') + pg_temp.queued('14')
     + pg_temp.queued('15') + pg_temp.queued('16') <> 0 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: someone else was reminded at 20:00Z';
  END IF;

  -- Chicago's Monday 20:00 is Tuesday 02:00Z; its key is the local Monday.
  PERFORM public.enqueue_journal_reminders(TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-11-03 01:50Z');
  IF pg_temp.queued('12') <> 1 OR NOT EXISTS (
       SELECT 1 FROM public.scheduled_notifications
        WHERE user_id = pg_temp.id('12') AND scheduled_for = TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-11-03 02:00Z'
          AND source_key = 'journal_reminder:' || pg_temp.id('12') || ':2026-11-02') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: Chicago''s reminder is missing or keyed to the UTC day';
  END IF;
  IF pg_temp.queued('13') <> 0 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: an unknown zone was guessed';
  END IF;

  -- The copy names nobody and is never moved by quiet hours.
  FOR r IN SELECT * FROM public.scheduled_notifications
            WHERE metadata->>'type' = 'journal_reminder' AND user_id::text LIKE '3e3c0000-%' LOOP
    IF r.metadata <> '{"type": "journal_reminder", "title": "Attune", "body": "A few quiet minutes for you, if you''d like."}'::jsonb THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the reminder is not the neutral copy: %', r.metadata;
    END IF;
    IF r.notification_type <> 'immediate' OR r.delivery_channel <> 'push' THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: not an immediate push: % %', r.notification_type, r.delivery_channel;
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- D. A change cancels what was queued, and the new time can still go out
--    today; a time set after today's minute waits a week.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
BEGIN
  PERFORM pg_temp.person('21', 'Africa/Accra', 1, time '20:00');
  PERFORM public.enqueue_journal_reminders(TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-11-02 19:50Z');
  IF pg_temp.queued('21') <> 1 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the first reminder was not queued';
  END IF;
  PERFORM pg_temp.as_user(pg_temp.id('21'));
  PERFORM public.set_my_journal_reminder(true, 1::smallint, time '21:00');
  RESET ROLE;
  IF pg_temp.queued('21') <> 0 OR NOT EXISTS (
       SELECT 1 FROM public.scheduled_notifications
        WHERE user_id = pg_temp.id('21') AND status = 'cancelled' AND source_key IS NULL
          AND last_error = 'journal_reminder_changed') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the old reminder was not cancelled and its key freed';
  END IF;
  -- The change is "now" (the real clock): date it before 21:00 Monday so
  -- the test's Monday reads as after it.
  UPDATE public.journal_reminder_settings SET updated_at = TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-11-02 19:55Z'
   WHERE user_id = pg_temp.id('21');
  PERFORM public.enqueue_journal_reminders(TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-11-02 20:50Z');
  IF pg_temp.queued('21') <> 1 OR NOT EXISTS (
       SELECT 1 FROM public.scheduled_notifications
        WHERE user_id = pg_temp.id('21') AND status = 'pending'
          AND scheduled_for = TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-11-02 21:00Z') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: the changed reminder did not go out at 21:00';
  END IF;

  -- Set at 20:30 for 20:00 the same Monday: next week, not now.
  PERFORM pg_temp.person('22', 'Africa/Accra', 1, time '20:00');
  UPDATE public.journal_reminder_settings SET updated_at = TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-11-02 20:05Z'
   WHERE user_id = pg_temp.id('22');
  PERFORM public.enqueue_journal_reminders(TIMESTAMPTZ '2026-11-02 20:10Z');
  IF pg_temp.queued('22') <> 0 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a reminder set after its time fired at once';
  END IF;
END $$;

-- =====================================================================
-- E. The account goes, the reminder goes.
-- =====================================================================
DO $$
BEGIN
  DELETE FROM public.users WHERE id = pg_temp.id('22');
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.journal_reminder_settings WHERE user_id = pg_temp.id('22')) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'CONTRACT VIOLATED: a deleted account kept its reminder';
  END IF;
END $$;

DO $$ BEGIN RAISE NOTICE 'journal_reminder_contracts: all held'; END $$;
ROLLBACK;
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the migration**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261026100000_journal_reminders.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- Spec 1 W3c, migration 8 (spec §4.7 #8): journal_reminders.
--   * journal_reminder_settings: one weekly reminder per person (J3), read
--     by its owner only; written only through set_my_journal_reminder.
--   * enqueue_journal_reminders (cron every 15 minutes): one neutral push
--     per reminder per local day, at the chosen minute in the person's own
--     zone (notification_recipient_tz; never guessed). The time is kept
--     between 08:00 and 21:59 local, so the quiet-hours guard (22:00-08:00)
--     never moves a reminder to another day (W3 plan P-21).
-- Grants in 20261026100010_journal_reminders_grants.sql; the server-only
-- function is revoked here as well.
BEGIN;
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '5s';

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.journal_reminder_settings (
  user_id uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES public.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  enabled boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  iso_weekday smallint
    CONSTRAINT journal_reminder_settings_weekday_check CHECK (iso_weekday BETWEEN 1 AND 7),
  local_time time
    CONSTRAINT journal_reminder_settings_time_check
      CHECK (local_time >= time '08:00' AND local_time < time '22:00'),
  last_enqueued_for date,
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  CONSTRAINT journal_reminder_settings_enabled_check
    CHECK (NOT enabled OR (iso_weekday IS NOT NULL AND local_time IS NOT NULL))
);
ALTER TABLE public.journal_reminder_settings ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS journal_reminder_settings_select_own ON public.journal_reminder_settings;
CREATE POLICY journal_reminder_settings_select_own ON public.journal_reminder_settings
  FOR SELECT TO authenticated
  USING (user_id = (SELECT auth.uid()));

-- J3's sheet. Turning it off keeps the day and time, so turning it on
-- again starts from them. A reminder already queued for the old setting is
-- cancelled (and its key freed), so the new one can go out today.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.set_my_journal_reminder(
  p_enabled boolean,
  p_iso_weekday smallint,
  p_local_time time
)
RETURNS void
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_uid constant uuid := auth.uid();
  v_cancelled integer;
BEGIN
  IF v_uid IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'journal_reminder:not_signed_in';
  END IF;
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.users AS u WHERE u.id = v_uid) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'journal_reminder:no_account';
  END IF;
  IF p_enabled IS NULL
     OR (p_enabled AND (p_iso_weekday IS NULL OR p_local_time IS NULL))
     OR (p_iso_weekday IS NOT NULL AND p_iso_weekday NOT BETWEEN 1 AND 7) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'journal_reminder:invalid';
  END IF;
  IF p_local_time IS NOT NULL
     AND (p_local_time < time '08:00' OR p_local_time >= time '22:00') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'journal_reminder:quiet_hours';
  END IF;

  UPDATE public.scheduled_notifications AS n
     SET status = 'cancelled',
         source_key = NULL,
         last_error = 'journal_reminder_changed',
         updated_at = now()
   WHERE n.user_id = v_uid
     AND n.status = 'pending'
     AND n.source_key LIKE 'journal_reminder:' || v_uid::text || ':%';
  GET DIAGNOSTICS v_cancelled = ROW_COUNT;

  INSERT INTO public.journal_reminder_settings AS s
    (user_id, enabled, iso_weekday, local_time, updated_at)
  VALUES (v_uid, p_enabled, p_iso_weekday, date_trunc('minute', p_local_time)::time, now())
  ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE
    SET enabled = EXCLUDED.enabled,
        iso_weekday = coalesce(EXCLUDED.iso_weekday, s.iso_weekday),
        local_time = coalesce(EXCLUDED.local_time, s.local_time),
        last_enqueued_for = CASE WHEN v_cancelled > 0 THEN NULL ELSE s.last_enqueued_for END,
        updated_at = now();
END;
$$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_my_journal_reminder()
RETURNS jsonb
LANGUAGE sql
STABLE
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
  SELECT coalesce(
    (SELECT jsonb_build_object(
              'enabled', s.enabled,
              'iso_weekday', s.iso_weekday,
              'local_time', to_char(s.local_time, 'HH24:MI'))
       FROM public.journal_reminder_settings AS s
      WHERE s.user_id = auth.uid()),
    jsonb_build_object('enabled', false, 'iso_weekday', NULL, 'local_time', NULL));
$$;

-- Every 15 minutes. A reminder is due when its local day and minute fall
-- within 15 minutes either side of now (a late run still catches it; the
-- source key and last_enqueued_for keep it to one per local day), it was
-- set before that minute, the person's zone is known and no hold is open.
-- It is queued for its own minute. The copy names nobody (checked phones).
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.enqueue_journal_reminders(p_now timestamptz DEFAULT now())
RETURNS integer
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = ''
AS $$
DECLARE
  v_count integer;
BEGIN
  WITH due AS (
    SELECT s.user_id, d.day, ((d.day + s.local_time) AT TIME ZONE z.tz) AS due_at
      FROM public.journal_reminder_settings AS s
      CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT public.notification_recipient_tz(s.user_id) AS tz) AS z
      CROSS JOIN LATERAL (
        SELECT ((p_now AT TIME ZONE z.tz)::date + k) AS day FROM generate_series(-1, 1) AS k
      ) AS d
     WHERE s.enabled
       AND z.tz IS NOT NULL
       AND extract(isodow FROM d.day) = s.iso_weekday
       AND (s.last_enqueued_for IS NULL OR s.last_enqueued_for < d.day)
       AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.account_holds AS h
                        WHERE h.user_id = s.user_id AND h.resolved_at IS NULL)
  ), picked AS (
    SELECT due.* FROM due
      JOIN public.journal_reminder_settings AS s ON s.user_id = due.user_id
     WHERE due.due_at > p_now - interval '15 minutes'
       AND due.due_at <= p_now + interval '15 minutes'
       AND due.due_at > s.updated_at
  ), queued AS (
    INSERT INTO public.scheduled_notifications
      (user_id, notification_type, scheduled_for, status, metadata, source_key, created_at, updated_at)
    SELECT p.user_id, 'immediate', greatest(p.due_at, p_now), 'pending',
           jsonb_build_object(
             'type', 'journal_reminder',
             'title', 'Attune',
             'body', 'A few quiet minutes for you, if you''d like.'),
           'journal_reminder:' || p.user_id::text || ':' || p.day::text,
           p_now, p_now
      FROM picked AS p
    ON CONFLICT (source_key) WHERE source_key IS NOT NULL DO NOTHING
    RETURNING user_id, split_part(source_key, ':', 3)::date AS day
  )
  UPDATE public.journal_reminder_settings AS s
     SET last_enqueued_for = q.day
    FROM queued AS q
   WHERE s.user_id = q.user_id;
  GET DIAGNOSTICS v_count = ROW_COUNT;
  RETURN v_count;
END;
$$;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.enqueue_journal_reminders(timestamptz) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;

DO $$
BEGIN
  PERFORM cron.unschedule(jobid) FROM cron.job WHERE jobname = 'enqueue-journal-reminders';
END;
$$;
SELECT cron.schedule(
  'enqueue-journal-reminders',
  '*/15 * * * *',
  $$ SELECT public.enqueue_journal_reminders(); $$
);

COMMIT;
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the grants twin**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261026100010_journal_reminders_grants.sql` with exactly:

```sql
-- Spec 1 W3c: grants for 20261026100000_journal_reminders.sql. The owner
-- reads their own row (RLS) and writes only through the RPC; the worker
-- (service role) keeps DML; the enqueue function is the cron's alone.
BEGIN;
REVOKE ALL ON public.journal_reminder_settings FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
GRANT SELECT ON public.journal_reminder_settings TO authenticated;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.journal_reminder_settings TO service_role;

REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.set_my_journal_reminder(boolean, smallint, time) FROM PUBLIC, anon;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.get_my_journal_reminder() FROM PUBLIC, anon;
REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.enqueue_journal_reminders(timestamptz) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.set_my_journal_reminder(boolean, smallint, time) TO authenticated;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.get_my_journal_reminder() TO authenticated;
COMMIT;
```

```bash
  psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f supabase/migrations/20261026100010_journal_reminders_grants.sql; echo "grants exit=$?"
done
psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f supabase/tests/journal_reminder_contracts.sql; echo "exit=$?"
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Mutation checks (each must turn the contract red with the message quoted)**

```bash
M=supabase/migrations/20261026100000_journal_reminders.sql
C=supabase/tests/journal_reminder_contracts.sql
D=.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-26-spec1-w3
sed 's/  USING (user_id = (SELECT auth.uid()));/  USING (true);/' $M > $D/mut_policy.sql
sed "s/             'title', 'Attune',/             'title', 'Your journal',/" $M > $D/mut_title.sql
sed 's/       AND due.due_at > s.updated_at/       AND true/' $M > $D/mut_fresh.sql
for m in policy title fresh; do
  grep -c 'USING (true)\|Your journal\|       AND true$' $D/mut_$m.sql
  psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f $D/mut_$m.sql > /dev/null 2>&1
  psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f $C 2>&1 | grep ERROR | cut -c1-160
done
psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f $M > /dev/null; echo "restored exit=$?"
psql -X -q -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -d attune_spec3a -f $C; echo "exit=$?"
```

```dart
class _Sdk implements PushSdk {
  bool granted = false;
  bool canPrompt = true;
  bool grantOnRequest = true;
  int prompts = 0;
  int optIns = 0;
  bool opted = false;
  String? subscription = 'sub-1';

  @override
  Future<void> initialize(String appId) async {}
  @override
  Future<void> login(String externalId) async {}
  @override
  Future<void> logout() async {}
  @override
  bool get permissionGranted => granted;
  @override
  Future<bool> canRequestPermission() async => canPrompt;
  @override
  Future<bool> requestPermission() async {
    prompts++;
    granted = grantOnRequest;
    return granted;
  }

  @override
  String? get subscriptionId => subscription;
  @override
  bool get optedIn => opted;
  @override
  Future<void> optIn() async {
    optIns++;
    opted = true;
  }

  @override
  Future<void> optOut() async => opted = false;
  @override
  void onSubscriptionChanged(void Function(String? id, bool optedIn) l) {}
}

class _Registry implements PushSubscriptionRegistry {
  int registers = 0;
  @override
  Future<void> register({
    required String subscriptionId,
    required String platform,
  }) async => registers++;
  @override
  Future<void> unregister(String subscriptionId) async {}
  @override
  Future<void> release(String subscriptionId) async {}
}

class _Owed implements OwedPushReleaseStore {
  @override
  Future<String?> read() async => null;
  @override
  Future<void> write(String subscriptionId) async {}
  @override
  Future<void> clear() async {}
}

class _OpenLock extends SafetyLockController {

}

User _user(String id) => User(

);

    OneSignalService service,
    _Sdk sdk,
    _Registry registry,
    List<String> settings,
  })
>
_build({bool pathsV2 = true, bool couplesV2 = false, String? link}) async {
  SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues({
    if (link != null)
      HomeStateStore.keyFor(
        'u1',
      ): '{"tab":"chat","link":"$link","needs":[],"hold":false,"at":"2026-10-26T08:00:00.000Z"}',
  });
  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  final sdk = _Sdk();
  final registry = _Registry();
  final settings = <String>[];
  final c = ProviderContainer(
    overrides: [
      sharedPreferencesProvider.overrideWithValue(prefs),
      signInFlowV2Provider.overrideWithValue(true),
      flowFlagsGatewayProvider.overrideWithValue(
        FakeFlowFlagsGateway(pathsV2: pathsV2, couplesV2: couplesV2),
      ),
      owedPushReleaseStoreProvider.overrideWithValue(_Owed()),
      chatConnectivityProvider.overrideWith(
        (ref) => const Stream<bool>.empty(),
      ),
      authStateProvider.overrideWith((ref) => Stream.value(_user('u1'))),
      currentUserProvider.overrideWith((ref) => _user('u1')),
      safetyLockProvider.overrideWith(_OpenLock.new),
      pushSubscriptionRegistryProvider.overrideWithValue(registry),
      oneSignalServiceProvider.overrideWith(
        (ref) => OneSignalService(
          ref,
          sdk: sdk,
          platform: 'ios',
          openSettings: () async => settings.add('opened'),
        ),
      ),
    ],
  );
  addTearDown(c.dispose);
  return (
    c: c,
    service: c.read(oneSignalServiceProvider),
    sdk: sdk,
    registry: registry,
    settings: settings,
  );
}

void main() {
  test('the automatic ask, by flag and link', () {
    for (final (paths, couples, link, want) in [
      (false, false, null, true),
      (true, false, null, false),
      (true, false, LinkState.none, false),
      (true, false, LinkState.pending, false),
      (true, false, LinkState.linked, true),
      (true, false, LinkState.paused, true),
      (true, true, LinkState.linked, false),
    ]) {
      expect(
        automaticPushAskAllowed(pathsV2: paths, couplesV2: couples, link: link),
        want,
        reason: '$paths $couples $link',
      );
    }
  });

  test('flow_paths_v2 on: no prompt after sign-in for Just for me', () async {
    final t = await _build(link: 'none');
    await t.service.attach();
    expect(t.sdk.prompts, 0);
  });

  test(
    'flow_paths_v2 on: a linked couple keeps today\'s ask while couples v2 is off',
    () async {
      final t = await _build(link: 'linked');
      await t.service.attach();
      expect(t.sdk.prompts, 1);
    },
  );

  test('flow_paths_v2 off: today\'s ask', () async {
    final t = await _build(pathsV2: false, link: 'none');
    await t.service.attach();
    expect(t.sdk.prompts, 1);
  });

  test('in context: the OS dialog, then opted in and registered', () async {
    final t = await _build(link: 'none');
    await t.service.attach();
    expect(t.service.permissionGranted, isFalse);
    expect(
      await t.service.askInContext(PushAskContext.j3),
      PushAskOutcome.granted,
    );
    expect(t.sdk.prompts, 1);
    expect(t.sdk.optIns, 1);
    expect(t.registry.registers, greaterThanOrEqualTo(1));
    expect(
      await t.service.askInContext(PushAskContext.j3),
      PushAskOutcome.granted,
    );
    expect(t.sdk.prompts, 1, reason: 'granted: no second dialog');
  });

  test(
    'in context: declined is declined; a refused OS opens Settings',
    () async {
      final t = await _build(link: 'none');
      await t.service.attach();
      t.sdk.grantOnRequest = false;
      expect(
        await t.service.askInContext(PushAskContext.r2),
        PushAskOutcome.declined,
      );
      t.sdk.canPrompt = false;
      expect(
        await t.service.askInContext(PushAskContext.r2),
        PushAskOutcome.openedSettings,
      );
      expect(t.settings, ['opened']);
    },
  );
}
```

```dart
Future<(FlowHarness, FakePushAsker)> _pump(
  WidgetTester tester, {
  required bool granted,
  String link = 'pending',
}) async {
  await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox());
  final asker = FakePushAsker(granted: granted);
  final h = await pumpFlowScreen(
    tester,

      home: snapshotOf(homeJson(path: 'relationship', link: link)),
    ),

      homeStateServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(
        HomeStateService(FakeHomeStateGateway()),
      ),
      invitePartnerServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(FakeInvites()),
      relationshipLifecycleProvider.overrideWith((ref) async => null),
      pushAskerProvider.overrideWithValue(asker),
    ],
  );
  return (h, asker);
}

void main() {
  testWidgets('pending, notifications off: the R2 ask', (tester) async {
    final (h, asker) = await _pump(tester, granted: false);
    expect(find.text('Get a heads-up when your partner joins'), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Turn on').first);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(asker.asked, [PushAskContext.r2]);
    expect(find.text('Get a heads-up when your partner joins'), findsNothing);
    expect(h.metrics, ['push_ask_shown:r2', 'push_ask_granted:r2']);
  });

  testWidgets('notifications on, or no invite: no ask', (tester) async {
    await _pump(tester, granted: true);
    expect(find.text('Get a heads-up when your partner joins'), findsNothing);
    await _pump(tester, granted: false, link: 'none');
    expect(find.text('Get a heads-up when your partner joins'), findsNothing);
  });
}
```

```dart
/// Where Spec 1 asks for notifications in context (§2.4): R2 "a heads-up
/// when your partner joins", R4 "when they message you" (W4b), J3 the
/// journal reminder. Each is one line of reason and one "Turn on".
enum PushAskContext { r2, r4, j3 }

/// What a contextual ask came to.
enum PushAskOutcome { granted, declined, openedSettings, unavailable }

/// Spec 1 W3c (§2.4): whether the automatic ask after sign-in may run.
/// Today's rule with flow_paths_v2 off. Under it the prompt is asked only
/// in context, except for a linked or paused couple while flow_couples_v2
/// is off: R4, the couples' context, exists only under that flag (W3 plan
/// P-24), so until then they keep today's ask. An unknown link is no ask.
bool automaticPushAskAllowed({
  required bool pathsV2,
  required bool couplesV2,
  required LinkState? link,
}) =>
    !pathsV2 ||
    (!couplesV2 && (link == LinkState.linked || link == LinkState.paused));
```

```dart
  /// Spec 1 W3c (§2.4): whether the automatic ask may run at all. Any
  /// failure to tell keeps today's behaviour.
  Future<bool> _automaticAskAllowed() async {
    try {
      final pathsV2 = await readFlowPathsV2(_ref);
      if (!pathsV2) return true;
      final userId = _ref.read(currentUserProvider)?.id;
      final home = userId == null ? null : _ref.read(homeStateProvider(userId));
      return automaticPushAskAllowed(
        pathsV2: true,
        couplesV2: _ref.read(flowCouplesV2Provider),
        link: home != null && home.known ? home.link : null,
      );
    } catch (_) {
      return true;
    }
  }

  @override
  bool get permissionGranted {
    if (!_sdkStarted) return false;
    try {
      return _sdk.permissionGranted;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  /// Spec 1 W3c (§2.4): the ask a person starts from inside a feature (R2,
  /// R4, J3). Granted: this phone is opted in and registered at once. The
  /// OS no longer shows its dialog: the app's settings open instead.
  @override
  Future<PushAskOutcome> askInContext(PushAskContext context) async {
    if (!_sdkStarted || _ref.read(currentUserProvider) == null) {
      return PushAskOutcome.unavailable;
    }
    if (permissionGranted) return PushAskOutcome.granted;
    bool osCanPrompt;
    try {
      osCanPrompt = await _sdk.canRequestPermission();
    } catch (_) {
      osCanPrompt = false;
    }
    if (!osCanPrompt) {
      try {
        await _openSettings();
      } catch (_) {}
      return PushAskOutcome.openedSettings;
    }
    _askedThisRun = true;
    try {
      await _sdk.requestPermission();
    } catch (_) {}
    if (!permissionGranted) return PushAskOutcome.declined;
    await _optInIfGranted();
    await _syncSubscription();
    return PushAskOutcome.granted;
  }

  Future<void> _maybeAskForPermission() async {
    // Spec 1 W3c: under flow_paths_v2 the prompt is asked in context.
    if (!await _automaticAskAllowed()) return;
    bool osCanPrompt;
```

Create `lib/core/notifications/presentation/widgets/push_ask_row.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:attune/app/theme/design_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/metrics/metric_counter.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/notifications/services/onesignal_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/notifications/services/push_permission_policy.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_colors.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

/// The contextual ask (Spec 1 §2.4): one line of reason and one "Turn on",
/// inside the feature, only while notifications are off. Dismissing is
/// carrying on (nothing else asks). Counts push_ask_shown:{context} once
/// per showing and push_ask_granted:{context} on a yes (S-2 exact keys).
class PushAskRow extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const PushAskRow({
    super.key,
    required this.askContext,
    required this.reason,
    required this.action,
  });

  final PushAskContext askContext;
  final String reason;
  final String action;

  @override
  ConsumerState<PushAskRow> createState() => _PushAskRowState();
}

class _PushAskRowState extends ConsumerState<PushAskRow>
    with WidgetsBindingObserver {
  late bool _granted;
  bool _busy = false;

  ClientMetricKey get _shownKey => switch (widget.askContext) {
    PushAskContext.r2 => ClientMetricKey.pushAskShownR2,
    PushAskContext.r4 => ClientMetricKey.pushAskShownR4,
    PushAskContext.j3 => ClientMetricKey.pushAskShownJ3,
  };

  ClientMetricKey get _grantedKey => switch (widget.askContext) {
    PushAskContext.r2 => ClientMetricKey.pushAskGrantedR2,
    PushAskContext.r4 => ClientMetricKey.pushAskGrantedR4,
    PushAskContext.j3 => ClientMetricKey.pushAskGrantedJ3,
  };

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);
    _granted = ref.read(pushAskerProvider).permissionGranted;
    if (!_granted) ref.read(metricCounterProvider).bump(_shownKey);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);
    super.dispose();
  }

  /// Back from the phone's Settings: the switch may be on now.
  @override
  void didChangeAppLifecycleState(AppLifecycleState state) {
    if (state != AppLifecycleState.resumed || _granted) return;
    final granted = ref.read(pushAskerProvider).permissionGranted;
    if (granted && mounted) {
      ref.read(metricCounterProvider).bump(_grantedKey);
      setState(() => _granted = true);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _ask() async {
    if (_busy) return;
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    final outcome = await ref
        .read(pushAskerProvider)
        .askInContext(widget.askContext);
    if (!mounted) return;
    if (outcome == PushAskOutcome.granted) {
      ref.read(metricCounterProvider).bump(_grantedKey);
    }
    setState(() {
      _busy = false;
      _granted = outcome == PushAskOutcome.granted;
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_granted) return const SizedBox.shrink();
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: Spacing.xs),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Icon(
                Icons.notifications_none_outlined,
                color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: Spacing.sm),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  widget.reason,
                  style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                    color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: _busy ? null : _ask,
            style: TextButton.styleFrom(
              foregroundColor: flowAccent(context),
              minimumSize: const Size(48, 48),
            ),
            child: Text(widget.action),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

Create `test/helpers/fake_reminder_rpc.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W3c: get_my_journal_reminder / set_my_journal_reminder, in memory.
class FakeReminderRpc {
  Map<String, dynamic> stored = {
    'enabled': false,
    'iso_weekday': null,
    'local_time': null,
  };
  Object? failWith;
  final calls = <(String, Map<String, dynamic>?)>[];

  Future<Object?> call(String fn, Map<String, dynamic>? params) async {
    calls.add((fn, params));
    if (fn == 'get_my_journal_reminder') return stored;
    if (failWith != null) throw failWith!;
    stored = {
      'enabled': params!['p_enabled'],
      'iso_weekday': params['p_iso_weekday'] ?? stored['iso_weekday'],
      'local_time': params['p_local_time'] ?? stored['local_time'],
    };
    return null;
  }
}
```

Create `test/features/me/journal_reminder_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W3c (§3.10 J3, §2.4): the weekly reminder: its summary, its sheet,
// 08:00-21:59 only, turning it off keeps day and time, and the contextual
// notification ask inside the sheet while notifications are off.
import 'package:attune/core/notifications/presentation/widgets/push_ask_row.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/notifications/services/onesignal_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/notifications/services/push_permission_policy.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/me/data/journal_reminder_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/me/presentation/widgets/journal_reminder_row.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

import '../../helpers/fake_reminder_rpc.dart';
import '../../helpers/fake_push_asker.dart';
import '../../helpers/flow_harness.dart';

Future<(FlowHarness, FakeReminderRpc, FakePushAsker)> _pump(
  WidgetTester tester, {
  Map<String, dynamic>? stored,
  bool pushGranted = true,
}) async {
  // A fresh ProviderScope each time (pumpWidget would reuse the last one).
  await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox());
  final rpc = FakeReminderRpc();
  if (stored != null) rpc.stored = stored;
  final asker = FakePushAsker(granted: pushGranted);
  final h = await pumpFlowScreen(
    tester,
    () => const Scaffold(body: JournalReminderRow()),
    overrides: [
      journalReminderRpcProvider.overrideWithValue(rpc.call),
      pushAskerProvider.overrideWithValue(asker),
    ],
  );
  return (h, rpc, asker);
}

void main() {
  test('the wire shape and the allowed times', () {
    final r = JournalReminder.fromJson({
      'enabled': true,
      'iso_weekday': 3,
      'local_time': '20:15',
    });
    expect(r.enabled, isTrue);
    expect(r.isoWeekday, 3);
    expect(r.time, const TimeOfDay(hour: 20, minute: 15));
    expect(JournalReminder.fromJson(null).enabled, isFalse);
    expect(reminderWireTime(const TimeOfDay(hour: 8, minute: 5)), '08:05');
    expect(reminderTimeAllowed(const TimeOfDay(hour: 8, minute: 0)), isTrue);
    expect(reminderTimeAllowed(const TimeOfDay(hour: 21, minute: 59)), isTrue);
    expect(reminderTimeAllowed(const TimeOfDay(hour: 22, minute: 0)), isFalse);
    expect(reminderTimeAllowed(const TimeOfDay(hour: 7, minute: 59)), isFalse);
  });

  testWidgets('the row reads "Off", then "{weekday}s at {time}"', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    await _pump(tester);
    expect(find.text('Remind me to write'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Off'), findsOneWidget);
    await _pump(
      tester,
      stored: {'enabled': true, 'iso_weekday': 1, 'local_time': '20:00'},
    );
    expect(find.text('Mondays at 08:00 PM'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('turning it on: the chosen day at 20:00 by default', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final (_, rpc, _) = await _pump(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Remind me to write'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('When should we remind you?'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Turn off'), findsNothing);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Wed'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Turn on'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    final set = rpc.calls.firstWhere((c) => c.$1 == 'set_my_journal_reminder');
    expect(set.$2, {
      'p_enabled': true,
      'p_iso_weekday': 3,
      'p_local_time': '20:00',
    });
    expect(find.text('Wednesdays at 08:00 PM'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('turning it off keeps the day and time', (tester) async {
    final (_, rpc, _) = await _pump(
      tester,
      stored: {'enabled': true, 'iso_weekday': 5, 'local_time': '09:30'},
    );
    await tester.tap(find.text('Remind me to write'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Turn off'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    final set = rpc.calls.lastWhere((c) => c.$1 == 'set_my_journal_reminder');
    expect(set.$2, {
      'p_enabled': false,
      'p_iso_weekday': 5,
      'p_local_time': '09:30',
    });
    expect(find.text('Off'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('a refused quiet-hours time is explained, not saved', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final (_, rpc, _) = await _pump(tester);
    rpc.failWith = const PostgrestException(
      message: 'journal_reminder:quiet_hours',
      code: 'P0001',
    );
    await tester.tap(find.text('Remind me to write'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('Turn on'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(
      find.text(
        'Choose a time between 08:00 AM and 10:00 PM. Attune stays quiet at night.',
      ),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
  });

  testWidgets('notifications off: the J3 ask is in the sheet', (tester) async {
    final (h, _, asker) = await _pump(tester, pushGranted: false);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Remind me to write'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Reminders need notifications.'), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Turn on notifications'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(asker.asked, [PushAskContext.j3]);
    expect(find.text('Reminders need notifications.'), findsNothing);
    expect(h.metrics, ['push_ask_shown:j3', 'push_ask_granted:j3']);
  });

  testWidgets('notifications on: no ask, nothing counted', (tester) async {
    final (h, _, _) = await _pump(tester);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Remind me to write'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.byType(PushAskRow), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Reminders need notifications.'), findsNothing);
    expect(h.metrics, isEmpty);
  });

  testWidgets('the sheet meets the guidelines', (tester) async {
    final semantics = tester.ensureSemantics();
    await _pump(tester, pushGranted: false);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Remind me to write'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await expectFlowGuidelines(tester);
    semantics.dispose();
  });

  testWidgets('J3 sheet goldens', (tester) async {
    await expectFlowGoldens(
      tester,
      'j3_sheet',
      () => const Scaffold(body: JournalReminderSheet()),
      overrides: [
        journalReminderRpcProvider.overrideWithValue(FakeReminderRpc().call),
        pushAskerProvider.overrideWithValue(FakePushAsker()),
      ],
    );
  });
}
```

Create `test/core/notifications/journal_reminder_tap_test.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// Spec 1 W3c (§3.11, §3.10 J3): a journal reminder tap opens Me (Tab 2,
// /home?tab=me) with the journal on top.
import 'package:attune/app/routing/app_router.dart' show RouteNames;
import 'package:attune/core/notifications/config/notification_config.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/notifications/domain/entities/app_notification.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

void main() {
  testWidgets('journal_reminder: Me, then the journal', (tester) async {
    late BuildContext tapContext;
    final router = GoRouter(
      initialLocation: '/somewhere',
      routes: [
        GoRoute(
          path: '/somewhere',
          builder:
              (context, _) => Builder(
                builder: (c) {
                  tapContext = c;
                  return const Text('ELSEWHERE');
                },
              ),
        ),
        GoRoute(
          path: RouteNames.home,
          builder: (_, s) => Text('HOME ${s.uri.queryParameters['tab']}'),
        ),
        GoRoute(
          path: RouteNames.reflectionJournal,
          builder: (_, __) => const Text('JOURNAL'),
        ),
      ],
    );
    addTearDown(router.dispose);
    await tester.pumpWidget(MaterialApp.router(routerConfig: router));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();

    buildNanoEmbryoNotificationConfig().onNotificationTap!(
      AppNotification(
        id: 'n1',
        title: 'Attune',
        body: "A few quiet minutes for you, if you'd like.",
        data: const {'type': 'journal_reminder'},
        createdAt: DateTime.utc(2026, 11, 2, 20),
      ),
      tapContext,
    );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('JOURNAL'), findsOneWidget);
    router.pop();
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('HOME me'), findsOneWidget);
  });
}
```

```dart
// Spec 1 W3b/W3c (§3.9, §3.10, §3.12): the Settings rows Spec 1 adds go in
// their sections, and only under flow_paths_v2.
import 'package:attune/core/providers/shared_prefs_provider.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/me/data/journal_reminder_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/me/presentation/widgets/journal_reminder_row.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/flow_flags.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/settings/data/settings_data.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/settings/models/settings_config.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

import '../../helpers/fake_home_state.dart';
import '../../helpers/fake_reminder_rpc.dart';

Future<List<SettingsConfig?>> _rows(
  WidgetTester tester, {
  required bool pathsV2,
}) async {
  SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues({});
  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  late List<SettingsConfig?> rows;
  await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox());
  await tester.pumpWidget(
    ProviderScope(
      overrides: [
        sharedPreferencesProvider.overrideWithValue(prefs),
        flowPathsV2Provider.overrideWithValue(pathsV2),
        journalReminderRpcProvider.overrideWithValue(FakeReminderRpc().call),
      ],
      child: MaterialApp(
        localizationsDelegates: AppLocalizations.localizationsDelegates,
        supportedLocales: AppLocalizations.supportedLocales,
        home: Consumer(
          builder: (context, ref, _) {
            rows = [journalReminderSetting(context, ref)];
            return const SizedBox();
          },
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
  await tester.pumpAndSettle();
  return rows;
}

void main() {
  testWidgets('flow_paths_v2 off: no row', (tester) async {
    expect(await _rows(tester, pathsV2: false), [null]);
  });

  testWidgets('flow_paths_v2 on: the row', (tester) async {
    final rows = await _rows(tester, pathsV2: true);
    expect(rows[0]!.title, 'Remind me to write');
    expect(rows[0]!.subtitle, 'Off');
  });

  testWidgets('each row sits in its section', (tester) async {
    late List<dynamic> sections;
    SettingsConfig row(String id) => SettingsConfig(
      id: id,
      title: id,
      subtitle: '',
      icon: Icons.circle,
      type: SettingsItemType.navigation,
    );
    await tester.pumpWidget(
      MaterialApp(
        localizationsDelegates: AppLocalizations.localizationsDelegates,
        supportedLocales: AppLocalizations.supportedLocales,
        home: Builder(
          builder: (context) {
            sections = SettingsDataSource.getSettingsSections(
              context,
              'u1',
              datingLock: row('dating_lock'),
              journalReminder: row('journal_reminder'),
            );
            return const SizedBox();
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
    String sectionOf(String id) =>
        sections.firstWhere((s) => s.items.any((i) => i.id == id)).id as String;
    expect(sectionOf('dating_lock'), 'safety');
    expect(sectionOf('journal_reminder'), 'notifications_location');
  });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The reminder service**

Create `lib/features/me/data/journal_reminder_service.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart';

/// J3's weekly reminder (Spec 1 §3.10, §4.2): a weekday (ISO, Monday 1)
/// and a local time, both kept while it's off.
@immutable
class JournalReminder {
  const JournalReminder({required this.enabled, this.isoWeekday, this.time});

  static const off = JournalReminder(enabled: false);

  final bool enabled;
  final int? isoWeekday;
  final TimeOfDay? time;

  /// get_my_journal_reminder()'s `{enabled, iso_weekday, local_time}`.
  factory JournalReminder.fromJson(Object? json) {
    if (json is! Map) return off;
    final day = json['iso_weekday'];
    final raw = json['local_time'];
    TimeOfDay? time;
    if (raw is String) {
      final parts = raw.split(':');
      final hour = parts.isNotEmpty ? int.tryParse(parts[0]) : null;
      final minute = parts.length > 1 ? int.tryParse(parts[1]) : null;
      if (hour != null && minute != null) {
        time = TimeOfDay(hour: hour, minute: minute);
      }
    }
    return JournalReminder(
      enabled: json['enabled'] == true,
      isoWeekday: day is int && day >= 1 && day <= 7 ? day : null,
      time: time,
    );
  }
}

/// The reminder's times: from 08:00 up to (not including) 22:00 local, so
/// quiet hours (22:00-08:00) never move it to another day (W3 plan P-21).
bool reminderTimeAllowed(TimeOfDay time) => time.hour >= 8 && time.hour < 22;

/// `HH:mm`, as set_my_journal_reminder takes a time.
String reminderWireTime(TimeOfDay time) =>
    '${time.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${time.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';

enum ReminderRefusal { quietHours, invalid }

class ReminderRefused implements Exception {
  const ReminderRefused(this.reason);
  final ReminderRefusal reason;
}

typedef ReminderRpc =
    Future<Object?> Function(String fn, Map<String, dynamic>? params);

final journalReminderRpcProvider = Provider<ReminderRpc>(
  (ref) => (fn, params) => Supabase.instance.client.rpc(fn, params: params),
);

class JournalReminderService {
  JournalReminderService(this._rpc);

  final ReminderRpc _rpc;

  static const timeout = Duration(seconds: 15);

  Future<JournalReminder> read() async => JournalReminder.fromJson(
    await _rpc('get_my_journal_reminder', null).timeout(timeout),
  );

  Future<void> save(JournalReminder reminder) async {
    try {
      await _rpc('set_my_journal_reminder', {
        'p_enabled': reminder.enabled,
        'p_iso_weekday': reminder.isoWeekday,
        'p_local_time':
            reminder.time == null ? null : reminderWireTime(reminder.time!),
      }).timeout(timeout);
    } on PostgrestException catch (error) {
      if (error.message.startsWith('journal_reminder:quiet_hours')) {
        throw const ReminderRefused(ReminderRefusal.quietHours);
      }
      if (error.message.startsWith('journal_reminder:')) {
        throw const ReminderRefused(ReminderRefusal.invalid);
      }
      rethrow;
    }
  }
}

final journalReminderServiceProvider = Provider<JournalReminderService>(
  (ref) => JournalReminderService(ref.watch(journalReminderRpcProvider)),
);

/// The signed-in person's reminder, read whenever a screen shows it (so a
/// change of account never shows the last one's). Off when unreadable.
class JournalReminderNotifier
    extends AutoDisposeAsyncNotifier<JournalReminder> {
  @override
  Future<JournalReminder> build() async {
    try {
      return await ref.read(journalReminderServiceProvider).read();
    } catch (error) {
      debugPrint('[journal-reminder] read failed: ${error.runtimeType}');
      return JournalReminder.off;
    }
  }

  /// Saves it; throws ReminderRefused or a transient error to the caller.
  Future<void> save(JournalReminder reminder) async {
    await ref.read(journalReminderServiceProvider).save(reminder);
    state = AsyncData(reminder);
  }
}

final journalReminderProvider =
    AsyncNotifierProvider.autoDispose<JournalReminderNotifier, JournalReminder>(
      JournalReminderNotifier.new,
    );
```

Create `lib/features/me/presentation/widgets/journal_reminder_row.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:attune/app/theme/design_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/notifications/services/push_permission_policy.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/notifications/presentation/widgets/push_ask_row.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/ui/text/allow_large_text.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/utils/date_formatter.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_buttons.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_colors.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_text_field.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/me/data/journal_reminder_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/me/presentation/widgets/your_space_section.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/flow_flags.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/settings/models/settings_config.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/semantics.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

String _clock(TimeOfDay time) =>
    MyDateFormat.toTime(DateTime(2000, 1, 1, time.hour, time.minute));

String _days(AppLocalizations loc, int isoWeekday) => switch (isoWeekday) {
  1 => loc.flow_weekdaysMon,
  2 => loc.flow_weekdaysTue,
  3 => loc.flow_weekdaysWed,
  4 => loc.flow_weekdaysThu,
  5 => loc.flow_weekdaysFri,
  6 => loc.flow_weekdaysSat,
  _ => loc.flow_weekdaysSun,
};

/// "Off" or "Mondays at 08:00 PM" (Spec 1 §3.10 J3; MyDateFormat.toTime).
String reminderSummary(AppLocalizations loc, JournalReminder reminder) {
  final day = reminder.isoWeekday;
  final time = reminder.time;
  if (!reminder.enabled || day == null || time == null) {
    return loc.flow_reminderOff;
  }
  return loc.flow_reminderWhen(_days(loc, day), _clock(time));
}

/// J3's row in Your space.
class JournalReminderRow extends ConsumerWidget {
  const JournalReminderRow({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final reminder = ref.watch(journalReminderProvider).valueOrNull;
    return SpaceRow(
      title: loc.flow_reminderTitle,
      subtitle: reminder == null ? '' : reminderSummary(loc, reminder),
      icon: Icons.alarm_outlined,
      onTap: () => showJournalReminderSheet(context),
    );
  }
}

/// Settings -> Notifications -> "Remind me to write" (W3 plan P-22): the
/// same sheet, from anywhere, so the reminder can always be turned off.
SettingsConfig? journalReminderSetting(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
  if (!ref.watch(flowPathsV2Provider)) return null;
  final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
  final reminder = ref.watch(journalReminderProvider).valueOrNull;
  return SettingsConfig(
    id: 'journal_reminder',
    title: loc.flow_reminderTitle,
    subtitle: reminder == null ? '' : reminderSummary(loc, reminder),
    icon: Icons.alarm_outlined,
    type: SettingsItemType.navigation,
    onTap: () => showJournalReminderSheet(context),
    iconColor: Colors.orange,
  );
}

Future<void> showJournalReminderSheet(BuildContext context) =>
    showModalBottomSheet<void>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      showDragHandle: true,
      builder: (_) => const JournalReminderSheet(),
    );

/// J3's sheet: "When should we remind you?" A weekday, a time (08:00 to
/// 21:59), "Turn on"; "Turn off" when on; the contextual notification ask
/// while notifications are off.
class JournalReminderSheet extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const JournalReminderSheet({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<JournalReminderSheet> createState() =>
      _JournalReminderSheetState();
}

class _JournalReminderSheetState extends ConsumerState<JournalReminderSheet> {
  int? _day;
  TimeOfDay? _time;
  bool _busy = false;
  String? _error;

  static const _defaultTime = TimeOfDay(hour: 20, minute: 0);

  void _fail(String message) {
    setState(() => _error = message);
    SemanticsService.announce(message, Directionality.of(context));
  }

  String _quietHoursMessage(AppLocalizations loc) =>
      loc.flow_reminderQuietHours(_time08, _time22);

  String get _time08 => _clock(const TimeOfDay(hour: 8, minute: 0));
  String get _time22 => _clock(const TimeOfDay(hour: 22, minute: 0));

  Future<void> _pickTime(TimeOfDay current) async {
    final picked = await showTimePicker(context: context, initialTime: current);
    if (picked == null || !mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _time = picked;
      _error = null;
    });
    if (!reminderTimeAllowed(picked)) {
      _fail(_quietHoursMessage(AppLocalizations.of(context)!));
    }
  }

  Future<void> _save(JournalReminder reminder) async {
    if (_busy) return;
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final time = reminder.time;
    if (reminder.enabled && (time == null || !reminderTimeAllowed(time))) {
      _fail(_quietHoursMessage(loc));
      return;
    }
    setState(() {
      _busy = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      await ref.read(journalReminderProvider.notifier).save(reminder);
      if (mounted) Navigator.of(context).pop();
    } on ReminderRefused catch (refused) {
      if (mounted) {
        _fail(
          refused.reason == ReminderRefusal.quietHours
              ? _quietHoursMessage(loc)
              : loc.flow_saveFailed,
        );
      }
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) _fail(loc.flow_saveFailed);
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final saved =
        ref.watch(journalReminderProvider).valueOrNull ?? JournalReminder.off;
    final day = _day ?? saved.isoWeekday ?? 1;
    final time = _time ?? saved.time ?? _defaultTime;
    final labels = [
      loc.flow_weekdayMon,
      loc.flow_weekdayTue,
      loc.flow_weekdayWed,
      loc.flow_weekdayThu,
      loc.flow_weekdayFri,
      loc.flow_weekdaySat,
      loc.flow_weekdaySun,
    ];
    return AllowLargeText(
      child: SafeArea(
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
            Spacing.lg,
            0,
            Spacing.lg,
            Spacing.md,
          ),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              Semantics(
                header: true,
                child: Text(
                  loc.flow_reminderSheetTitle,
                  style: theme.textTheme.titleLarge?.copyWith(
                    color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
              Text(
                loc.flow_reminderDay,
                style: theme.textTheme.titleSmall?.copyWith(
                  color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: Spacing.xs),
              Wrap(
                spacing: Spacing.sm,
                children: [
                  for (var i = 0; i < 7; i++)
                    ChoiceChip(
                      label: Text(labels[i]),
                      selected: day == i + 1,
                      onSelected: (_) => setState(() => _day = i + 1),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
              Row(
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          loc.flow_reminderTime,
                          style: theme.textTheme.titleSmall?.copyWith(
                            color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                          ),
                        ),
                        Text(
                          _clock(time),
                          style: theme.textTheme.bodyLarge?.copyWith(
                            color: theme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  TextButton(
                    onPressed: () => _pickTime(time),
                    style: TextButton.styleFrom(
                      foregroundColor: flowAccent(context),
                      minimumSize: const Size(48, 48),
                    ),
                    child: Text(loc.flow_reminderChangeTime),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              PushAskRow(
                askContext: PushAskContext.j3,
                reason: loc.flow_reminderNeedsPush,
                action: loc.flow_reminderTurnOnPush,
              ),
              if (_error != null) ...[
                const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
                FlowErrorLine(text: _error!),
              ],
              const SizedBox(height: Spacing.md),
              FlowPrimaryButton(
                label: loc.flow_reminderTurnOn,
                busy: _busy,
                onPressed:
                    () => _save(
                      JournalReminder(
                        enabled: true,
                        isoWeekday: day,
                        time: time,
                      ),
                    ),
              ),
              if (saved.enabled) ...[
                const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
                FlowSecondaryButton(
                  label: loc.flow_reminderTurnOff,
                  onPressed:
                      () => _save(
                        JournalReminder(
                          enabled: false,
                          isoWeekday: saved.isoWeekday,
                          time: saved.time,
                        ),
                      ),
                ),
              ],
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```
