```dart
// Spec 1 W3c (§3.12): Settings -> How you use Attune. Every switch is
// silent; each "from -> to" shows what the table says; an open shared space
// is paused or ended first.
import 'package:attune/app/routing/app_router.dart' show RouteNames;
import 'package:attune/app/theme/app_theme.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/providers/shared_prefs_provider.dart';
import 'package:attune/core/utils/screen_util_config.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/data/home_state_service.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/settings/presentation/screens/how_you_use_attune_screen.dart';
import 'package:attune/home/home_seams.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

import '../../helpers/fake_home_state.dart';
import '../../helpers/flow_harness.dart';
import '../../helpers/home_harness.dart';

Future<(GoRouter, FakeHomeStateGateway)> _pump(
  WidgetTester tester,
  Map<String, dynamic> server,
) async {
  tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(390, 1200);
  tester.view.devicePixelRatio = 1;
  addTearDown(tester.view.reset);
  await tester.pumpWidget(const SizedBox());
  SharedPreferences.setMockInitialValues({});
  final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
  final gateway = FakeHomeStateGateway(state: server);
  final router = GoRouter(
    initialLocation: '/settings',
    routes: [
      GoRoute(
        path: '/settings',
        builder: (_, __) => const Text('SETTINGS'),
        routes: [
          GoRoute(
            path: 'how-you-use-attune',
            builder: (_, __) => const HowYouUseAttuneScreen(),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/home',
        builder: (_, s) => Text('HOME ${s.uri.queryParameters['tab']}'),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/invite-partner',
        builder: (_, s) => Text('INVITE ${s.uri.query}'),
      ),
    ],
  );
  addTearDown(router.dispose);
  await tester.pumpWidget(
    ProviderScope(
      overrides: [
        sharedPreferencesProvider.overrideWithValue(prefs),
        homeAuthProvider.overrideWithValue(FakeHomeAuth(testUser('u-how'))),
        homeStateServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(HomeStateService(gateway)),
      ],
      child: ScreenUtilConfig.builder(
        builder:
            (_) => MaterialApp.router(
              theme: AppTheme.lightTheme,
              localizationsDelegates: AppLocalizations.localizationsDelegates,
              supportedLocales: AppLocalizations.supportedLocales,
              routerConfig: router,
            ),
      ),
    ),
  );
  router.push(RouteNames.howYouUseAttune);
  await tester.pumpAndSettle();
  return (router, gateway);
}

void main() {
  testWidgets('three ways, the current one chosen, the silent footer', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    await _pump(tester, homeJson(path: 'just_me'));
    expect(find.text('How you use Attune'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('In a relationship'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Single and open to dating'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Just for me'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(
      find.text(
        'Your journal, quizzes and Healing stay yours whichever you choose. Nobody is told when you change this.',
      ),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    final checked = tester.widget<Icon>(
      find.byIcon(Icons.radio_button_checked),
    );
    expect(checked, isNotNull);
  });

  testWidgets('Just for me -> Dating: Me with the slot', (tester) async {
    final (router, gateway) = await _pump(tester, homeJson(path: 'just_me'));
    gateway.onSetPath =
        (p) => gateway.state = homeJson(path: p['p_path'] as String);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Single and open to dating'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(gateway.calls.lastWhere((c) => c.$1 == 'set_my_path').$2, {
      'p_path': 'dating',
      'p_confirmed': false,
    });
    expect(find.text('HOME me'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('Just for me -> Relationship: R1, the switch at the invite', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final (_, gateway) = await _pump(tester, homeJson(path: 'just_me'));
    await tester.tap(find.text('In a relationship'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('INVITE switch=1'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(gateway.count('set_my_path'), 0);
  });

  testWidgets(
    'Relationship pending -> other: "Cancel your invite?" then switch',
    (tester) async {
      final (_, gateway) = await _pump(
        tester,
        homeJson(path: 'relationship', link: 'pending'),
      );
      gateway.failNext = FakeHomeStateGateway.refusal('path:confirm_withdraw');
      gateway.onSetPath =
          (p) => gateway.state = homeJson(path: p['p_path'] as String);
      await tester.tap(find.text('Just for me'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      expect(find.text('Cancel your invite?'), findsOneWidget);
      await tester.tap(find.text('Cancel invite and switch'));
      await tester.pumpAndSettle();
      final calls = gateway.calls.where((c) => c.$1 == 'set_my_path').toList();
      expect(calls.map((c) => c.$2!['p_confirmed']), [false, true]);
      expect(find.text('HOME me'), findsOneWidget);
    },
  );

  testWidgets('Relationship linked -> other: pause or end first', (
    tester,
  ) async {
    final (_, gateway) = await _pump(
      tester,
      homeJson(
        path: 'relationship',
        link: 'linked',
        relationshipId: 'r1',
        partnerFirstName: 'Kofi',
      ),
    );
    await tester.tap(find.text('Just for me'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text("You're in a shared space with Kofi"), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Pause our shared space'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('End relationship'), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Not now'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(gateway.count('set_my_path'), 0);
  });

  testWidgets('a paused space offers no second pause', (tester) async {
    await _pump(
      tester,
      homeJson(path: 'relationship', link: 'paused', relationshipId: 'r1'),
    );
    await tester.tap(find.text('Single and open to dating'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(
      find.text("You're in a shared space with your partner"),
      findsOneWidget,
    );
    expect(find.text('Pause our shared space'), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('Dating -> Just for me asks first', (tester) async {
    final (_, gateway) = await _pump(
      tester,
      homeJson(path: 'dating', datingEnrolled: true),
    );
    gateway.onSetPath =
        (p) => gateway.state = homeJson(path: p['p_path'] as String);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Just for me'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Use Attune just for you?'), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Switch'));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(gateway.calls.lastWhere((c) => c.$1 == 'set_my_path').$2, {
      'p_path': 'just_me',
      'p_confirmed': false,
    });
  });

  testWidgets('meets the guidelines', (tester) async {
    final semantics = tester.ensureSemantics();
    await _pump(tester, homeJson(path: 'just_me'));
    await expectFlowGuidelines(tester);
    semantics.dispose();
  });

  testWidgets('goldens', (tester) async {
    await expectFlowGoldens(
      tester,
      'how_you_use_attune',
      () => const HowYouUseAttuneScreen(),
      overrides: [
        homeAuthProvider.overrideWithValue(FakeHomeAuth(testUser('u-how'))),
        homeStateServiceProvider.overrideWithValue(
          HomeStateService(
            FakeHomeStateGateway(state: homeJson(path: 'just_me')),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  });
}
```

Create `lib/features/settings/presentation/screens/how_you_use_attune_screen.dart` with exactly:

```dart
import 'package:attune/app/routing/app_router.dart' show RouteNames;
import 'package:attune/app/theme/design_tokens.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/auth/sign_up/presentation/widgets/flow_frame.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/me/presentation/path_switch.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/domain/home_state.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/path_chooser.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/flow_flags.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/providers/home_state_providers.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/onboarding/presentation/widgets/path_step.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/relationships/presentation/widgets/end_relationship_action.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/relationships/presentation/widgets/pause_relationship_action.dart';
import 'package:attune/features/settings/models/settings_config.dart';
import 'package:attune/home/home_location.dart';
import 'package:attune/home/home_seams.dart';
import 'package:attune/i10n/generated/app_localizations.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

/// Settings -> "How you use Attune" (Spec 1 §3.12), under flow_paths_v2.
SettingsConfig? howYouUseSetting(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
  if (!ref.watch(flowPathsV2Provider)) return null;
  final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
  return SettingsConfig(
    id: 'how_you_use_attune',
    title: loc.flow_howTitle,
    subtitle: loc.flow_howSubtitle,
    icon: Icons.alt_route_outlined,
    type: SettingsItemType.navigation,
    routeName: RouteNames.howYouUseAttune,
    onTap: () => context.push(RouteNames.howYouUseAttune),
    iconColor: Colors.teal,
    order: 4,
  );
}

/// Settings -> How you use Attune (Spec 1 §3.12): the three paths with A6's
/// lines; every switch is silent to everyone else. An open or paused
/// shared space has to be paused or ended first (the Spec 3a actions).
class HowYouUseAttuneScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const HowYouUseAttuneScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<HowYouUseAttuneScreen> createState() =>
      _HowYouUseAttuneScreenState();
}

class _HowYouUseAttuneScreenState extends ConsumerState<HowYouUseAttuneScreen> {
  bool _busy = false;

  String? get _userId => ref.read(homeAuthProvider).currentUser?.id;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final userId = _userId;
    // The path is read live (D20): ask the server now.
    if (userId != null) {
      ref.read(homeStateProvider(userId).notifier).refresh();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _choose(HomeSnapshot home, AppPath to) async {
    if (_busy || home.path == to) return;
    final open = home.link == LinkState.linked || home.link == LinkState.paused;
    if (home.path == AppPath.relationship && open) {
      await showSharedSpaceSheet(context, home);
      return;
    }
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    final outcome = await switchPath(context, ref, to: to, home: home);
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() => _busy = false);
    if (outcome == PathChoiceOutcome.relationshipOpen) {
      await showSharedSpaceSheet(context, home);
      return;
    }
    if (outcome == PathChoiceOutcome.done && to != AppPath.relationship) {
      context.go(homeLocation(tab: AppTab.forPath(to)));
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final userId = _userId;
    final home =
        userId == null
            ? HomeSnapshot.unknown
            : ref.watch(homeStateProvider(userId));
    final current = home.path;
    final options = [
      (
        AppPath.relationship,
        loc.flow_pathRelationshipTitle,
        loc.flow_pathRelationshipLine,
      ),
      (AppPath.dating, loc.flow_pathDatingTitle, loc.flow_pathDatingLine),
      (AppPath.justMe, loc.flow_pathJustMeTitle, loc.flow_pathJustMeLineFull),
    ];
    return FlowFrame(
      title: loc.flow_howTitle,
      onBack: () => context.pop(),
      body:
          current == null
              ? const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator())
              : Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  Semantics(
                    container: true,
                    label: loc.flow_pathGroupSemantics,
                    explicitChildNodes: true,
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                      children: [
                        for (final (path, title, line) in options) ...[
                          PathChoiceCard(
                            title: title,
                            line: line,
                            selected: current == path,
                            onTap: () => _choose(home, path),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: Spacing.smMd),
                        ],
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: Spacing.sm),
                  Text(
                    loc.flow_howFooter,
                    style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                      color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
    );
  }
}

/// §3.12: "You're in a shared space with {name}. To use Attune another way,
/// end it first. Pausing keeps it, read-only, for up to 30 days." with the
/// existing Spec 3a actions (no pause while already paused).
Future<void> showSharedSpaceSheet(BuildContext context, HomeSnapshot home) {
  final loc = AppLocalizations.of(context)!;
  final relationshipId = home.linkDetail.relationshipId;
  final name = home.linkDetail.partnerFirstName;
  return showModalBottomSheet<void>(
    context: context,
    isScrollControlled: true,
    showDragHandle: true,
    builder:
        (sheet) => FlowConfirmSheetBody(
          title: loc.flow_sharedOpenTitle(
            name == null || name.isEmpty ? loc.flow_yourPartner : name,
          ),
          body: loc.flow_sharedOpenBody,
          actions: [
            if (home.link != LinkState.paused && relationshipId != null)
              (
                loc.flow_sharedPause,
                () {
                  Navigator.of(sheet).pop();
                  PauseRelationshipAction.confirmAndPause(
                    context,
                    relationshipId: relationshipId,
                  );
                },
              ),
            if (relationshipId != null)
              (
                loc.flow_sharedEnd,
                () {
                  Navigator.of(sheet).pop();
                  EndRelationshipAction.confirmAndEnd(
                    context,
                    relationshipId: relationshipId,
                  );
                },
              ),
            (loc.flow_notNow, () => Navigator.of(sheet).pop()),
          ],
        ),
  );
}
```

Create `lib/app/documentations/user_manual/data/how_you_use_attune_docs.dart` with exactly:

```dart
// lib/app/documentations/user_manual/data/how_you_use_attune_docs.dart
//
// "How you use Attune" (Spec 1 §3.7, §3.10, §3.12). Listed only while
// flow_signin_v2 and flow_paths_v2 are on (DocumentationRegistry).

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:attune/app/documentations/user_manual/models/documentation_model.dart'
    show DocumentationModule;
import 'package:attune/app/documentations/user_manual/models/faq_model.dart';
import 'package:attune/app/documentations/user_manual/models/manual_content.dart';
import 'package:attune/app/documentations/user_manual/models/manual_section.dart';

class HowYouUseAttuneDocs implements DocumentationModule {
  @override
  String getTitle(BuildContext context) => 'How you use Attune';

  @override
  String get id => 'howYouUseAttune';

  @override
  String getSubtitle(BuildContext context) =>
      'In a relationship, dating, or just for you, and how to change it';

  @override
  IconData get icon => Icons.alt_route_outlined;

  @override
  int get order => 2;

  @override
  List<ManualSection> getSections(BuildContext context) => [
    ManualSection(
      id: 'how_paths',
      title: 'Three ways to use Attune',
      subtitle: 'Choose one; change it any time',
      icon: Icons.alt_route_outlined,
      category: 'How you use Attune',
      order: 1,
      contents: [
        ManualContent(
          id: 'how_paths_list',
          title: 'The three ways',
          numberPrefix: '1',

          type: ManualContentType.bulletList,
          bulletPoints: [
            'In a relationship: a private space for you and your partner. Your second tab is Chat.',
            'Single and open to dating: a few introductions a day, only when it\'s mutual. Your second tab is Me, with Dating at the top.',
            'Just for me: your journal, quizzes and Healing. Your second tab is Me.',
          ],
        ),
        ManualContent(
          id: 'how_paths_yours',
          title: '',
          content:
              'Your journal, quizzes and Healing stay yours whichever you choose.',
          type: ManualContentType.tip,
        ),
      ],
    ),
    ManualSection(
      id: 'how_change',
      title: 'Changing how you use Attune',
      subtitle: 'Settings → How you use Attune',

      category: 'How you use Attune',
      order: 2,
      contents: [
        ManualContent(
          id: 'how_change_where',
          title: 'Where to change it',
          numberPrefix: '1',
          content:
              'Go to Settings → How you use Attune and choose another way. Nobody is told when you change it.',
        ),
        ManualContent(
          id: 'how_change_shared',
          title: 'If you share a space with a partner',
          numberPrefix: '2',

        ),
        ManualContent(
          id: 'how_change_invite',
          title: 'If your invite is still waiting',
          numberPrefix: '3',
          content:
              'Changing to another way cancels your invite: the code stops working, and nobody is told.',
        ),
        ManualContent(

    ManualSection(

      title: 'A reminder to write',
      subtitle: 'Only if you set one',
      icon: Icons.alarm_outlined,
      category: 'How you use Attune',
      order: 3,
      contents: [
        ManualContent(
          id: 'how_reminder_set',
          title: 'Setting it',
          numberPrefix: '1',
          content:
              'In Your space, tap "Remind me to write", choose a day and a time between 8 in the morning and 10 at night, and tap Turn on. The notification just says "A few quiet minutes for you, if you\'d like."',
        ),
      ],
    ),
  ];

  @override
  List<FAQModel> getFAQs(BuildContext context) => const [
    FAQModel(
      id: 'faq_how_told',
      question: 'Is anyone told when I change how I use Attune?',
      answer:
          'No. Nobody is told, not a partner, not a match, not anyone who invited you.',
      category: 'How you use Attune',
      order: 1,
    ),
  ];
}
```

## Owner deploy checklist (the plan never runs any of this)

1. **Order.** W1 and W2 are deployed first (their checklists). W3a and W3b are app-only. W3c adds migration 8 and its twin; deploy them before a W3c build reaches a tester, because J3's sheet calls `set_my_journal_reminder` and `get_my_journal_reminder`. No edge function changes in W3. On the development project `flow_paths_v2` is already on (W1's dev script, unchanged), so **installing a W3a build switches development to the v2 home and `OnboardingGate` v2 at once**.
2. **Server (W3c).** `supabase db push` applies `20261026100000_journal_reminders.sql` and `20261026100010_journal_reminders_grants.sql`. Afterwards, in the SQL editor: `select jobname, schedule from cron.job where jobname = 'enqueue-journal-reminders';` → `enqueue-journal-reminders | */15 * * * *`; `select has_function_privilege('authenticated', 'public.enqueue_journal_reminders(timestamptz)', 'EXECUTE');` → `f`; `select has_function_privilege('anon', 'public.get_my_journal_reminder()', 'EXECUTE');` → `f`; `select relrowsecurity from pg_class where oid = 'public.journal_reminder_settings'::regclass;` → `t`.
3. **The worker.** `scheduled_notifications` rows for reminders carry `metadata.type = 'journal_reminder'`; confirm the existing worker sends them as an ordinary immediate push (the contract checks the row's shape; the worker is unchanged) by setting a reminder 20 minutes ahead on a development account and waiting for it.
4. **Flags.** Nothing to insert: `flow_paths_v2` and `flow_couples_v2` exist (W1c) and are off in production. `supabase/sql/dev_enable_spec1_flags.sql` is unchanged.
5. **Device checks (development project, both platforms):**
   - A legacy account (made before W1, onboarding done) signs in on a W3 build: one "Add your date of birth", then home; Tab 2 is Chat if it has a partner or an invite, Me otherwise.
   - Kill the app at A5c ("We've updated our terms") on a v1 account: every reopen shows A5c before home; "Not now" signs out (M-3).
   - Airplane mode at A6: Just for me goes home and syncs later; Dating says it needs a connection.
   - Quick exit from Me with the Dating lock on: the lock is still on afterwards, and the Safety PIN too; sign out: both the home-state cache and the lock are gone (§5.8).
   - "Join Attune" → Back from A1 lands on the guest's Tab 2 ("Sign in").
   - R2 and J3 "Turn on": the OS dialog the first time; after a refusal, the app's settings page.
   - A reminder at 21:45 local: it arrives at 21:45 (± the cron's 15 minutes); a reminder for 22:30 can't be chosen.
   - Tap the reminder: Me opens, then the journal; Back returns to Me.
   - TalkBack and VoiceOver through A6, H1, Me, R3, the Dating slot and How you use Attune; 200% system text.
   - Kill-switch rehearsal: with a linked couple on Chat, turn `flow_paths_v2` off in development; the couple stays on Chat at once (batch 2 I#1), and turning it back on returns the v2 layout.
   - The Me nav pill at narrow width; tab targets and TalkBack/VoiceOver tab announcements (batch 2 I#3).
6. **W3 group gate (before `flow_paths_v2` in production):**
   - Spec 2 W2's path trigger is live, so Dating → Just for me pauses Dating (D18).
   - The Dating path has a push ask (Spec 2), so Dating users are asked for notifications (batch 6 review M-6).
   - `dating_mode_enabled` stays off in production until Spec 2 W3 enforces the Dating lock on the Dating routes (batch 5 review C-1).
   - Before W6a or Spec 2 place any hold other than `age_review`, `get_my_home_state` returns the hold kind and the gate blocks the device only on `age_review` (batch 5 carry).
   - **Support address:** replace the placeholder `support@yourapp.com` in `lib/core/utils/constants.dart` with the real support inbox, so H1's "Contact support" reaches someone (final review I-2).
7. **Production switch-on, last:** when items 1–6 are done on the production project, `select public.record_flag_gate_evidence('flow_paths_v2', '<review ref>');` as the service role, then `update public.feature_flags set enabled = true where key = 'flow_paths_v2';`. `flow_couples_v2` stays off until W4.
